- **Alternative rejected — give doctors `view_records`/`write_records` themselves (the task's first preference):** those two already mean "practice-wide" in four places, so a doctor would silently gain: the clinic report (`views.REPORT_VIEW = services.VIEW`), every doctor's follow-ups (`views.follow_ups` narrows to own only without VIEW), the dashboard follow-up tiles (`reports.activity_panel._clinic_tiles`), and editing another doctor's visit (`services.save_visit` override with WRITE, pinned by `test_reception_cannot_read_but_a_doctor_can`). Re-gating those would change what every manager-copied custom role can do. A third permission keeps every existing grant exactly as it was.
- **Also rejected:** a heuristic at run time (job title text, or "doctor on a visit"): the title is free text and the visit's doctor or an appointment's employee is chosen by whoever types it, reception included; not a deliberate grant. A per-user permission or a "clinician" flag on `Employee`: a schema change, and roles already carry it. Per-patient assignment ("the patients they treat"): not in the data today (an appointment's employee is set by reception); a clinic-wide clinical role is the conventional baseline. Possible later refinement.
- **Upgrade (what the migration does on an existing install):** one role per user and locked system roles mean the only lossless way to keep a clinician working is a copy of their role.
  1. Creates `medical.treat_patients`, the `doctor` role (holding only it), grants it to owner and manager, relabels the two practice-wide permissions.
  2. Finds the clinicians among linked users: an active employee with a login who is the `doctor` on a `ClinicalVisit` or a dental `TreatmentStep`, the therapist (`employee`) on a package session (`agreements.PackageUse`, counted only when its package was sold from a location of a medical entity, so a salon's stylist is not a clinician even in a group that also has a clinic; an entity with no activity of its own counts as the installation's, a location with no entity as the main entity's; Codex review of #356 found the first version counted every session once any entity was medical), or a linked login that entered a `LabResult` (results name no employee). Not evidence: an appointment's or a queue ticket's employee, which reception sets.
  3. For each such login that is active, whose profile is active and whose (active) role holds neither `view_records` nor `treat_patients`: moves it to `custom_hg084_<old role code>` («<old role> + كشف المرضى»), a non-system copy of the old role's permissions (minus `permissions.manage_roles`) plus `treat_patients`, one copy per old role, with a description naming HG-084; a clinician with no role gets `doctor`; one with no profile gets a profile with `doctor`. Every move is an audit row (`hg084_clinician_role`, before/after role).
  4. Everyone else linked to an employee loses the clinical screens. Owners and managers lose nothing. A switched-off profile or role stays off.
  - Fresh database: nobody is moved; only the permission, the role and the labels are written. The reverse moves the users back to their old role (or no role), deletes the copies, deletes `doctor` unless someone still wears it (`UserProfile.role` is PROTECT), deletes the permission and restores the labels; audit rows stay (append-only) and a `…_reversed` row is added.
  - The way back for anyone who lost access: the owner gives their login «الدكتور أو الأخصائي» on the users screen, or copies their role on the roles screen and ticks «الكشف على المرضى» (the medical section shows there in medical activities).
- **Risk:** (1) a clinician with no record yet (a doctor who joined but never saw a patient, a nurse) loses access on upgrade until the owner grants it; it fails closed, and the doctor role is one pick away. (2) A receptionist who once typed a visit with herself chosen as the doctor would be kept as a clinician; the audit row shows it and the owner can move her back. (3) Linked reception loses screens that sit behind the clinical guard today and that an unlinked cashier never had: billing dental steps from the chart, selling a physio package, printing a lab sheet, setting a patient's insurer (only on the file). See open questions.
- **Files:** `medical/services.py` (rule), `medical/queue.py` (`_doctor_of`), `medical/views.py` (403 messages only), `medical/migrations/0010_hg084_clinician_access.py`, `permissions/models.py` (`RoleCode.DOCTOR`; the field has no choices since 0011, so no schema migration), `settings_core/operational_views.py` (role description, `ROLE_NEEDS`), `docs/permissions_map.md` (owner note), tests `medical/tests_access.py` (new), `medical/tests_hg084_migration.py` (new), `medical/tests.py` (doctors in five tests now hold the doctor role; the privacy test also pins that a linked cashier gets 403).
- **Places checked and left unchanged (no clinical text, or already gated):** the appointments agenda, week and week JSON, appointment detail (the "record the visit" button follows `shows_files`), the queue desk and the public queue screen (numbers only), insurance list, contract and claim print (patient, service, invoice, amounts; no diagnosis), the customer card (file link follows `shows_files`), global search and the customer list (name, code, phone), the dashboard (queue and follow-up tiles gated), the follow-ups list (names, phones, dates, doctor; behind the clinical guard), customer messages (amounts, instalments, tomorrow's appointment time, pharmacy refills; nothing clinical), «اسأل حسبة» (no medical tool; `appointments_today` is a count), `staff.myday` (services only; appointments, not records), `workorders` "diagnosis" (repairs, not patients). The audit trail: `save_visit` writes the first 200 characters of the diagnosis into `after_data` (and the M5 attachments, PR #355, will write file names and categories). No Hesba screen shows audit rows' contents: the only reader is Django Admin's `AuditLogAdmin`, which is superuser-only (`config.urls._superuser_only`, and closed on a showcase), and a superuser passes every permission check, clinical ones included; the daily email's "events" only count `cancel_*`, `void_order_line` and `adjust_stock` actions; backups (`backup_data`, Drive) hold the whole database encrypted to the owner's key. So nothing is redacted here; if an in-app audit screen is ever built, it must hide `module="medical"` payloads from users without `can_view`. `can_view(user)` / `can_write(user)` keep their names and signatures and stay the one place the rule lives, so the attachment owners registered by PR #355 inherit it unchanged (its delete rule, `medical.write_records`, stays practice-wide: a doctor with only `treat_patients` attaches and reads but does not delete).
- **Tests:** `medical/tests_access.py`: the matrix owner / manager / doctor / linked receptionist / linked stock keeper / unlinked cashier / linked login with no role / switched-off doctor, across the patients list and search, the file, the visit form (GET, POST), visit edit, the prescription print, follow-ups, the clinic report (owner and manager only), the dental chart, lab results, lab sheet and lab tests, physio packages, the vet animal card and new animal, the queue desk and screen (reception and clinicians; not a linked stock keeper); marker strings planted in every clinical field and absent from every reception page (agenda, detail, week, week JSON, queue with name and phone search, insurance list, contract and claim, search, customer list, customer card, dashboard); reception checks in, calls without becoming the doctor, bills an insured visit; a custom «reception + treating» role works; the doctor role lands on the dashboard with «الملفات الطبية» in the menu; the seeded demo clinic: the owner (its doctor) and a doctor-role login linked to the demo doctor read and save visits, the demo cashier gets 403 on the file and sees no diagnosis. `medical/tests_hg084_migration.py` (style of `agreements/tests_pkg_unify.py`): clinician kept via a copy (cashier and stock-keeper copies shared by a doctor, a dentist, a therapist and a lab technician), non-clinician linked users lose it, manager unchanged, no-role and no-profile clinicians get `doctor`, switched-off profile and inactive employee stay off, copies hold exactly the old role's permissions plus the new one, six audit rows, the owner can make a role with the new permission, a salon's package session moves nobody, fresh database moves nobody, reverse puts everyone back and forward again lands in the same place.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Should reception print lab results for the patient to take home, and bill dental steps / sell physio packages without opening the clinical chart? (Not built: those screens stay clinical. A billing-only view for `sales.create_sales_invoice` without the chart is a small follow-up.)
  2. Should reception set a patient's insurer, which today is only on the patient file? (Not built: owner, manager and doctors set it; reception bills insured visits from the appointment as before.)
  3. Should a doctor read only the patients they treat (per-patient assignment)? (No: clinic-wide clinical role, the usual baseline.)

## HG-085 — M6: a lab patient opens their result from a link (a public page showing health data)

- **Status:** IMPLEMENTED under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review (branch `claude/m6-lab-result-link`; revised after Main Control's review: one factor per file, a lifetime cap, count before compare). T2/T3: no model, no migration, no money, but a public page that shows health data, so it is recorded here. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «3. طبي», item 19 «نتايج أونلاين للمريض (معمل)», PR M6.
- **Why it is a gate:** until now no page outside the login showed medical data. Lab results are a patient's medical secret (MED-001: `medical.view_records` is the owner's and the manager's by role). A link that leaks, is forwarded, or reaches a wrong number would show them to a stranger; so the link alone is not enough, and the patient proves who they are with a second factor before anything is shown.
- **Protected logic:** none touched. No model, schema or migration (`makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean); no posting, stock, cashbox, ledger, report calculation, closing or permission-core change. `medical.services.can_write` and `medical.lab.is_lab` are called, not copied, so the role change in open PR #356 (HG-084) applies here unchanged. `printing/share.py` is called (`whatsapp_link`) and not changed; S2's kinds, salt and behaviour are unchanged and pinned by its own tests.
- **Files:** `medical/public.py` (new: token, fingerprint, factor, throttle, cookie, the public view and `make_link`); `medical/views.py` (`lab_results`: the «ابعت النتيجة للمريض» action; words); `templates/medical/_lab_sheet_body.html` (new: the lab sheet's content, now shared by the printed sheet and the patient's page so the two cannot drift), `templates/medical/lab_sheet.html` (includes it; the printed sheet is unchanged apart from `data-label` / `data-out` attributes used by the phone layout), `templates/medical/lab_link.html`, `templates/medical/lab_link_dead.html` (new), `templates/medical/lab_results.html`; `static/hesba/css/lab_link.css` (new, on top of S2's `customer_link.css`); `config/urls.py` (`/results/<token>/`, `login_not_required`); `config/settings.py`, `.env.example`, `docs/DEPLOYMENT.md` (`MEDICAL_LINK_DAYS`); tests `medical/tests_lab_link.py`.
- **The token (stateless, no table):**
  - Django signing with the SECRET_KEY and its **own salt** `hesba.medical.lab.v1` (S2's is `hesba.share.v1`): a share link never opens this page and this token never opens `/share/…` or S2's approve / refuse endpoints (both pinned by tests).
  - Payload: `{"p": patient id, "d": sample day, "f": fingerprint, "n": 4 random bytes}`, compressed. The random bytes make every «ابعت تاني» a new token (with fresh counters, below) even within the same second. It is **signed, not encrypted**: whoever holds the link can decode the patient's internal number and the sample day (no name, no test, no value).
  - **Fingerprint:** a keyed HMAC (`salted_hmac`, salt `hesba.medical.lab.fingerprint.v1`, SHA-256, 32 hex), never a plain hash: the payload is readable, and a plain hash of a low-entropy result («negative» / «positive») could be guessed offline without passing the factor. Inputs: every result of that patient and day (id, test, value, text, unit, low, high, flag), plus the second-factor details (the birth date and the phone's digits). Any edit, addition or removal of a result kills old links; a re-save with the same values does not. **Deviation from the brief, stricter:** a change of the birth date or the phone also kills old links, so correcting a wrong number kills the link that went to it.
  - **Expiry:** `MEDICAL_LINK_DAYS` (default 7), read as `SHARE_LINK_DAYS` is. «ابعت تاني» simply makes a fresh link; old ones stay valid until they expire or the results change (nothing is stored, so there is no revoke list).
- **Second factor (before anything is shown): one question, never «or».**
  - **A file with a birth date: the birth year only** (`PatientFile.date_of_birth`). The phone's last 4 digits are then no factor at all: the link is sent to the number on file (`whatsapp or phone`), so when that number is wrong the stranger who receives the link owns it and would be in on the first try.
  - **A file with no birth date: the last 4 digits of the phone on file** (`Customer.phone`, read with Arabic digits, at least 7 digits to count as a phone). **Limit, stated plainly:** this protects against a link that is forwarded or leaks, not against the main mis-send case (a wrong number on file), because whoever receives the link at that number owns those 4 digits. The lab's screen says so before and after making the link («ملف المريض ده مفيهوش تاريخ ميلاد، فلينك النتيجة محمي بآخر 4 أرقام من الموبايل بس، ودي مش بتحمي لو الرقم نفسه غلط… الأحسن تضيف تاريخ الميلاد في ملف المريض الأول»): advice, not a block.
  - **Neither:** the lab screen shows why and offers no link, `make_link` refuses, and a link whose patient later loses both shows the dead page.
  - The prompt names the factor that applies (so it reveals whether a birth date is on file: accepted); within each kind the wrong-answer page is byte-identical (pinned: a file with a birth date and a phone answers exactly as one with a birth date only; two phone-only files answer alike). The WhatsApp text names the same factor. An answer that is not 4 digits gets a format message and is not counted. The comparison is `hmac.compare_digest`.
- **Before the factor the page shows** only the lab's identity (name, logo, its own phone and WhatsApp, the language switch) and the question: no patient name, no test, no value, no date (the page title is «نتيجة التحاليل · lab name»).
- **After the factor, the page shows** exactly the printed lab sheet, through the same partial: the lab's details, «تقرير نتيجة تحاليل», the patient's name, the sample day, the file number, the age that day, and per test its name, value or text, unit, normal range and flag (out of range: bold, the label «مرتفع ↑ / منخفض ↓» and a tinted card, never colour alone), and the signature line. Nothing else: no other day, no history or «last time», no file notes, allergies or other medical data, no staff names (the sheet never showed `entered_by`). A print / save-as-PDF button prints the sheet alone. Arabic and English, with the language switch kept on the same link (the pass survives it).
- **The remembered pass:** a passed factor sets a signed cookie (`set_signed_cookie`, salt `hesba.medical.lab.pass.v1`) named per link (`hs_lab_` + 16 hex of the token's SHA-256), whose value is the token's SHA-256, scoped to the link's path, `HttpOnly`, `SameSite=Strict`, `Secure` when the site is HTTPS or `SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE`, valid 30 minutes. It is checked by name **and** value, so one link's pass never opens another (tests copy and re-sign values across links). The Django session is never read or written: a staff member signed in on the same browser still gets the question, their session key and login are unchanged, and the view's audit row carries no actor. The answer is a POST (CSRF-protected) answered by a 303 to the same address, so a reload never re-posts it.
- **Throttle (Django's default cache, as the booking and payment pages):** keyed by the token's SHA-256 (never the raw token) and by address.
  - **Lifetime cap per link: 8 wrong answers in the link's whole life** (a cache key with no time window, kept for the link's max age plus a minute). Then the link is dead: any new answer, even the right one, and any visit without a pass get the dead page («… اطلب من المعمل يبعته تاني», 404, the same page as every other failure). A browser that had already passed keeps its 30 minutes. «ابعت تاني» makes a new token (it carries fresh random bytes), which starts clean (pinned).
  - **Per link, 5 per fixed 15-minute window** (`timestamp // window`): over it, even the right answer gets a calm «محاولات كتير…» (429) until the window passes. The former 10-per-day bucket is gone: the lifetime cap is tighter.
  - **Per address, 50 per fixed hour** (429). Any request to the page is also capped at 60 a minute per address (429).
  - **Count before compare:** every well-formed (4-digit) answer first takes a slot in the lifetime, 15-minute and address buckets (`cache.add` then `cache.incr`, atomic in the cache), is refused if any new count is over its limit (and then gives its slots back, so refused answers are not charged), and only then is compared. A right answer gives its slots back, so a patient who gets it right is never charged; a wrong one keeps them. So N answers sent at once can never compare more than the limit (pinned with 20 threads and with N takes before any compare).
  - **Per-process limit:** the default cache is per-process memory (as HG-070 notes for the payment pages). Each worker has its own counters and a restart clears them: with W workers a link allows up to W × 8 wrong answers in its life (2 workers: 16; 4 workers: 32), and every restart allows another W × 8. No shared cache was added (no new infrastructure); a shared cache (database or Redis) would make the cap exact.
  - **Per-address bucket:** behind the host's proxy `REMOTE_ADDR` can be the proxy for everyone (HG-070), so the address limit is generous and the per-link limit is the real one; a tight address limit would let one person lock every patient out.
- **One answer for every failure:** a forged, altered, garbage, expired, other-salt, other-patient, other-day, no-result, stale-fingerprint or no-factor link gets the same page (`medical/lab_link_dead.html`: «اللينك ده مبقاش شغال … اطلب من المعمل يبعته تاني», the lab's call and WhatsApp buttons) with status 404, byte-identical across failures (pinned). A medical variant was needed because S2's `expired_page` says «المحل».
- **Headers on every answer** (200, 303, 400, 404, 405, 429): `Cache-Control: no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: same-origin` (as S2), plus the base template's robots and referrer meta. No third-party resource: tokens.css, customer_link.css, lab_link.css and the brand icons are our static files; the lab logo is the stored data URI; the only outside address is the `wa.me` / `tel:` links.
- **The public page is not gated on the activity:** for an anonymous visitor there is no current entity, so a group whose lab is one entity would see every link 404. The signed token (made only by lab staff, below), the fingerprint and the factor are the gate.
- **The lab's side:** on «نتايج التحاليل», each day with results has «ابعت النتيجة للمريض» (a POST, CSRF-protected) for users with `can_write` on a lab install (`_lab_guard` 404s elsewhere, including non-lab medical sub-activities; a reader with only `medical.view_records` gets 403; `make_link` repeats the check and raises `PermissionDenied`). It shows the link (built from `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` only, never the request host; without it, S2's «عنوان الموقع مش متظبط (PUBLIC_BASE_URL)…» and no link), a WhatsApp button (`printing.share.whatsapp_link` to the patient's WhatsApp or phone) and a copy button, with a note of the days and that an edit kills the link. **The WhatsApp text** names the lab, says the results are ready and how to open them (the one factor that applies: the birth year, or the phone's last 4 digits for a file with no birth date) and the days the link works; never a test name, value, flag, diagnosis, the patient's name or the day (pinned with the text URL-decoded). No messaging-outbox kind in this PR (a new `MessageKind` is a `messaging` migration): a later step.
- **Audit:** making a link writes `AuditLog(event_type=export, action="send_lab_result_link", actor=the user, object = the patient, after_data={patient, day, results count, days})`, never the token. Each time the sheet is shown to the patient, `action="open_lab_result_link"` with `actor=None`, no IP, no user agent, `after_data={patient, day}` (S2's customer rows carry no actor, IP or device either). Failed answers are throttled, not audited. `export` is used because `AuditEventType` has no "view" and adding one is a migration.
- **Risks (residual, stated):**
  - **Low entropy:** a birth year is about 100 plausible values and often known to people close to the patient. A stranger holding one link has 8 guesses in its life (W × 8 with W workers, more after restarts, above): about an 8 % chance of a blind guess at the birth year with one worker, more if they can narrow the age. Each link has its own counter, so several links sent to the same wrong holder add up (correcting the phone kills them all). It protects against a forwarded or mis-sent link, not against someone who knows the patient.
  - **The phone factor and the wrong number:** for a file with no birth date the factor is the phone's last 4 digits, which the holder of a wrongly recorded number knows (above). The lab is told and advised to add the birth date first. A stronger factor (a one-time code by SMS) needs a sender and is a later step.
  - **Per-process throttle** and the shared proxy address, above.
  - **The token is readable:** the patient's internal number and the sample day decode from the link (no name, no results).
  - **No revoke:** a link sent to a wrong number stays valid for its days unless the results, the birth date or the phone change. Editing the phone to the right one kills it.
  - **A staff session's idle timer:** with `SESSION_SAVE_EVERY_REQUEST`, a signed-in staff member who opens the link in the same browser has their session's idle timer reset by that request (the session key and login are unchanged, pinned by a test; nothing is written into it).
  - **The 30-minute pass** stays in that browser: a shared phone could show the sheet to the next person within that time (the cookie is `SameSite=Strict`, so it is not sent when the link is opened from another site, such as WhatsApp: checked in a browser, a link opened from another site asks, the answer opens the sheet, and opening the link again from that site, or reloading that arrival, asks again).
  - **Display (checked, not a bug):** the first report of this gate said ranges and text values showed reversed («16 – 12», «+A») in the RTL table. That was a misreading of downscaled screenshots. Measured in Chromium (the left edge of every glyph, sorted), the staff printed sheet and the patient's page on a phone, on a wide screen and in print, in Arabic and English, all lay out «12–16», «70–100» and «A+» left to right: each range (dash and spaces included) sits in one `<span dir="ltr">` and each value in its own `<bdi>`, both isolated. A test pins that markup on both sheets. Nothing was changed.
- **Tests:** `medical/tests_lab_link.py` (30): ranges and values isolated left to right on both sheets (rendered markup); a valid link shows only the lab and the question; nothing from another site embedded; the right birth year opens exactly that day's sheet rows (the same rows as the staff's printed sheet) and nothing else (no other day, history, notes, staff); the last-4 (Arabic digits too) opens a file with no birth date, and never one with a birth date; the prompt names the one factor; the cookie's path, `HttpOnly`, `SameSite=Strict`, age, and no session cookie; wrong answers never pass and are byte-identical within each kind; a wrong-length answer is not counted; per-link (5 per 15-minute window, refusing the right answer, from any address, and opening again after the window); the lifetime cap (8 wrong, then the dead page for a right answer, from any address and on a visit, a browser that passed keeps its sheet, and «ابعت تاني» in the same second is a new token that starts clean); count before compare (12 takes before any compare admit 5; 20 threads admit 8; the view compares only after its slot is taken; a right answer gives it back); per-address and page-flood throttles; the lab's phone-only note on the screen and in the box (Arabic and English) and none for a file with a birth date; forged, garbage, share-salt, work-order-salt, other patient, other day, no-result day, not-a-date, no-such-patient and non-dict tokens give one identical 404 page on GET and POST; a lab token at `/share/…` and at S2's approve / refuse gives S2's expired page; expiry at `MEDICAL_LINK_DAYS` (and a passed browser does not outlive it); a stale fingerprint after a value edit, an addition, a removal, a text edit, a phone or birth-date correction (a same-value re-save stays valid); a patient who lost both factors; one link's pass (copied or re-signed) never opens another, a tampered cookie opens nothing, the pass expires; a signed-in staff member gets the question and keeps their session; headers on every status, 405, CSRF enforced; the lab action (200 with the link, WhatsApp and copy; the audit row; the token in no audit row; a bad day 404, an empty day refused); the WhatsApp text in both languages; a patient with neither factor (and a too-short phone) gets no link and no audit; no `PUBLIC_BASE_URL`, no link and no audit; only `can_write` (reader 403, cashier 403, manager and owner allowed); clinic and shop installs 404 and `make_link` refuses; English throughout; the view's audit row has no actor, IP or device and failed tries are not audited. Mutation-checked: removing the cookie's value check, the fingerprint check or the factor check each fails tests. S2, booking and payment tests unchanged and passing.
- **Note, 13 Nov 2026 (see HG-106):** two things in this entry are superseded. (1) The second factor for a file with a birth date is now the **full birth date** (day, month, year in three numeric boxes), not the birth year, and every «birth year» above reads that way; the phone's last 4 digits for a file with no birth date are unchanged. (2) The lifetime limit of 8 wrong answers is no longer in the per-process cache: it is a row in the new table `medical.LabLinkCounter` (migration `0016`), counted by one atomic guarded UPDATE before the answer is compared, so it is shared by every worker and survives a deploy; the "per-process limit" risk above now applies to the short windows only (5 per 15 minutes per link, 50 per hour per address, 60 page loads a minute). The old premise "no table, no migration" no longer holds. Links already sent keep working and now ask for the full date.

## HG-086 — M2 prescription lines from the reference drug list (`medical.VisitMedicine`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/m2-visit-prescription` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026 («اعمل الصح والمتعارف عليه»), awaiting his review. T2 (a new table and a clinical feature). The only protected item is the new migration `medical/0011_visit_medicine` (a new table; no existing row is read or changed). Posting, stock, cashbox, ledgers, average cost, report calculations, closing and the permission core are untouched.
- **Reason:** roadmap item 18 «روشتة من قاعدة أدوية» (`docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, M2): a doctor picks medicines from the R1 reference list (HG-082) or types them, with dose, frequency and duration per line, and the printed prescription lists them. A visit had only free text (`ClinicalVisit.treatment`). **Why no existing table serves:** `pharmacy.Prescription` / `PrescriptionLine` are the pharmacy's dispensing record: their own RX number, a status (open / dispensed / cancelled), refills, an entity, invoices that dispense them, and sales / master-data permissions (`sales.create_sales_invoice`, `master_data.manage_items`); they have no link to a visit and a clinic has no inventory or sales role for its doctors. `ClinicalVisit.treatment` is one text field and cannot hold structured lines. Free text would also lose the link to the list that the allergy advice and a later dispensing hand-off need.
- **Table** (`medical/models.py`): `visit` (FK, CASCADE, `related_name="medicines"`), `position`, `reference` (FK to `pharmacy.DrugReference`, null, SET_NULL), the **snapshot** `name_ar`, `name_en`, `active_ingredient`, `strength`, `form` (the DosageForm code, shown in the reader's language), then `dose`, `frequency`, `duration`, `route`, `notes`: short text, not enums. Ordered by `position`, `pk`. No price, stock or invoice field.
- **Writes** go only through `services.save_visit` (`data["medicines"]`, a list of dicts; without the key the lines are left as they are, so every older caller is unchanged). `medical/prescribing.py` checks them (`resolve`) and `save_visit` writes the visit, its lines and the audit row in one `transaction.atomic()` (the visit save was not atomic before). Rules: blank rows skipped; a line needs a name or a list row («الدوا رقم N: اكتب اسمه أو اختاره من القايمة.» / English); at most 20 lines; text cleaned and cut to the column; an id from another visit makes a new line; a posted reference that is not an active row turns the line into a hand-written one with the typed name; a visit with only medicines passes the "write something" rule (its message now says «أو ضيف دوا»). Lines follow the visit's own rules: a doctor changes only their own visit unless they hold `medical.write_records`. The audit row adds the number of lines (not their names).
- **Snapshot rule:** a chosen row's names, ingredient, strength and form are copied when the line is first saved. Saving the visit again keeps that copy while the line still points at the same row, **or at nothing after the row was deleted** (SET_NULL); only choosing another drug copies again. A hand-written line's name may be changed by the doctor, never blanked by an empty post. A re-import that renames a row, `--deactivate-missing`, or deleting rows never rewrites an old prescription.
- **Search endpoint** `medical:drug_search` (`medical/views_rx.py`): `_guard(write=True)`, so 404 outside a medical install and 403 without a clinical permission (HG-084: an employee link alone gives nothing); not the pharmacy's `drugs` capability or `master_data.manage_items`. GET only (405), `never_cache`, and the query handled exactly as the pharmacy endpoint: `pharmacy.picker.search_reference` (clean, fold, 2-letter minimum, 20 rows, one query); the pharmacy endpoint has no rate limit, so neither does this one. It returns `picker.as_json` **without** `public_price`, `price_date`, `price_month` and `stale` (a prescription is not priced; the list's price is a pharmacy's retail reference and would read as advice to the patient), plus `allergy` per row and `list_empty` when no list is loaded (the sheet then says so instead of "no results"; the form itself hides «ضيف دوا» and says how to load a list).
- **Picker:** a new `static/hesba/js/rx_picker.js` and a medical sheet partial (`templates/medical/_rx_lines.html`, `_rx_line.html`) that reuse the R1 sheet's markup classes and `drug_picker.css` as they are; `drug_picker.js`, `drug_picker.css`, `_item_picker.html` and their `?v=` are untouched, so the pharmacy's new-item picker cannot change. The sheet's behaviour (debounce, abort on typing, stale answers dropped, rows hidden while typing come back, Esc, backdrop, arrows, count wording) is ported and pinned in its own tests. The search box is detached from the visit form (`form="rx-picker-detached"`), every button is `type="button"`, and Enter in a line moves to the next box. Quick picks fill the text (dose by dosage form, how often, before/after food, for how long) and are never rebuilt under a tap. Residual: the sheet logic exists twice (pharmacy and clinic); extracting a shared sheet is a follow-up.
- **Allergy advice (never a block):** one Python rule, `prescribing.allergy_terms`, used for the search flag, the refused-form re-render, the message after saving, the saved visit (file page and animal card). The allergy text is the patient's file, or on a vet visit the animal's own (never the owner's file on a vet install, as the print). Both sides are reduced as the reference list reduces names (case, hamza forms, ة/ى, Arabic digits, harakat). A line is flagged when a part of its active ingredient (split as `ingredient_key` splits a combination, compacted the same way) or the first word of a trade name equals a word of the allergy text (also without a leading «ال», «و», «وال», «بال»), a run of up to four of its words written together, or a phrase between commas. **Limits (on purpose, no guessing):** no drug-class or synonym knowledge: «بنسلين» does not flag Amoxicillin; «Aspirin» flags only a row whose trade name starts with Aspirin, not «Acetylsalicylic acid» alone; an Arabic allergy text does not meet a Latin ingredient; a part of a word is not a word («amox» does not flag Amoxicillin). A hand-typed line is checked on save and on the saved visit, not while typing (its name is not looked up). A vet line picked before the animal is changed keeps its earlier flag until saved; the save checks again. The print shows the allergy line once, as before, and no per-line flag.
- **Print** (`templates/medical/prescription.html`): numbered lines (trade name, strength unless the name already carries its number, form; the ingredient smaller; dose · frequency · duration · route; notes), then the free text under «تعليمات». A visit with no lines prints byte for byte as before (the extra styles are only emitted with lines); one A4 page for a typical visit (checked with Chromium's PDF: 1 page for three lines and instructions).
- **Demo:** the clinic's template loads the hand-written demo list (`pharmacy/demo_data/drug_reference_demo.csv`) through the same `demo_reference_list` step as the pharmacy (and on the next top-up of a template built before), and `medical/demo.py` gives two sample visits medicine lines through `save_visit` (every other field passed back as it was). Real installs load their list with `import_drug_reference` (`docs/DRUG_REFERENCE_IMPORT.md`, new clinic section). No third-party data.
- **Files:** `medical/models.py`, `medical/migrations/0011_visit_medicine.py`, `medical/prescribing.py` (new), `medical/services.py`, `medical/views.py` (visit POST adds the lines and a warning message; the render call gets the section's context on its first line; the print gets its lines), `medical/views_rx.py` (new), `medical/urls.py`, `medical/templatetags/medical_rx.py` (new), `medical/demo.py` (new), `config/demo_templates.py`, templates `medical/visit_form.html` (inside the form only), `medical/_rx_lines.html`, `_rx_line.html`, `_rx_saved.html` (new), `medical/file.html`, `medical/animal.html`, `medical/prescription.html`, static `hesba/js/rx_picker.js`, `hesba/css/rx_lines.css` (new), docs, tests `medical/tests_prescribing.py` (new) and `pharmacy/tests_till_alternatives.py` (the "only the pharmacy demo gets the list" test now names the clinic too).
- **Tests** (`medical/tests_prescribing.py`, 41): the table's fields, ordering and CASCADE / SET_NULL; the migration forward on a database with visits; `save_visit` creating, updating, removing, reordering and adding in one save, blank rows, refusals in Arabic and English with nothing saved, over-long and control text, the transaction (a failing line write leaves neither the visit nor its lines nor an audit row, new or updated), a foreign line id, retired and forged references, the visit's own edit rules; the snapshot after a rename, a real re-import and a deletion, each re-saved through the form; the endpoint's matrix (owner, manager, doctor 200; linked receptionist, linked stock keeper, cashier, no-role login, switched-off doctor 403; signed out to login; POST 405; non-medical 404), the same rows as `search_reference`, no price keys, no `drugs` capability or `manage_items` needed, `no-store`, an empty list, allergy flags for the patient only (odd and foreign ids ignored); the allergy rule (ingredient, combination part, trade name, case, hamza, «ال» and «و», harakat) and eight false-positive cases; the warning on the line, after saving, on the saved visit and the reopened form, in English, on a refused save, never blocking; the vet animal's allergies (not the owner's, not another owner's animal); the form's markup (detached search box, no submitting button, hidden buttons without JavaScript, versions); the print (order, Arabic and English, instructions, no price, a visit with no lines as before); a guard that every other table in the default database (stock movements, invoices, cashboxes, ledgers, the reference list...) is identical after prescribing; the demo clinic built as the template builder builds it (the list, two prescribed visits, every other field unchanged, idempotent) and its top-up; the script's pinned behaviours and tokens-only CSS.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Should the doctor see the list's price on the visit form? (No: a prescription is not priced.)
  2. Should a prescription be handed to the clinic's own pharmacy as a `pharmacy.Prescription` (one click, a dispensing record)? (Not built: a later step if a clinic also runs a pharmacy.)
  3. Should the allergy advice know drug classes (penicillins, NSAIDs...)? (No: that needs a curated clinical dataset and its licence; the rule matches only what is written.)

## HG-087 — M3: automatic appointment and follow-up reminders (a choices-only migration, no column)

- **Status:** implemented under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. Branch `claude/m3-appointment-reminders`. T2. No posting, stock, cashbox, ledger, deposit, permission-core or report code touched. HG-085 (M6) and HG-086 (M2) are on other open branches and are appended before this one on merge.
- **Protected logic:** the only protected item is the migration `messaging/0005_followup_kind`, **choices-only** (the HG-075 and HG-083 precedent): an `AlterField` on `MessageTemplate.kind` and on `OutboxMessage.kind` that adds one choice (`followup`, inside the existing `max_length=12`). No column, default or data change; `sqlmigrate messaging 0005` prints `(no-op)` for both; `makemigrations --check --dry-run` is clean. **No appointment column was added**: «confirmed» is the appointment's existing `confirmed` status, and the patient's cancellation request is an append-only audit row.
- **Reason:** medical activity 13 («تذكير المريض بميعاده») was «جزئي»: tomorrow's appointment reminder already existed (T9) but only when someone opened the outbox or ran a command by hand, there was no follow-up reminder, and the patient could not answer. The first two need a trigger and a kind; the answer needs a page.
- **What was found, and where this departs from the plan:**
  1. *The appointment reminder kind already existed* (`MessageKind.APPOINTMENT`, gathered by `messaging.services._appointments`), and so did the commands `gather_messages` / `send_messages`. Only the hourly trigger was missing.
  2. **Scheduling: a separate `/ops/messages/` endpoint, not a call inside `/ops/digest/`.** The digest is hourly too, so chaining the send onto it would run the API send every hour, and the first run of the day is shortly after midnight: patients would get a WhatsApp at night. The new endpoint (`config.nightly.messages_run`, name `ops_messages`) has the digest's guard copied exactly (`NIGHTLY_TOKEN` from the environment, `X-Hesba-Token` header or `?token=`, `hmac.compare_digest`, a plain 404 when the token is unset, missing or wrong, `csrf_exempt`, `never_cache`, no method restriction as the digest has none, no new secret). It runs `messaging.services.run_scheduled()`: `build()`, then `send_by_api()` bounded to 40 seconds (the Codex #282 rule; what is left goes on the next hourly run). **It sends only between 08:00 and 21:00 Cairo** (`SEND_FROM_HOUR`, `SEND_UNTIL_HOUR`); it gathers at any hour. Its answer is counts only (`status`, `added`, `sent`, `failed`, `left`), never a name, number or text. With no `WHATSAPP_TOKEN` / `WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID` it only gathers and the messages wait in the outbox for a click, which is what happens today. The keys stay env-only. One more cron-job.org row in `docs/GO_LIVE.md`.
  3. **Follow-up reminders (`MessageKind.FOLLOWUP`), sourced from `insights.coming_follow_ups`** (latest visit only, active patients only, nothing booked), narrowed to the next 2 days (`FOLLOWUP_AHEAD_DAYS`). **Once per follow-up, ever:** the key is `followup:<visit>:<due date>` and any key already sent or skipped, on any day, is never queued again (the `every_days` rule alone would repeat it). A patient with anything booked in *any* entity is not reminded, and a waiting reminder is withdrawn when they book or when its date passes. **Per entity:** a visit carries no entity (patients and visits are group-wide, HG-084), so each follow-up is gathered in exactly one entity: the entity of the visit's own appointment (none: the main entity, HG-034) when that is a medical entity, else the first medical entity; a group of two clinics reminds a patient once. A non-medical entity gathers none (`medical.services.is_medical_install()` per entity), and the template is hidden on the messages screen outside medical activities.
  4. **Switch: no new column.** It reuses the existing per-kind switch, `MessageTemplate.active`, but a new kind has no saved row and no row means «on» for the older kinds, so `services.DEFAULT_OFF = (FOLLOWUP,)` makes an unsaved follow-up template **off**. The owner switches it on by saving its template on the messages screen (`settings.manage_settings`, as for every kind).
  5. **The text names the clinic and the date and nothing else:** «أهلاً {name}، تذكير من {company}: ميعاد المتابعة يوم {date}. تحب نحجزلك ميعاد؟ …» (English default too). The placeholder whitelist has no diagnosis, doctor or visit word, so an edited template cannot pull clinical text in (tested: `{diagnosis}`, `{treatment}`, `{doctor}`, `{complaint}` are refused). In a group the company is the entity's own name. The outbox row links to the agenda of the due day (`/appointments/?day=`), not to a clinical page that reception cannot open (HG-084).
  6. **Appointment reminder in a medical entity carries the patient's link** (`printing.share` kind `appointment`, `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` only, never the request's host; no address, no link, the plain reminder). It is appended after the text unless the owner's template places `{link}` itself; a shop's reminder is byte for byte what it was (tested).
  7. **Masking:** `holds_link`, `shown_text`, `sendable` and `mark` now cover the new link-bearing kind. `may_hold_link(user, kind)` is per kind: a work-order message keeps `workorders.services.can_hold_customer_link`; an appointment message is held by `sales.create_sales_invoice` (reception books and changes appointments; this is the BOOK permission of the appointment screens). Someone without it sees `[لينك العميل]` in the outbox and the sent history, cannot send it by a click (403), and the bulk send skips it. The scheduler is trusted server code and is not masked.
  8. **Confirm link:** share kind `appointment` and the page `appointments/public.py` on the S2 pattern. **Confirm and cancel-request are POST-only** (`share/<token>/confirm/`, `share/<token>/cancel-request/`, `require_POST`, `csrf_protect`; a WhatsApp link preview that opens the address must change nothing). Confirm moves `booked` to `confirmed` through the ordinary `set_status` as the inactive booking system user (one audit row, `appointment_status`), once; a second tap writes nothing. **«طلب إلغاء» only records a request:** one append-only audit row (`appointments` / `appointment_cancel_requested`, no personal data) and nothing else; asking twice records once; the appointment keeps its status, time and any held deposit. The page never calls `cancel_with_deposit_policy`. Reception sees a quiet flag («المريض طلب إلغاء الميعاد») on the agenda row and on the appointment's page, until a later audit row shows reception moved, changed or cancelled it (cancelling still runs the S5 policy) or pressed «تم التواصل» (`appointment_cancel_request_handled`, a new reception-only action on the detail page, same permission as every change there). While a request waits, the patient's page shows it and no buttons, and a stray confirm changes nothing.
  9. **The public page shows** the clinic's name (the entity's in a group), the day and time and the service's name; **never** the patient's name, phone, file number, the doctor, the diagnosis, the appointment number, a price or deposit, or another appointment. There is no separate «public name» field for a service, so the item's name is shown, as the booking page does. **A dead link is one page:** forged, altered, expired (`SHARE_LINK_DAYS`), another document's token, a missing appointment, and an appointment that is cancelled, a no-show, arrived, done or already past all give `printing/share_expired.html` with status 404, on a GET and on a POST (the dispatcher handles the appointment kind before `get_object_or_404`).
  10. **The link is bound to the patient it was sent to** (added in review). The token carries the customer's id next to the appointment's (`printing.share.make_token(..., owner=)`, read with `read_token_data`); `appointments.services.link_alive` refuses it when the appointment belongs to someone else now. Reception can move an appointment to another patient (`reschedule` saves `customer`), and without the binding the first patient's link would have opened the second patient's service and time and let them confirm or ask to cancel it. A token without a patient is dead. A reminder still waiting when the patient changes takes a new link for the new patient.
- **Money, stock, balances:** none touched. Confirming and requesting create no invoice, payment, cashbox movement, journal entry, stock movement or deposit change (counted in the tests, with a held deposit).
- **Risks / things to know:**
  1. **A patient's number is needed** and is the customer's `whatsapp` or `phone`; the follow-up and the appointment reminder are one click or one API call away from a stranger's number if the file's phone is wrong.
  2. **The cancel request is an audit row, not a field.** It is cheap and cannot overwrite anything, but «pending» is computed from the order of audit rows for the appointment (one query per agenda page, tested). If an in-app audit screen is ever built it must keep these rows readable by reception.
  3. **Follow-up rows are visible in the outbox to customer-audience staff** (name, date, clinic; nothing clinical), although HG-084 put the follow-ups *list* behind the clinical guard. See question 2.
  4. **HG-085 (M6) may also extend `printing.share.KINDS` and add a messaging migration**: expect a merge conflict in `printing/share.py` and a second `messaging` migration number to rename.
  5. `send_by_api` only sends kinds that have an approved Meta template (`wa_template`); the owner must register one for `appointment` and `followup` before anything goes out by itself.
- **Files:** `messaging/models.py`, `messaging/migrations/0005_followup_kind.py`, `messaging/services.py`, `config/nightly.py`, `config/urls.py`, `printing/share.py`, `printing/views.py`, `appointments/services.py` (new functions only; no existing rule changed), `appointments/public.py` (new), `appointments/views.py` (the flag and `cancel_seen`), `templates/appointments/confirm_public.html` (new), `templates/appointments/agenda.html`, `templates/appointments/detail.html`, `reports/tests_route_auth.py` (the new route is public on purpose), `docs/GO_LIVE.md`, `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`; tests `messaging/tests_m3.py`, `appointments/tests_confirm_link.py` (new).
- **Tests:** `messaging/tests_m3.py`: the follow-up is off until switched on; one message with clinic and date and none of the diagnosis, specialty, doctor or complaint; edited templates cannot carry clinical placeholders; English wording; the 2-day lead; once only (sent, skipped, a new date is a new reminder); booked in any entity stops it; no number or deactivated patient; latest visit only; a passed date withdraws it; a shop and a non-medical entity get none; two clinics give one message, attributed to the appointment's entity and naming it; a non-medical main entity; the scheduled run (no keys: stays in the outbox and no call; keys: sent through a mocked provider, never at night, a refused one stays for a click, no double send); the endpoint (no token, wrong token, unset token, header and query, GET and POST, counts only, no login or CSRF); the reminder's link (medical only, refreshed, bare when the template places it, none without `PUBLIC_BASE_URL`, a shop's text unchanged); masking (viewer sees the mask in both languages and gets 403, bulk send skips, reception sends, work orders keep their rule). `appointments/tests_confirm_link.py`: the page's content and what it never shows (both languages), headers, a group of clinics; confirm once and idempotent, POST-only, CSRF, only its own appointment, no money; the cancel request is only a request, recorded once, with a held deposit still held and no money row created; the reception flag (agenda one query, detail, handled, cancelled, moved, permissions); every dead link the same page and status on GET and POST, in both languages.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Should the follow-up reminder be on for every clinic, or only when the owner switches it on? (Off until the owner saves the template.)
  2. Should follow-up rows in the outbox be limited to clinical staff, as the follow-ups list is? (No: they hold name, date and clinic only, and reception sends them, as it sends appointment reminders.)
  3. Should the appointment link go to non-medical businesses too (a salon's customers confirming)? (No: medical only; the page and the share kind work for any appointment if it is wanted.)
  4. Where should a patient's cancellation request live? (An audit row; a real field would be a second protected change.)
  5. The sending hours are 08:00 to 21:00 Cairo; should they be a setting? (A constant for now.)

## HG-089 — M1 visit templates per specialty (`medical.VisitTemplate`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/m1-visit-templates` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2 (one new table and a clinical feature). The only protected item is the new migration `medical/0012_visit_template` (a new table; no existing row is read or changed). It depends on `0011_visit_medicine` on purpose: another open branch holds `0012`, so whichever merges second needs a merge migration (or a renumber). Posting, stock, cashbox, ledgers, average cost, report calculations, closing, `save_visit`'s rules, the prescription snapshot rule (HG-086) and the permission core are untouched.
- **Reason:** roadmap item 14 «قوالب كشف لكل تخصص»: a doctor opens the visit form half-filled for a common complaint instead of typing the same words every time. **Why a new table:** a template is not a visit (no patient, date or vitals), and not any existing table's row (`ClinicalVisit` is per patient and `VisitMedicine` per visit). The medicine lines are a small JSON list on the template, not a second child table: they are only ever read whole, applied to the form and re-checked by `prescribing.resolve` on save, so a child table would add nothing JSON cannot do.
- **Table** (`medical/models.py`): `name`, `lang` (the language the texts are written in), `specialty` (a code from `visit_templates.SPECIALTIES`, kept out of the column's choices so the list grows without a migration), `owner` (a user, null means shared with every clinician; PROTECT, so a personal template never turns shared), `complaint`, `examination`, `diagnosis`, `treatment` (the visit form's own fields and limits: 5000 characters), `lines` (JSON), `active`, `position`, `seed_key`, `created_by` (null for built-in ones), timestamps.
- **What a template may hold:** clinical wording and medicine lines (`reference` of an active drug row or empty, `name`, `dose`, `frequency`, `duration`, `route`, `notes`). **What it never holds:** a patient, an animal, vitals, any date, a doctor, an appointment, a price. A visit never points at a template (no FK, no "template used" field). «احفظ ده كقالب» reads only the four texts and the medicine rows from the form; the patient's name, file number and phone and the animal's name are blanked wherever the doctor typed them into those texts (the ids are used only for that and never stored). Residual risk: a first name alone, a nickname or another identifying detail typed into a text is not recognised; the doctor is sent to the template's edit page to review it, and the audit row carries only the name, specialty and line count.
- **Built-in starting templates** (`medical/visit_template_seeds.py`): 28 per language (2 to 4 per specialty: general, paediatrics, OB-GYN, ENT, eye, skin, ortho, dental, vet), Arabic (Egyptian) and English, text only: prompts for the complaint and the examination and a few lines of general instructions. **No drug name, no dose and no diagnosis**: the diagnosis box and the medicine lines are empty (a test scans for dose patterns and drug words). Written once on first use of the sheet or the manager; the keys written are kept in one `SystemSetting` row (`medical.visit_templates.seeded`), so a template someone edited is never overwritten and one they deleted is never brought back; a key added in a later release is written the next time. A vet install gets the vet ones only, a dental clinic the dental ones, any other the human ones.
- **Access (HG-084: an employee link alone gives nothing):** every screen and the JSON endpoint need `services.can_write` (404 outside a medical install, 403 without a clinical permission). A doctor uses their own templates and the shared ones; another doctor's personal template is never offered on a visit, not even to the owner. A doctor changes only their own; a holder of `medical.write_records` (owner, manager) also writes the shared ones and may fix another doctor's (screen «قوالب الدكاترة»); a doctor reads a shared one and can copy it to theirs. A doctor asking for «مشترك» gets a personal one. The employee link is not used at all: `owner` is the user (departure from "doctor/employee": a clinician without an employee link could not have a personal template, and a link grants nothing anyway). The specialty is not guessed from the employee's job title (HG-084 rejected title heuristics); the sheet starts on the install's own specialty where it is known (vet, dental) and has chips for the rest. A vet install offers vet templates only and a human clinic never does.
- **Applying (browser only):** `static/hesba/js/visit_templates.js` fills only the boxes that are still empty, appends the template's lines after the doctor's (through `window.HesbaRx`, added to `rx_picker.js`, as ordinary editable lines, up to the 20-line cap) and shows one line saying what was filled and what was left alone. No server round trip, nothing the doctor typed is overwritten. A line whose drug is still active comes back in the drug picker's shape (no price); one whose row was switched off or deleted comes back as a typed line with its stored name; on save `prescribing.resolve` snapshots it like a hand-picked line and the allergy advice runs (also on the form, from `patient` / `animal` query ids exactly as `medical:drug_search`: only the drug's own matching word is returned, never the allergy text). The control, the two sheets and the save button are outside the visit's posted fields: nothing new has a `name` inside the form, the search box is detached, Enter never saves the visit, so the visit posts the same field names with or without a template (pinned by a test on the rendered form, and checked in the browser on the real `FormData`).
- **Endpoints:** `medical:visit_templates_data` (GET, `never_cache`, same guard as `drug_search`), `medical:visit_template_from_visit` (POST), the manager (`visit_templates`, `visit_template_new`, `visit_template_edit`, `visit_template_action` for up/down, on/off, copy, delete). At most 200 templates per person (and for the shared set); the answer carries at most 400.
- **Files:** `medical/models.py`, `medical/migrations/0012_visit_template.py`, `medical/visit_templates.py`, `medical/visit_template_seeds.py`, `medical/views_templates.py`, `medical/urls.py`, `medical/views.py` (the tab, the form's context), templates `medical/_vt_bar.html`, `_vt_sheets.html`, `_vt_filter_fields.html`, `visit_templates.html`, `visit_template_form.html`, `_tabs.html`, `visit_form.html`, `_rx_lines.html` (script version only), static `hesba/js/visit_templates.js`, `hesba/css/visit_templates.css`, `hesba/js/rx_picker.js` (the `HesbaRx` hook; `?v=m1-1`), tests `medical/tests_visit_templates.py` and one version string in `medical/tests_prescribing.py`.
- **Risks:** (1) the text of a template is free text a person types: a patient detail beyond the literal name, file number and phone can slip in and be shared with every doctor if a manager makes it shared. (2) A template's text saved on a visit is only text: editing or deleting the template never changes an old visit (by design). (3) Migration numbering with the other open branch (see Status). (4) The seeded wording is a starting point written for review by a doctor; Hesba does not present it as medical advice.
- **Tests** (`medical/tests_visit_templates.py`, 43): the table and its migration (after 0011, existing visits untouched, nothing seeded by the migration); the seeds (2 to 4 per specialty, both languages, no dose, drug or diagnosis, idempotent, edits kept, deletions respected, a later key added alone, vet and dental sets, first use from the sheet and the manager, a refused request writes nothing); the access matrix (owner, manager, doctor allowed; linked receptionist, linked stock keeper, cashier, no-role login, switched-off doctor 403; signed out to login; non-medical 404; wrong methods 405); another doctor's personal template (not offered, not shown, not editable, manager may fix it and it stays that doctor's); shared templates (read-only for a doctor, copy, manager edits); the JSON (order, switched-off hidden, no-store, no patient data, no price, retired drug as a typed line, vet and dental); lines through `resolve` (snapshot, retired reference, allergy advice); «احفظ ده كقالب» (no vitals, dates, ids, patient, animal; names blanked; refusals; cap); the manager (create, edit, off and on, reorder, copy, delete, filters, English); the form's POST shape and markup (outside the form, detached search, no submitting button, hidden without JavaScript, the vet's save sheet offers vet only); the script's pinned behaviours; tokens-only CSS; one-line template comments; no money, stock or visit row moves.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Structured per-specialty fields (visual acuity, pregnancy dates, tooth charts inside a template)? (Not built: templates fill the existing text boxes; a prompt such as «النظر: يمين ... / شمال ...» is plain text.)
  2. Record which template a visit used, for later analytics? (Not built: a visit never points at a template.)
  3. Share templates between branches or between clinics? (Not built: shared means every clinician of this installation.)
  4. Should a treating doctor (not owner or manager) be able to publish a template as shared? (No: shared is a practice-wide decision, taken by `medical.write_records` holders.)
  5. Hand-picked specialty names are Hesba's own list (general, paediatrics, OB-GYN, ENT, eye, skin, ortho, dental, vet); the app had no list to reuse. Add or rename any?

## HG-088 — M8 inpatient: wards and beds, admission and discharge, the stay's charges and its draft bill

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/m8-inpatient` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026 («اعمل الصح والمتعارف عليه»; his standing default for this item: «فاتورة الإقامة مسودة والمقدم عربون», the stay bill is a draft and the advance is a deposit), awaiting his review. T3: it sits next to money (an advance on admission, a bill at discharge). Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «3. طبي», item 20 «إقامة وأسرّة وعمليات (مركز طبي)», PR M8. The only protected item is the new migration `medical/0013_inpatient` (four new tables; no existing table or row is read or changed).
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:**
  - **Admitting a patient** moves nothing. **With an advance**, the advance is taken by the existing deposit service (S5, HG-069), unchanged: a customer payment on account in the patient's name, numbered `DEP-…`, into a cashbox or bank account of the admission's entity, dated today. The patient's account goes down by it and the cashbox up by it, and the ledger holds it as the patient's money in «2105 دفعات مقدمة من العملاء», exactly like a booking's deposit. Nothing in this PR writes those rows; `deposits.services.take` does.
  - **A charge** (a service, an operation, a supply) moves nothing: it is a row saying what was done, at a quantity and a price. A supply does **not** leave stock when it is charged.
  - **A move to another bed** moves nothing: it writes the old bed's days as charges.
  - **Discharge** moves nothing. It writes the last bed's days, frees the bed, and (for a user who may make invoices) builds **one ordinary DRAFT sales invoice** in the patient's name through the existing tax-aware draft service (`agreements.services.draft_invoice` → `taxes.services.create_sales_draft_with_tax`), the way `medical.dental.bill_done` bills dental steps: `SI-` number, no cashbox, nothing paid, tax as that service computes it, on the selling store of the admission's entity. No customer-ledger row, no stock movement, no cashbox movement, no journal entry (pinned by counting every one of those tables before and after).
  - **Posting that draft** on the sales page, by the ordinary posting service, by whoever may post, is what puts the stay on the patient's account, takes the supplies off the shelf of that store and (if paid) moves cash. Posting is never called from here. The held advance was pointed at the draft (`deposits.services.attach`), so the existing hook applies it when the draft posts: an `InvoiceSettlement` row for the advance, the rest stays due; an advance larger than the bill settles it in full and the difference stays the patient's credit.
  - **Cancelling a mistaken admission** (one with no charges) frees the bed, and a held advance follows the booking's refund path, unchanged: the collection permission, the closed-month blocker, HG-068's second person when the owner holds cancelled collections, then `deposits.refund` (`cancel_customer_payment`). **Never a forfeit:** an admission has no lateness (`deposits.is_late` is False and `deposits.policy` answers "refund" for it, at any time, pinned), no «رسوم إلغاء» invoice is ever made from these screens, and the stay page offers no forfeit button.
- **New tables** (`medical/models.py`, `medical/migrations/0013_inpatient.py`, after M1's `0012_visit_template`):
  - `Ward`: `name_ar`, `name_en`, `entity` (NULL: the main entity, HG-034), `position`, `active`.
  - `Bed`: `ward` (PROTECT), `code` (unique in its ward), `daily_service` (the service Item billed per day, PROTECT), `active` (off: out of service).
  - `Admission`: `number` (`ADM-00001`, unique), `patient` (Customer, PROTECT), `bed`, `bed_since` (when the patient took the bed they are in now), `doctor` (Employee, SET_NULL, as a visit's), `admitted_at`, `discharged_at`, `state` (admitted / discharged / cancelled), `diagnosis` (clinical text), `entity`, `created_by`, `created_at`. Two partial unique constraints: one **admitted** row per patient and one per bed, so the database itself refuses a second open admission.
  - `AdmissionCharge`: `admission` (PROTECT), `kind` (bed_day / procedure / operation / supply), `item`, `quantity`, `price`, `day`, `bed` (a bed-day's), `surgeon` (an operation's), `note` (clinical), `invoice` (SalesInvoice, PROTECT, NULL until billed), `created_by`, `created_at`. A partial unique constraint: one bed-day per day of a stay.
- **Departures from the approved plan, and why:**
  1. **`Admission.state`, not `status`.** `deposits.services.take` reads its target's `status` as a booking's and refuses anything but "booked" / "confirmed"; a target with no `status` is always open to it (its own docstring: the target is generic). So an admission carries no `status`; `medical.inpatient.take_advance` decides when an advance may be taken (only while admitted, only into a box of the admission's entity). This is what lets the deposit code stay untouched.
  2. **No invoice link on `Admission`.** The charges carry it (a charge is billed once); `inpatient.invoices(admission)` reads them. A second link on the admission could only disagree with them.
  3. **`Admission.bed_since` and `AdmissionCharge.bed`** were added: the bed-days of a move need to know when the current bed was taken, and a bed-day says which bed it was.
  4. **Discharge and billing are two rights behind one button.** Discharge is clinical (`can_write`); the draft invoice needs `sales.create_sales_invoice`, as every clinical bill does (`dental.BILL`). A user with both (owner, manager) gets «خروج وفاتورة» in one transaction (if the bill cannot be made, nobody is discharged). A doctor (the seeded role holds only `medical.treat_patients`) gets «خروج», and reception then sees «اعمل فاتورة الإقامة» on the discharged stay.
  5. **A fourth screen, «العنابر والأسرّة»,** because wards and beds have to be made somewhere: owner and manager (`medical.write_records`) add a ward, add a bed with its daily service, take a bed out of service.
  6. **The insurer split is not built** (below). **Dates inside an invoice line use slashes** (`2026/11/09`): a hyphenated ISO date is shown back to front inside an Arabic line by the bidi algorithm (found in the browser check).
- **The bed-day rule** (`medical.inpatient.bed_days`, one function, the common private-hospital practice in Egypt of counting nights):
  - days are **calendar days in Cairo local date**;
  - the **admission day is billed; the discharge day is not**;
  - a stay that begins and ends on the same day is **one day** (the minimum);
  - on the day of a **move** the bed the patient ends the day in (the new one) is billed; the old bed is billed for the days before the move, at its own daily service and price, written at the moment of the move. Moved and discharged on the same later day: the new bed is billed nothing. Admitted, moved and discharged on one day: one day, on the last bed;
  - so every day of a stay is billed exactly once whatever the moves (pinned for every stay of up to six days with up to two moves on any days), and the table refuses a second bed-day on a day;
  - the price of a bed-day is its bed's daily service's price **when the bed-day is written** (at the move or the discharge), then frozen on the charge.
  - The stay page shows the bed-days a discharge now would write as «لسه ماتقفلش» without saving them; the review before discharge shows the same lines the draft will carry (the same function builds both).
- **Rules:** a charge is added only while the patient is admitted (after discharge a forgotten line is an ordinary sales invoice to the patient). A service or an operation is recorded on a service item; a supply on a stock item, and only where the business keeps stock («المخزون» on). A charge on a live invoice cannot be removed; a bed-day is never removed by a person; running the bill again finds nothing and makes nothing (pinned); if the stay's invoice is cancelled, its charges are billed again on a new draft (as dental steps are). A charge's note and the diagnosis never go on the invoice or into an audit row. Cancelling is only for an admission with no charges. One advance per stay while it is held (the deposit table's own rule: one held deposit per target).
- **Access (decision recorded; HG-084's rule is called, not copied):**
  - **The bed board and a stay's page:** clinical staff (`medical.services.can_view`) and reception (`sales.create_sales_invoice`, the permission the queue and the appointments desk already use). Reception needs the board to admit and to bill, and sees there what it already sees on the queue and on an invoice: the patient's name, the bed, the day of the stay, the doctor, and the charges' names and amounts.
  - **The diagnosis and a charge's note** are clinical content: shown only to `can_view`, written only by `can_write`. Reception admits without a diagnosis (the field is not on its sheet, and the service drops a posted one).
  - **Admit, move, cancel:** reception or `can_write`. **Charges, the diagnosis, discharge:** `can_write` only. **The draft invoice:** `sales.create_sales_invoice`. **The advance and its refund:** `sales.receive_customer_payment` (inside the deposit service). **Wards and beds:** `medical.write_records` (owner, manager).
  - An employee link alone gives nothing (a linked stock keeper and a linked login with no role get 403 on every screen and `PermissionDenied` from every service; pinned). No new permission, no seeded role, no change to the permission core.
  - **Entities (HG-074):** a ward belongs to the entity it was made in; an admission to its ward's entity. The board, the free beds and a stay's page narrow to the entity being worked in (another entity's stay answers 404 before any action runs); a patient is not moved to another entity's bed; the advance lands only in a box of the admission's entity and the draft on its selling store, whatever entity the biller works in.
  - **Audit rows** (`module="medical"`, `object_type="medical.Admission"`): `admit_patient`, `move_bed` (with the bed-days written), `add_admission_charge`, `remove_admission_charge`, `set_admission_diagnosis`, `discharge_patient`, `bill_admission` (invoice number, charges, totals), `cancel_admission`; `save_ward`, `save_bed` for the set-up. The deposit and the draft write their own rows as before.
- **Where it shows:** a new capability «الإقامة والأسرّة» (`inpatient`, `settings_core/capabilities.py`): suggested for a medical centre, offered (off) to a clinic and "other medical", never to a lab, a dentist, a physio or a vet; asked in the setup interview in the clinic's words. Off: no «الأسرّة» in the menu and the whole `/medical/inpatient/` path is closed by the existing gate. An install made before this keeps it off until its owner switches it on. **For supplies** the owner needs «المخزون» on and the supplies stocked in the selling store of that entity; without it «ضيف مستلزم» is not offered. **For the advance** the owner needs «العربون وسياسة الإلغاء» on (it rides on the appointments module); without it the sheet says where to switch it on and admits without one.
- **Insured patients:** billed to the patient in full, and the stay page says so. `insurance.insured_drafts` was not used because it does not fit without changing it: every part goes on each invoice as quantity 1 at a split price, so a supply of three bottles would take one bottle off the shelf from each of the two invoices instead of three in all; it bills on the biller's working entity's store, not the admission's; and `InsuredBill.kind` has no inpatient value (a choices migration). The split is an open question.
- **NOT touched:** sales posting, cancelling and returns (`sales/services.py`), the draft services (`taxes/services.py`, `agreements/services.py`: called), stock movements and average cost, cashbox movements, customer and supplier ledgers, the ledger projector and chart, report calculations, closing, the deposit code (`deposits/`: called only: `take`, `attach`, `held_for`, `latest_for`, `refund_blocker`, `refund`, `status_label`, `enabled`), settlements, approvals (`hold` called), insurance, the permission core and its seeds, `medical.services.can_view` / `can_write` (called).
- **Files:** `medical/models.py`, `medical/migrations/0013_inpatient.py`, `medical/inpatient.py` (new: the rule, the services), `medical/views_inpatient.py` (new), `medical/urls.py`; templates `medical/beds.html`, `admit.html`, `stay.html`, `wards.html` (new); `static/hesba/css/inpatient.css` (new, tokens only, `?v=m8-1`); `templates/partials/shell_icons.html` (a bed icon); `settings_core/capabilities.py`, `setup_interview.py`, `operational_views.py` (the capability, its question, its landing page); `reports/navigation.py` (the «الأسرّة» entry); the demo: `settings_core/demo_inpatient.py` (new), `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py`, `config/demo_templates.py`, `config/demo.py`, `templates/partials/demo_tour.html`; tests `medical/tests_inpatient.py`, `settings_core/tests_demo_inpatient.py` (new), `config/tests_demo_first_run.py` (the clinic's tour has four steps); docs.
- **Demo:** the landing page offers the clinic and has no medical-centre template, so the **clinic** demo switches the capability on (demo-only, as its online booking is) and shows two wards, five beds (one out of service), a patient in bed 101 since the day before yesterday with two charges, and a stay that ended two days ago with its draft bill (two nights in the private room and an operation), all through `medical.inpatient`, nothing posted. A medical centre built by `prepare_demo` gets the same from its own preset. «جرّب دلوقتي» on the clinic gains a fourth step, «الأسرّة والإقامة». The demo takes no advance («العربون» is off in the clinic's demo).
- **Risks:**
  1. **The price of a bed-day is read when it is written.** A change to the daily service's price during a stay applies to the days of the current bed not yet closed. Hospitals that fix the rate at admission would want it copied onto the admission (a later column).
  2. **Times are "now".** The services accept a moment (`when`, never in the future, never before the bed was taken) and the demo and tests use it, but the screens always admit, move and discharge at the present moment. A discharge recorded after midnight bills one more day; an admission recorded the next morning loses the first night. Bed-days are not removable, so correcting a day today means posting the bill and returning that line by the ordinary sales return.
  3. **A charge reserves no stock.** If the shelf is short when the draft is posted, the ordinary posting rule answers (refusal, or the owner's selling-without-stock policy, HG-065). Supplies leave the entity's selling store, not a ward store.
  4. **The draft is an ordinary draft:** the sales page posts it; it does not edit or delete a draft (the same holds for a dental or an appointment draft). A wrong stay bill is posted and then cancelled or returned by the ordinary services; a cancelled one frees its charges, the advance is held again by the existing hook, and the stay can be billed again.
  5. **The deposit service can still forfeit any held deposit** if called (`deposits.forfeit`); only the appointment page calls it. These screens never do.
  6. **One advance per stay while held.** A second payment during a long stay is an ordinary collection from the patient («تحصيل»), not a second advance.
  7. **Reception reads the charges' names** (an operation's name is on the invoice too) but not the diagnosis or the notes.
  8. **Checked on SQLite only:** the row locks (`select_for_update(of=("self",))`) and the partial unique indexes are written for PostgreSQL as the rest of the app, and not run against it here; two admissions at the same instant are covered by the locks and the constraints, not by a threaded test.
- **After the independent review (11 Nov 2026, before merge).** A second reviewer tried to break the money paths (PostgreSQL races included) and found no wrong amount, no charge billed twice and no missing or doubled bed-day. It found seven gaps, all closed in this PR:
  1. **A stay that left its bed could not be reached from any screen** (the board listed occupied beds only), so a stay a doctor discharged was never billed unless someone typed its address, and a held advance sat unseen. The board now lists «إقامات محتاجة إجراء»: discharged stays with charges on no live invoice, stays whose bill is still a draft, and closed stays with an advance still held; and, folded, the last 20 stays that ended. Both keep to the entity being worked in.
  2. **«إلغاء الدخول» erased a stay with nights behind it**, and reception could do it: bed-days are written only at a move or a discharge, so a patient five nights in their first bed had no charges, and the cancel also refunded the advance. Now a same-day admission is cancelled as before; a stay with one night or more behind it is cancelled by the owner or a manager only (`medical.write_records`), the page says how many nights and how much will not be billed, and the audit row keeps both (`nights_behind`, `bed_days_waived`).
  3. **A move at the same instant as another action** gave the other user a server error on PostgreSQL (the locking query joined the bed). The row is now locked bare and read with its relations after.
  4. **A deadlock** between an admission with an advance and a cash sales return for the same patient on PostgreSQL: the patient row is now locked `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`.
  5. **An advance larger than the bill** was shown as fully used. The stay's page now says what came off the invoice and what stays the patient's credit. Paying that credit out is existing S5 behaviour (no payout screen): **question 9 below**.
  6. **An advance that nothing will use had no button after discharge** (a bill of zero that posted). «رد المقدم» now also shows on a discharged stay once every charge is on a live invoice and none of them is a draft; it follows the same refund path (collection permission, closed-month blocker, HG-068).
  7. **The page's running total could differ from the draft by piastres** (unrounded lines). Each line is rounded as the invoice's is.
- **Left as they are, known:** a discharge a few seconds after midnight bills one more day than a review made before midnight showed (risk 2); the services accept a back-dated admission time that the screens never send, without checking the bed's past occupancy; a doctor may price a charge at zero (the audit row records it); with the capability off the pages answer the «مقفول» page (403), and 404 on a non-medical installation.
- **Tests:** `medical/tests_inpatient.py` (76): the rule alone (same-day, one night, three nights, month, year and leap-day ends, a move, a move on the admission day, all on one day, a move and a discharge on one later day, every stay up to six days with two moves billed once per day); the same boundaries through the services with amounts (same-day 500; across midnight 23:59 → 00:00 one day; three nights 1,500; a move 2 × 500 + 2 × 1,200 = 3,400 with four distinct days; there and back in a day;
