the constraint refusing a duplicate bed-day; the running view writing nothing; impossible times refused); one open admission per patient and per bed by service and by the table, out-of-service beds, who cannot be admitted, a move freeing the old bed, re-admission, cancel; charges and what they refuse; the review equal to the bill; **the draft with exact lines, quantities, prices and totals (9,735.00) and every money and stock table unchanged**; **posting through `post_sales_invoice` taking 3 bottles off the shelf (10 → 7), one stock movement, the patient owing 9,735.00**; VAT at 14% per line (140.00, 84.00, 1,120.00, 18.90; 11,097.90) equal to the review; billed once and frozen; billed after discharge only; a cancelled invoice re-billed once; a failed bill leaving the patient admitted; an insured patient billed in full with no insurer invoice; **the advance**: a held deposit on the admission (patient −2,000, cashbox +2,000, 2105 −2,000), no lateness and no forfeit ever, attached to the draft and applied on posting (settlement 2,000, patient owes 7,000, 2105 back to 0), a larger advance leaving 3,000 as credit, a cancelled posted bill holding the advance again and the stay billed once more, one advance while held and none after discharge, a refused advance admitting nobody, the refund on a cancelled admission (all back to zero, no fee invoice), a doctor's cancel leaving it held, HG-068's second person; access (the matrix for the board, the sheet and the stay; the diagnosis and the note absent from every page reception opens; reception admitting without a diagnosis; charges and discharge clinical; a doctor discharging and reception billing; set-up for owner and manager; the capability off closing every page; where the capability is offered and where the interview asks about it); the screens end to end in Arabic and English; the templates' house rules; entities (scope of the board and the stays, the bill on the admission's store, no move across entities, a ward with no entity counted as the main entity's, the advance in its own books, another entity's stay 404); the audit rows and no clinical text in any. `settings_core/tests_demo_inpatient.py` (4): the clinic's and the medical centre's demo, idempotent, none for a dentist, the owner's pages and the tour's fourth step. Checked in Chromium at 390, 1024 and 1280 in Arabic and English: admit, charges, remove, move, review, discharge, open the draft; no horizontal overflow, no console error, no target under 44px.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Is the discharge day billed, or is it by nights? (By nights: the admission day counts, the discharge day does not, the shortest stay is one day. A noon check-out rule or billing by 24 hours would be one change in `bed_days`.)
  2. Should an insured stay be split between the patient and the insurer? (No: billed to the patient in full, and the page says so. A split needs a decision on supplies and stock, and an inpatient kind on the insured bill.)
  3. Do you want an operating-room calendar? (No: an operation is a charge with its surgeon, a day and a note.)
  4. Who sees the bed board? (Clinical staff and reception; reception admits, moves, cancels and makes the invoice; charges and discharge are clinical; the diagnosis and notes are hidden from reception.)
  5. Should reception add supplies or services to a stay? (No: clinical only.)
  6. Should a long stay be billed in parts, or take more than one advance? (No: one bill at discharge, one advance while held; other payments are ordinary collections.)
  7. Should the admission and discharge time be typed, for a stay registered late? (No: the screens use the present moment.)
  8. Should the room rate be fixed at admission? (No: each bed-day takes the daily service's price when it is written.)
  9. Should there be a way to pay back the part of an advance the bill did not use (today it stays the patient's credit on account, as any S5 deposit larger than its invoice)? (Not built: it is a refund of money already settled against an invoice, a posting decision for the owner.)

## HG-090 — M7 vet vaccinations and their due dates (`medical.Vaccine`, `medical.Vaccination`, and a choices-only `messaging` kind)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/m7-vet-vaccinations` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2 (two new tables, a clinical feature, an optional draft invoice, a reminder kind). Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «3. طبي», item 15 «تطعيمات ومواعيدها (بيطري)», PR M7.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing moves. Recording a dose writes a row and moves no money, stock, cashbox, ledger or journal entry (pinned by counts before and after). The only money-adjacent thing is **optional and never automatic**: «اعمل فاتورة» on one dose makes **one ordinary DRAFT sales invoice** in the animal owner's name through the same draft service the dental steps and the stay use (`agreements.services.draft_invoice` → `taxes.services.create_sales_draft_with_tax`): one line, the vaccine's item at the item's price on the day it is billed (an item at 250.00 gives a draft of exactly 250.00, plus VAT as the draft service computes it when VAT is on), `SI-` number, no cashbox, nothing paid. A dose is billed once; if its invoice is cancelled it can be billed again. **Posting that draft** on the sales page, by the ordinary posting service, is what puts the dose on the owner's account and takes a stock vaccine off the shelf (a service item moves no stock). Posting code is never called from here (a test scans the two modules for it). The reminder only writes an outbox row, sent by a click or by the existing WhatsApp run; it carries no amount.
- **Protected items touched:** the two migrations only: `medical/0014_vaccinations` (two new tables; no existing table or row is read or changed; it follows M8's `0013_inpatient`, so whichever of two open branches merges second renumbers or needs a merge migration) and `messaging/0006_vaccine_kind` (an `AlterField` on the `kind` column of two tables: one more **choice**, same `max_length` 12, no data, no column; the same shape as `0005_followup_kind`, HG-087). Posting, stock, cashbox, ledgers, average cost, report calculations, closing, the permission core and `save_visit` are untouched. No new permission and no seeded role.
- **Why no existing table serves:**
  - **`Vaccine`:** a vaccine is the clinic's own catalogue entry (names, species, days to the next dose, the item to bill). `master_data.Item` is what is sold and has no species or interval, and a vaccine may have no item at all (a campaign, a free dose); an `Item` row per vaccine would mix a clinical catalogue into the price list. `VisitTemplate` is text for a visit. The vaccine only *points* at an item, as a bed points at its daily service.
  - **`Vaccination`:** a dose is a dated fact about one animal and one vaccine with a due date, a batch and an invoice link. A `ClinicalVisit` is free text (today's «تطعيم» in a visit plus `follow_up_date` is a note nobody can list, filter or remind from), and one visit can hold several doses or none. `TreatmentStep` is dental and per patient. The dose optionally *points at* its visit.
- **Tables** (`medical/models.py`): `Vaccine`: `name_ar`, `name_en`, `species` (one of the animal file's own `Species` values, empty for any species), `interval_days` (empty for a one-off), `item` (Item, PROTECT, NULL), `active`, `entity` (NULL: the main entity, HG-034), `created_by`, `created_at`. `Vaccination`: `animal` (PROTECT), `vaccine` (PROTECT), `given_on`, `next_due` (NULL for a one-off), `batch`, `given_by` (Employee, SET_NULL), `visit` (ClinicalVisit, SET_NULL), `note` (clinical), `invoice` (SalesInvoice, PROTECT, NULL until billed), `entity`, `created_by`, `created_at`.
- **Left out on purpose: a nullable patient link** (the plan suggested one so a paediatric clinic could reuse the table). No checklist item asks for it (item 15 is «بيطري»), the animal's owner is already the customer, and a second subject would make `animal` nullable and every query two-sided. A paediatric schedule is a later item with its own Hard Gate and a migration then. Also folded away: the plan's separate «booster days» is one interval.
- **Rules** (`medical/vaccines.py`):
  - **Next due:** worked out from the vaccine's interval when the dose is recorded, unless the vet typed one (kept as typed, must be after the day given) or ticked «من غير جرعة جاية». An interval that is a whole number of years (365, 730, ...) lands on the **same calendar day** that many years on (29 Feb clamps to 28 Feb), so «سنوي» does not drift a day across a leap year; any other interval adds that many days.
  - **A later dose closes the earlier one's due date:** worked out when read, never stored (`open_doses`: no dose of the same animal and vaccine with a later day, or the same day and a later row). Removing a mistaken later dose brings the earlier due date back; a dose entered late for an older day is closed from the start. A later dose counts across entities (the animal belongs to the group).
  - **Recording:** the vaccine must be active, in the entity being worked in and for the animal's species (a cat's vaccine is refused for a dog; the form offers only matching ones); the day is today or earlier; one dose per animal, vaccine and day (stops a double click); the visit must be this animal's; the vet who gave it must be active.
  - **Removing:** a dose recorded by mistake, by its own recorder or an owner/manager, and only while it is on no live invoice (a cancelled one frees it).
  - **The due list:** the last dose of each vaccine of each animal whose date is overdue or within 30 days, in the entity being worked in, with the owner's name and phone; an animal that no longer comes («لسه بييجي» off) and an inactive owner are left out. Filters: all, overdue, this week (today to 7 days), this month (today to 30); search by animal, owner, phone or vaccine.
- **Reminder** (`messaging`): a new kind «vaccine» (`MessageKind.VACCINE`), **off until the owner switches it on** (as M3 did for the follow-up), listed in «نصوص الرسائل» only when some entity is a vet. Source `messaging.services._vaccines`: the last dose of a vaccine whose date falls today to 3 days ahead (`VACCINE_AHEAD_DAYS`; a constant because the template has no lead-time column and adding one is a schema change), not superseded, for an active animal and owner with a number; **one message per dose** (key `vaccine:<dose>`; a sent or skipped one is never queued again, whatever the day or `every_days`). The text carries the owner's name, the animal, the vaccine (in the business's language), the date in words and the clinic's name: **no note, no batch, no diagnosis, no doctor, no link** (the kind is not in `LINK_KINDS`; the placeholders cannot carry anything clinical: `{diagnosis}`, `{note}` and the like are refused). Two new words, `{animal}` and `{vaccine}`. A dose recorded before the message is sent makes the source stop yielding it and the existing gathering (`_gather`, last line) withdraws the waiting message (pinned; and the message comes back if that dose is removed). A date that passes while the message is unsent also withdraws it. Vet installations only (`vet.is_vet()` per entity). The outbox row's own `link` is reception's agenda on the due day, exactly as M3's follow-up (the animal's page is clinical and `link_ok` cannot tell that to a cashier; pinned).
- **Access (HG-084's rule is called, not copied):** every screen and POST needs `medical.services.can_view` / `can_write` (404 outside a vet install, 403 without a clinical permission; an employee link alone gives nothing). **Recording and removing:** `can_write` (owner, manager, doctor); a doctor removes only their own. **The vaccines set-up:** `medical.write_records` (owner, manager). **«اعمل فاتورة»:** `sales.create_sales_invoice` on top of clinical read (reception cannot open the animal's page, HG-084, so in practice owner and manager bill; the seeded doctor role holds no sales permission). **The due list and the owner's phone:** clinical read only; a cashier, a stock keeper or an accountant is refused and never shown a phone. The animal and its visits are group-wide (HG-084); a **vaccine and a dose belong to the entity they were made in** (HG-074): another entity's vaccine or dose answers 404 before any action runs; the due list, the set-up and the reminder keep to the entity; the draft lands on the dose's own entity's selling store whatever entity the biller works in. The animal card's dose history is group-wide, like its visits.
- **Audit rows** (`module="medical"`): `save_vaccine`, `record_vaccination` (ids and dates, `has_note` only, never the note), `remove_vaccination` (before data), `bill_vaccination` (invoice number and totals).
- **Where it shows:** a vaccination card at the top of the animal's page (what was given, when, next due, batch, who, the invoice, «سجّل تطعيم», «اعمل فاتورة», «شيل»), «التطعيمات» (the due list) and «أنواع التطعيمات» (set-up, owner and manager) as tabs next to the other vet screens, and the record form. A vet installation only. Dates are written in words (`day_text`); controls are 44 px; no horizontal scroll at 390 px.
- **Files:** `medical/models.py`, `medical/migrations/0014_vaccinations.py`, `medical/vaccines.py`, `medical/views_vaccines.py`, `medical/urls.py`, `medical/views.py` (the card's context, the tabs), templates `medical/_vaccination_card.html`, `_vaccine_fields.html`, `vaccination_form.html`, `vaccinations.html`, `vaccine_setup.html`, `_tabs.html`, `animal.html`, static `hesba/css/vaccines.css` (`?v=m7-1`), `messaging/models.py`, `messaging/migrations/0006_vaccine_kind.py`, `messaging/services.py`, `messaging/views.py` (the words list), `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py` (the vet extra), tests `medical/tests_vaccines.py`, `medical/tests_vaccines_ui.py`, `messaging/tests_m7.py`.
- **Tests:** `medical/tests_vaccines.py` (55), `medical/tests_vaccines_ui.py` (48) and `messaging/tests_m7.py` (34), 137 in all. **Rule and services:** the next-due rule at its boundaries (a year, from a leap year, the leap day, whole years, other intervals, a one-off; a typed date kept; none), a later dose closing the earlier without storing anything, removing the later one reopening it, a late-entered older dose closed from the start, validation (species, future day, due before given, another animal's visit, a duplicate day, a switched-off vaccine), set-up access and entities. **The due list:** overdue and the window, the filters and their counts, superseded and one-off doses absent, search, the owner's phone for those allowed and a refusal (never a phone) for a cashier, a stock keeper and an accountant, another entity's doses absent, a vet-only guard. **The bill:** a draft of exactly 250.00 with its lines, VAT as the draft service computes it (285.00 at 14%), once, again after a cancelled invoice, the price on the day billed, no item or a switched-off one refused, the permission, the dose's own entity's store, and counts of stock movements, cashbox movements, customer-ledger rows, payments and journal rows unchanged by recording and by drafting, stock leaving only when the draft is posted through the ordinary service (a service item never). **The reminder:** off by default, lead time and the due day, once per dose and never again after sent or skipped, withdrawn when the next dose is recorded first (and back when it is removed), withdrawn when the date passes, nothing for a non-vet clinic or a shop, a vet entity of a group with the clinic named, the text with no note, batch, doctor or link, the date in words, English, the two new words accepted and clinical ones refused, the outbox page, and the migration (two `AlterField` on `kind`, the choices only one longer, `makemigrations --check` clean). **Screens:** the card, the form, the due list, the set-up in Arabic and English, every screen and POST for every kind of person (403), another entity's dose, vaccine and visit ids (404), a non-vet and a non-medical business (404), the template and stylesheet scanners, no posting or stock call in the two modules, `0014` on a database holding an animal and a visit, and the vet demo data.
- **Risks:** (1) the due date of a dose is only as good as the interval the owner typed on the vaccine; a vet who gives a dose early or late types the real date. (2) The reminder is off until switched on, and it reaches the owner only if the customer has a number; it also needs the usual WhatsApp set-up to go by itself. (3) A dose cannot be edited, only removed and recorded again (removal is blocked on a live invoice). (4) Migration numbering with another open branch (see the protected items).
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. «سنوي» = the same calendar day next year, or exactly 365 days? (Calendar day for whole years; days for other intervals.)
  2. Reminder lead time and whether it also nags overdue doses? (3 days ahead, a constant; overdue doses are on the list but not messaged, so switching the kind on does not send a year of old reminders; off until switched on.)
  3. The message's date in words («12 نوفمبر 2026») or as numbers like M3's follow-up? (In words: it is read by an owner.)
  4. May reception bill a dose or see the due list to phone owners? (No: clinical read is required, as every clinical screen; reception would need its own narrower screen and a decision about owners' phones.)
  5. Should a vaccine of another species be refusable by a vet who knows better? (Refused; switch the vaccine's species to «كل الأنواع» for such a vaccine.)
  6. A sidebar entry for the due list? (Tabs only, where the other vet screens are.)
  7. A paediatric vaccination schedule? (Not built; see above.)
  8. Edit a recorded dose (batch, date)? (Not built: remove and record again.)

## HG-091 — E2 exams, marks and the report card (`education.Exam`, `education.ExamMark`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/e2-exams-report-card` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2 (two new tables, and who sees children's marks). Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «4. تعليمي», item 15 «امتحانات ودرجات وتقرير لولي الأمر», PR E2.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing moves. Creating an exam, entering a mark, publishing, and printing a report card write no sales invoice, cashbox movement, ledger row, journal entry, stock movement or outbox row (pinned by counts before and after a whole run). No mark, percentage or attendance rate is ever put in a message: there is no «send» button on any of the new screens, the services module imports neither `messaging` nor any posting module (a test scans its imports), and a saved sheet's flash message holds no number (pinned).
- **Protected items touched:** the migration only: `education/0008_exams_and_marks` (two new tables and three constraints; no existing table, column or row is read or changed; it follows `0007_language_levels`, so a branch that merges later renumbers). Posting, stock, cashbox, ledgers, average cost, report calculations, closing and the permission core are untouched. No new permission and no seeded role.
- **Why no existing table serves:**
  - **`PlacementTest`:** one test per student per course that **sets a level**. `levels.latest` and `levels.groups_at` read it to suggest groups with a free seat, so every monthly quiz stored there would become a "placement" and move the student's suggested level. It has no group and no day shared by a roster: a paper sat by a whole group on one day with one full mark has nowhere to live in it, and its `level` column is required.
  - **`Attendance` / `ClassSession`:** a mark is not a presence, and an exam need not fall on a class day.
  - **`Exam` is the group's, as a class session is:** it has no `entity` column; every read goes through `services.scoped(StudyGroup.objects)`.
- **Tables** (`education/models.py`): `Exam`: `group` (PROTECT), `title`, `kind` (quiz / monthly / midterm / final / other), `held_on`, `out_of` (decimal 7,2, greater than 0: a database check), `published` (default **off**), `notes`, `created_by`, `created_at`. `ExamMark`: `exam` (CASCADE), `student` (PROTECT), `score` (decimal 7,2, empty allowed), `absent`, `note`, `entered_by`, `entered_at`; one row per (exam, student) and a database check that a row is never absent **and** scored.
- **Rules** (`education/exams.py`):
  - **The roster:** marks are kept only for the students on `attendance.roster_on(group, held_on)`, the same rule attendance uses (enrolled by that day and not stopped before it). A student off that roster is refused, never skipped; so is a forged id.
  - **A mark:** a number from 0 to the exam's full mark (zero is a mark), at most two decimals; Arabic digits and a decimal comma are read; `NaN` and `Infinity` are refused. «غايب» and a score together are **refused with a message**, never silently reduced to one. A blank score with absent off means «not entered yet»: no row is kept for it (an earlier row is removed; a note alone keeps its row but still counts as not entered). The whole sheet is checked before anything is written.
  - **The day:** an exam may be announced for a day still to come, but marks wait for the day. A new exam needs a running group. Once any mark exists the exam's day cannot change (it decides the roster) and its full mark cannot fall below a score already given.
  - **A correction** writes one audit row (`record_marks`) holding every changed student's mark before and after; publishing and un-publishing and every exam edit are audited too. Nothing is audited when nothing changed.
  - **The stale sheet** (two people with one sheet open): the page carries a short digest of the exam's full mark and every mark with its absence and note. At save it is recomputed **from the database under a row lock on the exam**, the same lock every writer of the exam's marks takes; if it differs, the save is refused whole, nothing is written, and the page comes back from the database (not from its own old boxes) saying someone saved before. A page that carried no digest is refused as soon as any mark exists. It is the idea E1 gave the attendance sheet, done inside the service so it cannot be skipped.
  - **The report card for a period:** attendance by `attendance.rate` over `Attendance` rows in the period (held classes only; late counts as attended; whole percent) with present, late, absent and excused counts; every exam of the period the student was on the roster of, with score, full mark and percentage to one decimal; the **average of the percentages** over the exams the student sat (so 18 of 20 and 7.5 of 10 give 90% and 75% and an average of **82.5%**, not the 85% that summed marks would give), rounded once, half-up. An absent exam is listed and left out; an exam with no score entered is left out and counted as «not entered». **No rank, no other student's mark, ever:** every read is filtered to one student.
- **Who sees marks, and why:** **a manager** (`master_data.manage_parties`) and **the group's own teacher, for their own groups only** (`attendance.can_take`). Students are often minors and a mark is the kind of fact a family treats as private; the general read permission on student files, which a cashier, a stock keeper and an accountant hold (it shows the phone, the school and attendance), is **not** enough. A cashier gets **403** on the exams list, the new-exam form, the mark sheet and the report card, every POST on them is refused the same way, and the student and group pages draw **no marks card** (pinned with marker strings). A teacher is limited **per group**: for a student in groups A and B, the teacher of A sees only A's exams on the student page and in the report card, and A's classes in its attendance block. A teacher of none of the student's groups gets no card and a 403. Another entity's group, exam or student answers 404 before any permission check. A teacher with no student-file permission cannot open the group page, so «الامتحانات» is also a link on each of **today's classes** (the page such a teacher works from).
- **Files:** `education/models.py`, `education/migrations/0008_exams_and_marks.py`, `education/exams.py`, `education/views_exams.py`, `education/urls.py`, `education/views.py` (the two cards' context, the today link), `education/demo.py`, `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py` (one call), templates `education/exams.html`, `exam_form.html`, `marks.html`, `exam_report.html`, `_exam_card.html`, `_exam_list.html`, `_exam_fields.html`, `_marks_card.html`, `group.html`, `student.html`, `today.html`, static `hesba/css/education_exams.css` (`?v=e2-1`), `education_report.css`, `hesba/js/edu_marks.js`, tests `education/tests_exams.py`, `education/tests_exams_screens.py`, `reports/tests_demo_catalogs.py`.
- **Tests:** `education/tests_exams.py` (26, rules, card arithmetic, visibility, no money, the migration) and `education/tests_exams_screens.py` (25, screens and scanners), plus one demo test in `reports/tests_demo_catalogs.py`; 52 in all. **Rules:** an exam's fields, audit and refusals; who may save; the day and full mark held once marks exist; Arabic digits and the decimal comma; every refused mark with nothing written; absent with a score; blank means not entered; the roster of the exam's day (joined after, left before, left on the day itself, another group, a forged id); marks wait for the day; who may enter; the audit of a correction before and after; the stale digest at the service; publishing by a manager only. **The card:** 18/20 = 90.0, 7.5/10 = 75.0, average 82.5, an absent exam listed and excluded, an exam with no score counted as not entered, exact percentages averaged and rounded once (1/3 and 2/3 average 50.0), attendance 6 present + 1 late + 1 absent of 8 held = **88%** (7 of 8 is 87.5, which `attendance.rate` rounds to even), a cancelled class not held, a reversed range refused. **Visibility:** manager and teacher, a teacher of A not seeing B's exam of a shared student, a cashier and a teacher of none seeing nothing, the group list's counts, another entity. **No money, no message:** counts of sales invoices, cashbox movements, journal entries and outbox rows unchanged, the module's imports. **Screens:** the cards for each kind of person, 403 on every page and POST, 404 across entities, the sheet in both languages, saving, refusing, forging, the stale second save, an exam still to come, creating, editing and publishing, the report card in both languages, the empty range, a reversed period, no send button and no rank, the template and stylesheet scanners, `0008` on a database holding students, a group and attendance, and the education demo's two exams.
- **Risks:** (1) a published flag does nothing yet: it is read by the guardian portal (E3), which does not exist. (2) The report card is printed by staff and handed over; once printed it is paper and can be shown to anyone. (3) An exam cannot be deleted in this version (a mistaken one is edited or left with no marks entered; it is then left out of every average). (4) The percentage and the average use the exam's full mark as it is now; lowering a full mark below a given score is refused, but raising it changes every percentage of that exam. (5) Migration numbering with another open education branch (E3 `0009`, E6 `0010` are planned after this).
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Who sees marks? (A manager and the group's own teacher for their own groups; not the general student-file read permission. A dedicated permission, in the way HG-084 did for clinical records, is possible later.)
  2. May a teacher publish an exam to guardians? (No: a teacher enters marks, a manager publishes. Publishing is a click on the sheet and is audited.)
  3. Is a rank («الأول على المجموعة») ever wanted? (No: not built, not computed, and the report card reads one student only. Adding it later would need its own decision about children seeing each other's results.)
  4. How are percentages rounded? (One decimal, half-up, shown like money as `82.5%`; the average is taken over the exact percentages and rounded once, not over the rounded ones.)
  5. Should a mark change after the exam is published be blocked or flagged? (Allowed and audited; not flagged to the guardian.)
  6. Should a manager be able to delete an exam? (Not built; see risk 3.)

## HG-092 — M9 lab sample barcode label, finding a sample by its code, and importing results from an analyzer's file (`medical.LabTest.code`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/m9-lab-sample-barcode` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2 (one new column and its uniqueness, a clinical feature that writes lab results from a file). Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «3. طبي», item 21 «باركود العينة وربط أجهزة المعمل», PR M9; counted «جزئي» (see Out of scope).
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing. No sales invoice, payment, cashbox movement, stock movement, ledger row or journal entry is made, changed or read for its amounts: printing a label and opening a sample write nothing; the import writes lab **results** (and audit rows), through the same `lab.record_results` a person types into. Counts of stock, cashbox, customer-ledger, payment, journal and invoice rows are pinned equal before and after the label, the scan, the preview and the apply. The only place money is even *looked at* is to know whether a test was ordered: the lab bills its tests as ordinary services on a sales invoice, so the import reads the patient's live invoices of the day (customer, date, status and the lines' items; **never an amount**) and nothing else of them. Three modules (`samples.py`, `lab_import.py`, `views_lab.py`) are scanned by a test for any posting, stock, cashbox, ledger, journal or average-cost word.
- **Protected items touched:** one migration only: `medical/0015_lab_test_code` (an `AddField` `LabTest.code`, `CharField(max_length=20, blank=True, default="")`, and an `AddConstraint`: unique when not empty, `UniqueConstraint(fields=["code"], condition=~Q(code=""))`). No existing row is read or changed (every existing test gets an empty code), no data is seeded, and it follows M7's `0014_vaccinations`, so whichever open branch merges second renumbers. Posting, stock, cashbox, ledgers, average cost, report calculations, closing, the permission core and `record_results`/`save_visit` are untouched (`save_test` learns one optional key, `code`, and **leaves a test's code as it is when a caller sends none**, so the demo and older callers never wipe it). No new permission and no seeded role.
- **Why no table for the sample or for the import:** a sample is one patient's tubes of one day, the same pair M6's link is keyed on (patient, `taken_on`); its code is computed from them. The import is a read of a file, a preview and a write of results: the rows travel from the preview to the apply in a signed, 30-minute token bound to the user (no table), and every line is judged again inside the transaction that writes it.
- **The test code** (`LabTest.code`): the analyzer's own short name for the test (`GLU`, `HB`), kept **upper case**, up to 20 characters of letters, digits and `. - _ / +` (no space, comma or semicolon: it travels in a CSV and on a label), unique among the lab's tests when set (checked in `save_test` with a message naming the other test, and by the database for two people saving at once). It is edited where the tests are set up («التحاليل والمعدلات»), shown there, and printed on the label; a test without one shows the start of its name (12 characters) on the label and **cannot be matched by an import**. An analyzer's code for a test that is switched off reads as «كود تحليل مش معروف».
- **The sample code:** `L<patient id>-<yyyymmdd>`, e.g. `L12-20261112`. It is two numbers: the customer's internal id and the day. It carries **no phone, name, file number or diagnosis**, and it is **not a secret and not M6's key**: M6's link is a signed token (own salt, expiry, a fingerprint of the day's results, a second factor). The label never carries that token, any part of it, `/results/` or a WhatsApp link (a test makes an M6 link first and asserts none of it is on the label). Why that is safe: a label is seen by anyone who holds the tube, so the code must open nothing by itself. It does not: typing or scanning it only takes a **signed-in** member of the lab's staff, who may read lab results, to the patient's results page of that day, behind the same checks as the page itself (lab entity, `can_view`). An id is an enumerable counter; knowing it tells a stranger only how many customers the lab has numbered so far.
- **Who may do what** (HG-084's rule is called, not copied): **opening a sample by its code and printing its label** need `medical.services.can_view` (owner, manager, doctor, or a custom role with `medical.view_records`): 404 outside a lab (or in a non-lab entity of a group), 403 otherwise (a cashier, a stock keeper, a login with no role; reception cannot print labels, see question 2), a login with no account goes to sign-in. **Importing** (the upload, the preview and the apply POST) needs `can_write` (`medical.write_records` or `medical.treat_patients`: owner, manager, doctor): a read-only role gets 403 on all three, and a preview token made by someone who has since lost the right is refused at the apply (checked again there and again inside `lab_import.apply`). A token is bound to the user who made it.
- **Entities:** lab tests, results and customers are **group-wide** (HG-043, HG-084): there is no `entity` column on `LabTest` or `LabResult`, and this PR adds none (only `code` was allowed). So «another entity's code → not found» holds the way it can: every M9 screen follows `lab.is_lab()` for **the entity being worked in**, so from a non-lab entity of a group the scan, the label and the import all answer 404 (tested), and the one entity-scoped thing the import reads, the patient's order (invoices of the entity being worked in, `entities.scope.SALES_INVOICE`), is read in that entity only: an order made in another entity's store reads as «مش مطلوب للمريض», and nothing is written (tested both ways).
- **The label** (`/medical/patients/<id>/lab/<day>/label/` or `.../lab/label/?day=`): a standalone print page. Its own print CSS for a common **50 × 25 mm** label (one per page for a label printer, named `@page`) and a fallback **A4** sheet of the same labels (4 per row); 2 copies by default on the roll, 8 on A4, at most 40. Each label: the patient's name, the day in words (`day_text`), a **Code 128** barcode of the sample code (`barcode.symbology`, the same drawer the item labels use; the test decodes the drawn bars back to the code), the code in text, and the tests' short names (the first 6, then «+n»). Printable **before any result exists**: the tests are those ordered that day (below) or already resulted. A button «اطبع ملصق العيّنة» with a day picker sits on the patient's results page, and a link on every day that has results.
- **Finding a sample:** a «العيّنات» tab with one box that takes a hardware scanner (types, Enter) and the phone camera (`camera_scan.js`; one 10-line script submits the form the camera's synthetic Enter cannot). A good code redirects to the patient's results page on that day (`?day=`, the entry form's date is that day, the day is highlighted); anything else (malformed, no such patient, the walk-in customer, a day that has not come) is the same 404 page with «مفيش عيّنة بالكود ده», and shows nothing about any patient.
- **The import** (`medical/lab_import.py`, `medical/views_lab.py`, tab «استيراد النتايج»):
  - **The file:** `.csv` only (an xlsx, a `.txt`, or a binary renamed `.csv` is refused), up to 5 MB and 5000 lines (the opening-data import's limits, `imports.screen_services.MAX_BYTES` / `MAX_ROWS`), UTF-8 with or without a byte-order mark and then Windows Arabic (the same order that import tries), comma **or** semicolon (chosen from the first line: `;` when it has more semicolons than commas, because `csv.Sniffer`, which the other import uses, reads `L5-20261112;GLU;12,5` as comma-separated), Windows or Unix line ends, blank lines skipped, a header row **or none** (the first line is a header only when its first cell names the sample or its third names the value), extra columns ignored. Not built on `read_table`: it always takes line 1 as a header, accepts xlsx and sniffs.
  - **Per line, what applying would do** (shown in a preview that writes nothing): **write** (a new result); **same** (a result is already entered with this value: left); **differs** (already entered with another value: left, both values shown, never overwritten); **not ordered** (the test was not ordered for that patient that day); **unknown sample** (not a code, no such patient, the walk-in customer, or a day that has not come); **unknown test** (no active test has that code); **not a number** (the test has a normal range and the value is not a number: refused for that line only); **duplicate** (the same sample and test again: an identical repeat is dropped, a conflicting one holds **every** occurrence back, none is written); **incomplete** (fewer than 3 columns, an empty or a too long cell).
  - **Numeric means a range:** a test with a low or a high limit is numeric and must get a number (`lab.number`: Arabic digits, `12,5`, `7,500`; `<0.5` is refused: see question 6). A test with no range takes a number if the value is one, else the value is its **text** result (blood group, `negative`).
  - **Apply** (`lab_import.apply`, one `transaction.atomic`): judges every line **again**, writes **only** the lines that would be written, grouped per patient and day, through `lab.record_results` (the typed path: the test's current unit and range are copied onto the result, the flag is worked out by `lab.flag`, one audit row per result), and reports what was written and what was left, line by line. A failure halfway rolls everything back (tested). **Importing the same file twice changes nothing the second time**: every line is then «same» and nothing is written, no audit row of any kind is made, no `updated_at` moves, and an M6 link made before still opens (all tested). The audit row for an import that wrote something is `import_lab_results` (file name, line count, written, patients, days, what was left, never a value or a name).
  - **A line never creates a patient, a test, an invoice or an order.** It fills a test that was **ordered** for that patient that day, where *ordered* = a live (not cancelled, draft or posted) sales invoice of the entity being worked in, that customer, `invoice_date` = the sample day, with a line whose item is the test's `service` (one service may cover several tests: the CBC covers Hb and WBC), **or** the test already has a result that day. A day with no order and no result says «مفيش تحاليل مطلوبة للمريض في اليوم ده».
- **Out of scope** (recorded under «بعدين» in the roadmap): **live analyzer connections (ASTM, HL7) and bidirectional worklists** (sending the day's orders to the analyzer): they need a real analyzer to test against and the manufacturer's protocol, and the same «mock first, Ahmed decides to switch it on» rule as every external link. A lab table of orders (below, question 1).
- **Files:** `medical/models.py`, `medical/migrations/0015_lab_test_code.py`, `medical/lab.py` (the code), `medical/samples.py`, `medical/lab_import.py`, `medical/views_lab.py`, `medical/views.py` (the tabs, the results page's day and label card, the tests words), `medical/urls.py`, templates `medical/lab_label.html`, `lab_sample.html`, `lab_import.html`, `lab_results.html`, `lab_tests.html`, `_tabs.html`, static `hesba/css/lab_label.css` (`?v=m9-1`), `hesba/css/lab_sample.css` (`?v=m9-1`), `hesba/js/lab_sample.js`, `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py` (the lab demo: two tests carry `HB` and `GLU`), tests `medical/tests_m9.py` and `medical/tests_m9_ui.py`.
- **Tests:** `medical/tests_m9.py` and `medical/tests_m9_ui.py`. **The code:** upper case and trimmed, audited, unique whatever the case with the other test named, characters and length, a caller that sends none never wipes it, the database constraint with many empty codes. **The sample code:** its form, what a scanner or an Arabic keyboard types, 13 malformed codes, a real patient on a day that has come, the drawn bars equal `code128_modules` and decode back to the code, the order rule (a service covering two tests, a cancelled invoice, another day, another entity's store, results). **The file:** header or none, comma or semicolon (including the decimal-comma semicolon file), BOM, Windows line ends, blank lines, extra columns, Windows Arabic, bad rows kept, not a CSV, binary, empty, header only, too big, too many lines. **Every status** with one small file (and the conflicting and identical repeats, numeric versus word tests, a switched-off test). **Apply:** exactly the good lines written; the same flag, range, unit and value as the same result typed (17.5 is high in both); words stored as text; the same file twice changes nothing (no audit row, `updated_at`, `entered_by`, and an M6 link still opens); a typed result never overwritten; a stale preview judged again; one transaction; the audit row holding no value or name; only writers; no patient, test, order or money row made. **The screens, both languages:** the label (barcode, name, day, tests, no phone, file number, diagnosis, note, result or M6 token, sizes and copies, bad days and strangers, who prints it, nothing moves), the box (every kind of code, English, who opens it, a non-lab entity), the results page and tests set-up (the code field and its errors), the import (upload, preview, every status in words, apply and the second apply, forged, stale, someone else's and a de-permissioned maker's token, files that cannot be read, who may open, preview and apply for every kind of person, a non-lab business, an order in another entity, no money), the template and stylesheet scanners, `0015` on a database holding tests and a result, `makemigrations --check`, and the lab demo.
- **Risks:** (1) **a wrong file for the wrong day:** a line carries its own sample code, hence its own patient and day, so a file exported on another day only matches the day in its codes: it never lands on today by accident; but an analyzer that prints a code the lab typed wrongly sends the result to the patient that code names, and the preview shows the patient's name so a person can see it before confirming. (2) **a value overwriting a typed one:** not built: an already-entered result is never written by a file, and the preview says «متسجّل بقيمة تانية» with both values (question 3). (3) **M6:** importing into a day kills any results link already sent for that day (the link's fingerprint covers the day's results, exactly as typing a result does); the result screen says so. (4) **A lab that does not bill by test** cannot import a result for a test no invoice orders and that has no result yet (it is «مش مطلوب للمريض»): question 1. (5) **Camera scan** works only over HTTPS (as the other scan boxes). (6) A number an analyzer prints with a flag (`<0.5`, `>1000`, `12.5 H`) is refused on a numeric test (question 6). (7) Migration numbering with another open branch (see the protected items).
- **Found by Codex's review and closed here:** (1) between the import's judging and its write, a result typed by a clinician could be overwritten, because `lab.record_results` corrects an existing row. The import now calls it with `only_new=True`: an existing row, or one that arrives at the same moment, is left exactly as it is and the line is reported «متسجّل قبل كده» (same or different value). Typing a result still corrects, as before. (2) A headerless semicolon file whose first value carried thousands separators (`L12-20261112;PLT;1,250,000`) was read as comma-separated; the separator is now chosen by which one makes the first lines look like rows.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. What is an «order» in a lab, given the program has no order table? (A test is ordered when the patient has a live sales invoice that day with the test's service, or the test already has a result. A lab that does not invoice by test needs either that link set on its tests, or a real orders table with its own Hard Gate; the check is one function, `samples.ordered`, so relaxing it, for example to «any active test», is a one-line change.)
  2. May reception print the label? (No: it names a patient, so it needs the right to read patient records, and reception holds none under HG-084; a narrower «print labels» permission would be a change to the permission core.)
  3. Should a file overwrite a result that is already entered? (No; it is shown and left. An «overwrite what differs» tick on the preview would be the next step, and then the audit row should carry the old value.)
  4. A check digit on the sample code, so a mistyped one cannot open another patient's page? (No: the label also prints the name, and the page opened shows the patient's name; the code stays short enough for an analyzer's field.)
  5. Should the sample code carry the **entity** or a **sequence per sample** (several tubes of one day)? (No: one code per patient and day, the same pair as M6's link; two tubes of the same day share it and the label can be printed twice.)
  6. Analyzer values like `<0.5` or `>1000`? (Refused on a numeric test for that line; keep them as typed text on the results page. Reading `<` and `>` into a number would need a flag column, a schema change.)
  7. An analyzer's code for a test that is **switched off**? (Unknown test; switch the test on to import it.)
  8. A `.xlsx` or a tab-separated analyzer export? (Not read: `.csv` with a comma or a semicolon only, as asked.)
  9. Live ASTM / HL7 and a worklist to the analyzer? (Not built; see Out of scope.)

## HG-093 — E5 automatic absence and fee messages to the guardian (a choices-only `messaging` migration)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/e5-absence-fee-messages` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2 (a protected choices-only migration, a change to who the general reminder reaches). Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «4. تعليمي», item 13 «رسالة لولي الأمر», PR E5.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing moves and no number changes. The two sources read attendance marks, fee invoices and the customer balance and write **only outbox rows** (`messaging.OutboxMessage`); a test scans `education/messages.py` for posting, payment, cashbox, journal and write calls and finds none, and counts of sales invoices, payments, cashbox movements, customer-ledger rows and journal entries are identical before and after gathering, skipping, sending by click and the hourly send. The balance in the fees message is read from `education.billing.dues` (`reports.aging.aging_rows`, the customer ledger's FIFO), the same figure the «المتأخرات» screen shows; no second way to work out a balance was written.
- **Protected item touched:** `messaging/migrations/0007_absence_fees_kinds.py` only: an `AlterField` on the `kind` column of `MessageTemplate` and of `OutboxMessage`, two more **choices** (`absence`, `fees`), same `max_length` 12, no data, no column, the shape of `0005_followup_kind` (HG-087) and `0006_vaccine_kind` (HG-090). It depends on 0006. Posting, stock, cashbox, ledgers, average cost, report calculations (`reports/aging.py` is only called, never edited), closing, the permission core and attendance (`take`, `scan`) are untouched. No new permission, no new table.
- **The absence message:**
  - **Contains:** the guardian's name, the student's name, the group's name, the day in words («12 نوفمبر 2026») and the centre's name. Default: «أهلاً {name}، {student} كان غايب يوم {date} عن {group} في {company}.» (English: «Hello {name}, {student} was absent on {date} from {group} at {company}.»).
  - **Never contains:** a reason, the mark's note, an attendance rate, a grade, the student's stage or notes, the student's own phone, or a link. Students are often minors. A test puts a marker string in the mark's note and in the student's notes and asserts neither reaches the text; the kind is not in `LINK_KINDS`; the placeholders cannot carry a note, a rate or a grade (`{grade}`, `{note}`, `{rate}`, `{reason}` are refused by the template editor, as before). The outbox row's own `link` is the student's page inside Hesba, shown only to people who may open it, never in the text.
  - **To whom:** the student's payer (the guardian's customer record), WhatsApp number first, else phone; nothing if there is no number or the guardian is switched off.
  - **Which marks:** «غايب» in a class that was **held**, dated **today or yesterday only**, for an active student of the entity being gathered. Late and excused are not messaged (the attendance sheet's existing one-tap WhatsApp for a late arrival stays). A cancelled class is never messaged.
  - **Why two days:** the kind is off until the owner switches it on, and switching it on must not send a term of old absences to every guardian. Two days also covers a class closed late in the evening and gathered the next morning.
  - **One message per student per class, once:** key `absence:<class session id>:<student id>`. A sent or skipped one is never queued again, whatever `every_days` says (the same «closed» rule as M7's vaccine), including after a teacher saves the sheet again.
  - **Correcting a mark:** a mark changed to present before the message goes out stops being yielded and the existing gathering (`_gather`, last line) withdraws the waiting message (pinned; it comes back if the student is marked absent again). A message **already sent cannot be withdrawn**; the settings text for the kind says so in Arabic and English.
  - **Scan (E1):** a scan writes no absent mark; absent marks appear when a teacher saves (closes) the sheet, and from that moment they are messaged. A student who scans in late afterwards turns absent into late: the waiting message is withdrawn, a sent one stays.
- **The fees reminder:**
  - **Contains:** the guardian's name, the amount still due and the centre's name. Default: «أهلاً {name}، تذكير ودّي من {company}: المتبقي من مصروفات الدراسة على حضرتك {amount}. لو حضرتك دفعت خلاص، اعتبر الرسالة دي كأنها ماوصلتش. شكرًا لحضرتك.» **Never** the child's name, the group, an invoice number, a date or a link.
  - **Who:** a guardian with at least one **posted** fee invoice (an invoice a `FeeCharge` points at, not cancelled; a draft never) dated at least `FEES_AFTER_DAYS` (3, a constant: no lead-time column exists on the template and adding one is a schema change) days ago, who still owes (a row on the dues screen), and whose ledger still holds an open piece that old (FIFO). A guardian whose old invoice is paid but who has a newer open debt is not chased.
  - **How much:** the guardian's balance as the dues screen shows it, capped at what the old fee invoices were sold on credit for (`remaining_due`), so a non-fee debt (a shop sale, an opening balance) is not named in a fees message. Explicit cases pinned: an invoice of 900.00 posted 4 days ago with 300.00 paid → 600.00; two children of one guardian with 600.00 and 450.00 → one message of 1,050.00; two posted invoices of 900.00 → one message of 1,800.00; 2 days old → none (and 3 days old tomorrow → one); a draft → none; fully paid → none and a waiting message withdrawn; a cancelled invoice → none.
  - **One per guardian, repeated at most every 7 days:** key `fees:<guardian id>`, `every_days` 7: a sent one is not queued again until the seventh day (day 6 none, day 7 one). A **skipped** one counts like a sent one (it is not queued again every morning; this differs from the general reminder, where a skipped one comes back the next day). A waiting message moves to today with today's balance (a part payment lowers it).
  - **Relation to the general «مبلغ مستحق» reminder (`DUE`):** while the fees kind is **on** and the entity being gathered is a school or a centre, the general reminder skips every guardian the fees source covers (`messaging.services._fee_covered`), so the same money is not asked for twice; a waiting general message for such a guardian is withdrawn by the same gathering. A guardian the fees source does not cover (no fee invoice, or one under 3 days old) still gets the general reminder. With the fees kind **off**, or in any business that is not a school, `DUE` is exactly what it was (pinned with a commercial installation: key, name, phone, link and text identical with the fees kind on and off). The covered set is the same helper the fees source uses.
- **Off by default:** both kinds are off until the owner switches them on, as M3's follow-up and M7's vaccine are (`DEFAULT_OFF`). An absence message cannot be taken back, and the fees reminder changes who is reminded, so neither should start by itself on deploy.
- **Where they show:** in «رسائل العملاء» (the outbox and its «نصوص الرسائل») with the names «غياب طالب» / «Student absence» and «مصروفات مستحقة» / «Fees due», **only when some entity is a school or a centre** (`templates()` hides them otherwise, as it does the clinic and vet kinds). A one-line hint under each kind's wording says what it sends, to whom, and that a sent absence message cannot be withdrawn. No new screen, no new route. The attendance sheet's one-tap WhatsApp (`attendance.parent_message`) is unchanged. `{student}` and `{group}` are two new words the template editor accepts and `render` fills.
- **Sending by itself:** the hourly `run_scheduled` (8:00-21:00 Cairo) gathers both kinds like the others and sends a kind through the existing WhatsApp Business account **only if that kind has an approved template name** (`wa_template`). That needs the shop's own WhatsApp Cloud API keys (`WHATSAPP_TOKEN`, `WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID`) **and** a template Meta approved for each of the two kinds (body parameters: the company and the message). Without them the messages wait in the outbox for one tap, as every other kind does today. Nothing external was connected or called.
- **Files:** `messaging/models.py`, `messaging/migrations/0007_absence_fees_kinds.py`, `messaging/services.py`, `messaging/views.py` (the words list), `templates/messaging/outbox.html` (the hint line), `education/messages.py` (new: the two sources), tests `messaging/tests_e5.py` and one line in `messaging/tests_m7.py` (its migration test pinned 0006 against the live enum; it now pins 0006's own list).
- **Tests:** `messaging/tests_e5.py` (51): both kinds off by default; last week's absence not queued, today's and yesterday's once each; the key; a cancelled class, late, excused and present not messaged; a class closed after a scan (the unscanned are messaged only then, and a late scan afterwards withdraws the waiting one); a correction to present withdraws the waiting message (and it comes back); a sent one stays in the record; saving the sheet again after a send queues nothing (the mark ids change, the key does not); sent and skipped never again the next day; the text with a marker in the note and in the student's notes and no link; the guardian's WhatsApp, then phone, never the student's own; no number / inactive guardian / inactive student; another entity's student; the English wording; the date in words; fees 900.00 − 300.00 = 600.00, 450.00 + 600.00 = 1,050.00, two invoices 1,800.00, 2 days none, draft none, paid none and withdrawn, cancelled none, a non-fee debt not chased, 7-day repeat, skip, today's balance, no number; `DUE` not doubled and a guardian outside the fees source still reminded; a commercial installation's `DUE` identical and neither kind listed or gathered; the two words accepted and unknown ones refused; both screens in both languages; `run_scheduled` with the sender mocked sending inside the hours and leaving a kind without a template waiting, nothing at night, never twice; counts of sales invoices, payments, cashbox movements, ledger rows and journal entries unchanged; the migration (two `AlterField` on `kind`, two more choices, `makemigrations --check` clean).
- **Risks:** (1) An absence is gathered once its class has ended (the group's start time plus its length; a group with no start time has no end to wait for, and a class of an earlier day is over): a teacher who saves the sheet mid-class cannot have a guardian messaged about a student who is still on the way. After the class ends the next hourly run (inside 8:00-21:00) sends it; a correction before that run still withdraws it. (2) The fees amount is the guardian's balance as the dues screen shows it, capped at the old fee invoices' credit; with an old fee invoice, a newer open invoice and a part payment (FIFO pays the oldest first), the message can name more than the old invoice alone is still owed. (3) An absence message cannot be taken back once sent; the hint says so. (4) Automatic sending needs WhatsApp Cloud API keys and two Meta-approved templates, not connected. (5) The outbox page gathers on every open, so a message can appear the moment a sheet is saved. (6) Migration numbering with another open branch (another `messaging` migration renumbers). (7) The general reminder skips a covered guardian **entirely** while the fees kind is on, whereas the fees amount is capped at the old fee invoices' credit: a covered guardian's overdue non-fee debt (a book sale, an opening balance) gets no message from either kind until the fee invoices are settled. Default: skip the guardian entirely (never two messages for one guardian about money); the owner can switch the fees kind off to bring the general reminder back.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Should the absence message wait until the class has ended (so a late student is not reported absent mid-class)? (Yes: built that way after review. The alternative, messaging as soon as the teacher saves, reaches the guardian sooner but can alarm them for a ten-minute delay.)
  2. Both kinds on or off when the owner first sees them? (Off, as M3 and M7; the owner switches each on.)
  3. Message a late arrival too? (No: late stays the one-tap on the attendance sheet.)
  4. Absence window: two days (today and yesterday)? (Two days, a constant.)
  5. Wait 3 days after the invoice before the fees reminder, and repeat every 7? (3 days, a constant; 7 days, the owner can change the repeat on the kind.)
  6. A skipped fees reminder: ask again the next morning like the general reminder, or wait the 7 days? (Wait the 7 days.)
  7. The fees amount: the guardian's whole balance, or only the old fee invoices? (The balance as the dues screen shows it, capped at the old fee invoices' credit.)
  8. Should a guardian's reminder name the child? (No: the amount and the centre only; one message may cover several children.)
  9. A guardian message to a number the guardian typed as phone only, not WhatsApp? (Yes: WhatsApp number first, else phone, as every other kind.)

## HG-094 — Restaurants R13: the table bill obeys the shift rule, and voiding a line already sent to the kitchen is controlled

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/resto-r13-shift-void` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2: no table, no column, no migration, no new permission; one behaviour change to the waiter role (below). Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «5. مطاعم وكافيهات», item 12 «كاشير شغال من غير نت وورديات» and item 6's footnote (the waiter voiding fired food). The roadmap's R1–R3 ids are taken by other work, hence "Restaurants R13".
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:**
  - **No posting change.** A table's bill is still one posted `POS-` invoice made by the cashier screen's own `sales.pos.checkout`, unchanged: same lines, same VAT per line, same bill discount after tax, same cashbox movement, same customer-ledger row, same stock movements, same journal. A characterization test written before the refactor pins every row (`restaurant/tests_pay_characterization.py`): for example two dishes at 120.00 and 85.00 with 14% VAT post 205.00 + 28.70 = 233.70, and exactly 233.70 goes into the chosen cashbox.
  - **Where «وردية قبل البيع» (`shift_required`) is on, a table's cash now always falls inside an open shift.** Paying a table calls the till's own check, `shifts.gate.require_open_shift`, at the same place the till calls it (the screen, before the service runs). Without an open shift of their own the cashier is refused with the till's sentence, «افتح وردية الأول عشان تسجّل بيع.» / "Open a shift first to record a sale.", and nothing is written: no plate made, no invoice, no payment, no cash or stock movement, no ledger row, no journal entry (pinned by counts). Inside a shift the movement is the cashier's on that cashbox, so `shifts.services.summary` counts it: a 500.00 float plus a 233.70 bill reads 733.70 expected; a bill left partly on account adds only the cash taken. The shift's calculation is not changed. With the rule off nothing changes. The services stay ungated, exactly like the till's `checkout` (AN4's design: the demo and imports post without a shift).
  - **A line already sent to the kitchen needs a right and a reason to be voided.** A line not sent yet is removed by whoever may take orders, as before. A sent one needs `sales.create_sales_invoice` (the right that already guards paying, dispatching and cancelling an order) and a reason. Voiding moves no money: the line just leaves the bill, as before.
- **Why:**
  - With the rule on, a cashier with no open shift could still take money from the order screen. `shifts.services.movements` counts by cashier, cashbox and time, so that cash belonged to no shift and the drawer count could not reconcile.
  - HG-048 lets the waiter "add and void lines" and is silent on lines the kitchen already has, so a waiter could void fired food: voiding every sent line is a cancel by another name, and cancelling needs the selling right.
- **The pay seam:** `restaurant.services.pay` keeps its signature and its single transaction and now reads one call per step: `_payable` (lock the open order, its live lines, the selling store, the cashbox), `kitchen.prepare_shortfall`, `bill_rows(order, lines, lang)` (the invoice rows, the delivery fee as its own line), `_settle_and_post` (the unchanged `checkout`, which drafts, takes the payment and posts in one transaction, so settling and posting cannot be separate calls without changing the sales engine) and `_close` (the order keeps its invoice, the table is free, the `pay_order` audit row). Later restaurant tasks add one call to a step.
- **Files:** `restaurant/services.py` (`pay` split; `void_line` with the right and the reason; `VOID_SENT_RIGHT`; two messages), `restaurant/views.py` (`require_open_shift` before paying; `shift_missing` for the screen; the reason passed to `void_line`; one word), `templates/restaurant/order.html` (the till's «افتح وردية الأول» bar in place of the bill for whoever may sell and has no shift; the sent line's «إلغاء السطر» with its reason box only for whoever may sell), tests `restaurant/tests_pay_characterization.py`, `restaurant/tests_shift_void.py`, and small changes to `restaurant/tests.py` and `restaurant/tests_roles.py` (three screen tests that paid with no shift now open one; two service tests that voided a sent line now give a reason).
- **Found in review and closed here:** the bill's POST read its cashbox by id alone, so a crafted request could put a table's cash into another entity's cashbox (the form itself only ever listed this entity's). The view now takes only a cashbox the form lists (`_own_cashbox`: this entity's, active, never a held kind); a foreign id is treated as no cashbox and the bill is refused. Pinned by `test_a_crafted_pay_cannot_put_the_cash_in_another_entitys_cashbox`. The customer picker on the same form is left as it is (customers are listed unscoped on the page itself today).
- **Audit:** the void keeps its action name `void_order_line` (the daily follow-up in `reports/followup.py` counts it) and now carries the line, the dish code and name, the quantity, the amount, the ticket number and the reason; who and when are the audit row's own actor and time.
- **Tests:** `restaurant/tests_pay_characterization.py` (5): every row written by paying five orders (VAT on and off, a bill discount with a stocked drink and a voided sent line, a delivery with its fee for a named customer paying part, a kitchen-made dish produced at payment), row counts of every model included. `restaurant/tests_shift_void.py` (13): refused with the till's words in Arabic and English and nothing written; the bar in place of the bill and the shift opened there coming back to the order with its cashbox chosen; the shift's expected cash (733.70, then + 205.00 with VAT off, then + only the 50.00 paid in cash); the till and the table refusing and accepting together with no shift, another user's shift, a closed shift, the rule off and their own shift; a waiter never shown the bar and still refused with a 403; voids by a waiter (unsent allowed, sent refused even with a reason or by calling the service), by the right without a reason (refused in both languages), with the right and a reason (voided, audited, the order's total after each, nothing written off, the void never billed), crafted posts (another table's line id, a closed order), and who is shown which void action in Arabic and English.
- **Risks:** (1) A cashier can still pick a cashbox other than their shift's in the bill's list; that cash then belongs to no shift, exactly as at the till (the order screen at least preselects the shift's cashbox; the till preselects the default cashbox). Not changed here. (2) A café that switched the rule on and bills tables without opening shifts will now be stopped at the bill until the cashier opens one; the bar opens it in place. (3) The reason lives in the audit row only, not on the line (no column), so the order screen does not show it yet.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. May a waiter still void a line that already went to the kitchen? (No: the selling right and a reason, as cancelling the order. A waiter still removes a line not sent yet. The alternative is to keep HG-048's wording literally and let the floor void anything, with the reason only.)
  2. What counts as "sent"? (A line on a kitchen ticket, that is after «ابعت للمطبخ», printed or not. A line typed but not sent is the waiter's to change.)
  3. Should voiding a sent kitchen-made dish write the plate off as waste? (Not in this task: nothing is written off and a cooked-then-voided kitchen dish still never leaves stock, so stock and the cost of sales miss it. Planned with the kitchen screen, where the kitchen says «اتعمل فعلاً؟», because it moves stock.)
  4. A second person's approval for voids above an amount? (Not built.)
  5. List the open tables when a shift is closed? (Not built here: a table still eating is billed in the next shift by whoever takes the money.)

## HG-095 — Contracting C2a / C2b: a project's documents and the daily site report (`projects.SiteReport`, `projects.SiteReportRow`, and two new owner types in the attachments registry)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/contract-c2-documents-site-report` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2 (two new tables and a migration, a registry change in `attachments`, no money). Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «6. مقاولات», items 21 «مستندات ورسومات المشروع» (C2a) and 13 «تقرير يومي للموقع: صور وعمالة وطقس» (C2b). The id «C1» belongs to an older Hard Gate, so these are always written Contracting C2a and C2b.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing. A document or a daily report moves no money, stock, cashbox, ledger or journal entry and changes no quantity of the bill of quantities, no certificate, no cost and no contract value (pinned by counts and by the contract's own position before and after: sales invoices, payments, customer-ledger rows, stock movements, cashbox movements, journal entries and lines, certificates and their lines, every BOQ line's quantity and rate and what is certified of it, every project's contract value). **The quantities executed on a certificate are NOT taken from the daily report**: a report says «صب 40 م³» in words and in a head count, and the certificate still bills exactly what was typed on the certificate screen (a test makes a report say 40 and a certificate bill 12, and the figure is the certificate's). The report modules do not import `projects.contract` and a test scans them for posting, stock, cashbox and ledger calls.
- **Protected items touched:** the migration and its tables only: `projects/0003_site_reports` (two new tables and one unique constraint; no existing table or row is read or changed; it follows `0002_contract_boq_certificates`, so whichever of the open branches that add a `projects` migration merges second renumbers). In `attachments`: **no migration and no schema change** (see C2a below), but the registry (`attachments/services.py`) gains two owner types, two optional per-owner limits and one optional flag, and the scope helpers are touched; the existing owners (work order, purchase invoice, visit, patient file) behave exactly as before (the whole attachments suite is unchanged and green). Posting, stock, cashbox, ledgers, average cost, report calculations, closing and the permission core are untouched. No new permission code and no seeded role.
- **C2a needs no new table.** A project's document is an ordinary `attachments.Attachment` owned by the project (the same upload endpoint code, the same type sniffing, the same 5 MB per-file cap, the same PDF script refusal, the same audit rows, files stored in the database as HG-072). What the plan called a choices-only `attachments/0003` migration is **not needed and was not made**: the six kinds ride on six of the seven stages the table already allows (`projects/documents.py` `KIND_STAGE`: contract → `receive`, drawing → `scan`, letter → `report`, permit → `deliver`, photo → `photo`, other → `other`). The cost, honestly: the audit rows and the database show the table's own words (`receive`, `scan`...) and only `projects.documents` knows what they mean on a project; if Ahmed prefers readable stored words, that is a later choices-only migration plus a data migration of those six values. The plan's kinds list had no «letter» and invented `site_photo`/`other_doc` although `photo` and `other` exist; the six built are contract, drawing, letter, permit, photo, other. A **title** has no column either: the typed title becomes the stored file name (the extension stays the detected type's), and a file chosen without one keeps its own name; several files chosen at once keep their own names.
- **Why no existing table serves C2b:** nothing records a day on a site. `attendance.AttendanceDay` is one employee's day and pays nobody here; a certificate's quantities are a cumulative bill, not a diary; `Project.notes` is one short line. A report is a dated fact about one project with the site crew counted by trade and rows of equipment. The head count is a sum of rows kept as rows (not a stored number), so it cannot drift from them.
- **Tables** (`projects/models.py`): `SiteReport`: `project` (PROTECT), `report_date`, `weather` (one of clear, hot, cloudy, rain, wind, dust, cold, or empty: chosen by hand, no weather service), `work_done`, `delayed`, `materials`, `visitors` (text), `created_by`, `created_at`, `updated_by` (NULL until edited), `updated_at`; **unique (project, report_date)**. `SiteReportRow`: `report` (CASCADE), `kind` (crew or equipment), `label`, `count`, `position`. Photos are attachments owned by the report.
- **Rules:** `projects/site_reports.py` (service) and `projects/access.py` (who). One report per project per day: **a second save of a day edits that day's report** (rows replaced as typed, `updated_by` set, the author kept); a future day is refused (`timezone.localdate()`); something must be said (what was done, or what was delayed: a rained-off day is a report); at most 15 rows a section, a count 1 to 999 (an equipment row without a count is 1), names up to 80 characters, texts up to 3000; the project must be open (planned, active or on hold); saved under a project row lock. The **delay mark** is «delayed is not empty». The **head count** is the sum of the crew rows. The form edits the report of the day it was opened for: posting another date to an edit URL is ignored.
- **Who sees and who edits (the project's own rule, no new permission):** **sees** a project's documents and reports: `projects.view_projects` (the site engineer's) or `sales.view_sales_invoices` (the office's), exactly the project screens' rule; **adds a document, writes or edits a report, adds and deletes its photos:** `projects.record_progress` or `sales.create_sales_invoice` (the certificate screens' own `can_record`), and a write right without a view right is not enough; **deletes a document:** `sales.create_sales_invoice` only (the contract is the record: the site engineer adds, the office removes). A stock keeper holds neither view right: 403 on every tab, upload, file and delete, and the generic `/attachments/` URLs answer the same. A reader sees and opens but gets no add controls and the reason in words. Every screen looks a report or a file up **through its project**, so another project's report or file id answers 404 (checked on detail, edit, print, photos, file and delete). The two owner types are closed when the module is off.
- **Entities (the plan was wrong here):** a project has no entity column and `project_list` / `project_detail` are not narrowed to an entity; only money writes call `contract.in_reach`. Attachments, however, are scoped on `Attachment.entity`, so a project file stamped when uploaded would vanish from another entity while the project page stays open (and the plan's own trap: a drawing uploaded before the first certificate vanishing after it). So the two project owner types carry `group_wide`: the entity scope on the attachment row is skipped for them (one `_scoped` helper in the three listing and reading functions) and their files follow the project page, exactly as the page is not narrowed. They are stamped with no entity. Every other owner type keeps the entity scope untouched (a test pins that only these two are flagged). The plan's «another entity's project → 404» therefore does not hold, by design: the refusals are the project right, the project and the owner type.
- **Where the files are stored, and the limits (trap 13: drawings can fill the database):** in the database (HG-072). The plan's 100 files and 100 MB per project **equals the whole global share** at the defaults (`DATABASE_SIZE_LIMIT_MB` 500 × `ATTACHMENTS_GLOBAL_SHARE` 0.2 = 100 MB), so one project could take every attachment's room. Built instead: **a project holds up to 40 files and 40 MB** and **a daily report up to 12 photos and 12 MB**; the 5 MB per file and the 20% share of the database still apply on top, unchanged. Two optional rule keys (`max_count`, `max_owner_bytes`) let an owner type set its own limits, read by one function (`owner_limits`) with the settings as the fallback, so no other owner changes. The documents tab shows what is used and what is left (`room()`: the lower of the project's room and the database's share), warns below 8 MB left and says plainly when there is no room. The page tells the user to split or shrink a large drawing before uploading it as a PDF; the browser already shrinks a large phone photo before it is sent (the attachments script).
- **Screens:** the project's tabs gain «اليوميات» and «المستندات» (a sideways-scrolling row on a phone, links at least 44 px); «المستندات»: files grouped by kind with who added each and when (in words), the camera and the files chooser, a title, delete for those allowed; «اليوميات»: the list newest first (the day in words, the weather, the head count, a mark where something was delayed, the photo count, print), the form (new and edit, one screen), one report with its photo card, and the A4 print (Arabic or English, the photos as thumbnails, no internal number on the paper). **«تقرير النهارده»** is the primary action on the project's overview and on the reports tab for those who may write and only on an open project: under the tabs on a wide screen, a bar above the floating tab bar on a phone. The save row of the form sticks above the tab bar. Screen URLs are named `diary*` because the shell prints a «اطبع / Excel» toolbar on any URL name containing «report» and the project already has `projects:report` (the portfolio).
- **Audit rows:** `module="projects"`, `save_site_report` (create or update; the day, the weather, the head count, the row counts and four flags; **never the text**); the attachments service writes `add_attachment` and `delete_attachment` for every file.
- **Files:** `projects/models.py`, `projects/migrations/0003_site_reports.py`, `projects/access.py`, `projects/documents.py`, `projects/site_reports.py`, `projects/views_documents.py`, `projects/views_reports.py`, `projects/demo.py`, `projects/urls.py`, `projects/views.py` (the overview's primary action), `projects/contract_views.py` (two tab words), `attachments/services.py` (registry, limits, scope), `attachments/views.py` (the optional title), templates `projects/_tabs.html`, `_site_cta.html`, `_file_row.html`, `documents.html`, `reports.html`, `report_form.html`, `report_day.html`, `report_print.html`, `detail.html`, static `hesba/css/projects.css` (`?v=c2-1`), `hesba/js/site_report.js`, `hesba/js/attachments.js` (one title line), `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py` (one call), tests below.
- **Tests:** `projects/tests_documents.py` (33), `projects/tests_site_reports.py` (45) and `projects/tests_c2_ui.py` (17), 95 in all. **Documents:** the six kinds map to six distinct existing stages; who sees, adds and deletes for owner, engineer, reader and stock keeper; add with a title and without, every kind grouped newest first with who and when, camera image as photo and PDF as document, the right project only, a finished project still takes as-built files; opening (inline image, PDF download, safe headers, ETag), delete audited and gone, the engineer refused with the reason in the page language, delete POST-only; 403 on tab, upload, file and delete (and the generic endpoints) without the project right, a reader sees but cannot add, either view right alone is enough and a write right alone is not, another project's file id and a stray non-project file refused (404); a document added in one entity shows in the other and for the group, the plan's trap (a drawing before the first certificate still shows after it), only the two project owner types are group-wide; too large (5 MB, in both languages), wrong types by real bytes, a script in a PDF, empty, a signature or a clinic stage refused, the count cap and the size cap and the global share each refusing with a clear message, the room shown and its warnings; counts of money, stock, ledger, certificates and BOQ unchanged by adding, opening and deleting, and a source scan. **Reports:** one a day (a second save edits: same row, rows replaced, author kept, editor recorded;
