the database constraint; another project the same day fine), a future day refused and today and yesterday allowed, bad dates, the head count 6 + 4 + 10 = 20 and equipment not counted in it, blank rows dropped, every bad row refused with its number and section (and in English), empty reports refused and a delay-only one allowed, the weather list, text limits, a closed or cancelled project refused, who may write (engineer, office; stock keeper, reader and a write-right-only user refused), audit rows with figures and never text, newest first with the delay flag, lookup through the project; the screens: write, edit, the form opening the day's report for editing, no future day, what was typed kept after a refusal, an edit cannot move a day, every POST 403 for a stock keeper and a reader with nothing changed, reading rights, another project's report id 404 on every screen, a closed project read-only, the list's day in words, head count and delay mark (exactly one), the primary action only for those who may write, the two tabs; photos attach to the report (not the project), photos only (no PDF, no clinic stage, no document kind), the 12-photo cap, delete by those who write and not by a reader, the project's documents untouched; the print carries every field in Arabic and English with dir, thumbnails through the project URL, no internal id and empty sections left out, 403 without the project right; nothing financial or quantitative moves across write, edit, photograph, delete, list and print, the certificate still bills what was typed on it, and a source scan. **Screens and scanners:** every screen in Arabic RTL and English LTR, no Arabic sentence opening with an English word, dates in words and not ISO, the shared row roles, no pre-written `data-label` in any new template, the stylesheet uses tokens only and every token is defined, versioned assets, the phone hooks (camera, files, sticky save, the bar above the tab bar); `0003` on a database holding a project and a BOQ line, and `makemigrations --check` clean with attachments still at `0002`; the demo (below).
- **Demo:** `prepare_demo._projects` calls `projects.demo.seed`: each of the two demo projects gets three documents (a contract and a drawing as one-page PDFs and a drawn site picture) and four days of reports (yesterday and the three before it, none today so «تقرير النهارده» still has a day to write), one with a delay and two with photos. The files are generated from nothing (no photograph, no real plan, a few KB each). It is idempotent, moves no money, and `reports/tests_demo_catalogs.py` is unchanged (the project count is still two).
- **Risks:** (1) the stored stage words are the table's, not readable (see C2a). (2) Anyone who may write on a project may edit any day's report of any author, with no lock window: the audit row records who and what changed (figures only). (3) A report's text is unrestricted free text on screen and paper. (4) Documents can still use up the database's attachment share together with every other owner's files; the page shows the room and refuses at the limit. (5) Migration numbering with another open branch that adds a `projects` migration.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Limits: 40 files and 40 MB for a project's documents, 12 photos and 12 MB for a day's report? (Built so: under the 100 MB global share; raise the share first if drawings need more.)
  2. Should the site engineer be able to delete a document? (No: the office only, because a contract and a drawing are the record; the engineer adds.)
  3. Should a report lock after a day or two, with the office able to unlock? (No lock built; the plan's lock window, unlock and its audit are a follow-up if wanted.)
  4. Temperature, a subcontractor on a crew row and a «fill from the labour sheet» button (the plan's extras)? (Not built: not asked; labour sheets are C5.)
  5. Missing-days marks on the list and a dashboard tile «مشاريع من غير تقرير النهارده»? (Not built: not asked.)
  6. A report on a done or cancelled project? (Refused, read-only; documents are still accepted on a finished project as as-built files.)
  7. Readable stored words for the document kinds (a choices-only attachments migration)? (Not built: the six existing stages carry them.)
  8. Should the quantities done be offered on the certificate screen from a report's text? (No, never automatically: a certificate takes only what is typed on it.)

## HG-096 — M10 online consultation: a call link on an online booking (`appointments` migration `0004`: one choice and one column)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/m10-tele-consult` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2 (a protected migration, a link that gives access to a medical call, a public page that now lives after the start). Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «3. طبي», item 22 «كشف عن بعد (فيديو)», PR M10.
- **Money:** nothing moves. The deposit and the invoice are only **read** (`deposits.services.for_target`, `payments.services.outstanding`, `PaymentLink` rows); no invoice, payment, deposit, cashbox movement, journal entry or pay link is created, changed or cancelled by anything here (counted in the tests across every action and page, with a deposit taken by the existing service only to set the scene). `deposits`, `payments`, `sales`, posting, stock, ledgers, report calculations, closing and the permission core are untouched. No new permission and no seeded role.
- **Protected items touched:**
  1. `appointments/migrations/0004_online_consultation.py`: an `AddField` (`Appointment.call_url`, `URLField(max_length=600, blank=True, default="")`) and an `AlterField` on `kind` (one more choice, `online`, inside the existing `max_length=20`). It follows `0003_online_booking`; no data is read or changed (tested on a database that already holds bookings: every old booking keeps all its values and gets an empty link). `makemigrations --check` is clean.
  2. **Settings storage:** no new table or column. The three clinic settings are installation-wide `SystemSetting` rows, the same way `deposits.cancel_hours` is kept: `appointments.online.base_url`, `appointments.online.prepaid`, `appointments.online.public_booking`. They are **not per entity** (a group of two clinics shares them); a per-entity setting would be a column on `BookingSettings`.
- **Where the video runs, and what Hesba does and does not do:**
  - The call runs on a meeting service **outside Hesba**. Hesba has no account, key or SDK for any of them, makes **no network call** from the server, and does **not embed** the call: the button opens the link in a new browser tab (`target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" referrerpolicy="same-origin"`; `rel="noreferrer"` is what keeps the address from the meeting service, and `same-origin` is used instead of `no-referrer` because `config/tests_referrer_policy.py` forbids that word in any template (it breaks a page's own POST under CSRF); toward another site `same-origin` sends nothing).
  - Two ways to get a link, both on the booking's page (`call_generate`, `call_paste`; `call_remove` clears it). **«اعمل لينك مكالمة»** makes `<base>/<room>`: `<room>` is a recognisable ASCII prefix (the clinic's own name, letters and digits of it, else `clinic`) followed by 26 random lower-case letters and digits from `secrets` (36^26, about 2^134; lower case only because Jitsi reads room names case-insensitively, so mixed case would look stronger than it is). Nothing about the patient, the phone, the booking number or its id can flow into it (`make_link` takes no booking). `<base>` is the clinic setting, `https://meet.jit.si` until the clinic points it at its own Jitsi server (checked like a pasted link, no query or fragment). **«حط لينك»** accepts a link made elsewhere (Google Meet, Zoom, Teams, an own server): `https://` only, a host, no user name or password in it, no space or quote, no non-ASCII character, at most 600 characters; `http:`, `javascript:` and anything else is refused with a plain message in Arabic and English.
  - **The public Jitsi server asks the first person who opens a room, the doctor, to sign in with a Google, Facebook or GitHub account.** The settings screen says so in plain words, and that the clinic may prefer its own Jitsi server or to paste its own link (Meet, Zoom, Teams). The default is the public server because it needs no account of the clinic's.
  - Hesba does **not** record the call, does **not** control a waiting room or a lobby (whoever holds the link enters when the meeting service lets them), and does **not** know whether anyone joined: the visit is recorded by the doctor, as every visit.
  - A link can be set, replaced and removed while the booking is not finished (booked, confirmed, in progress); a finished, cancelled or no-show booking, and a booking that is not online, refuse. Every change is audited (`set_call_link` with «set» or «replaced» and the source, `remove_call_link`; the actor and the time are the audit row's): **the audit never stores the link**. Changing the kind away from online drops the link (audited).
- **Who sees the link:**
  - **Staff:** the booking's doctor (an employee holding a clinical permission, the HG-084 rule, not a bare employee link) and those who hold `sales.create_sales_invoice` (the permission every appointment screen books and changes with) see «ادخل المكالمة» on the agenda row, the booking's page, the doctor's queue (the ticket, the reception's list of today's bookings, and a «كشوفاتك الأونلاين النهارده» list for a doctor with or without a check-in) and the visit page, **while the booking is open**. A user without that right gets no link in any page (tested on the agenda, the booking page, the week page and its JSON, the queue and the visit page) and **403** on the three link POSTs; a booking of another entity answers 404 on a crafted POST. Setting, replacing and removing need the same booking permission as every change on that page (the doctor role does not have it; the doctor sees and uses the link, reception makes it).
  - **The seeded doctor role holds only `medical.treat_patients`, so a doctor has no agenda or booking page** (`sales.view_sales_invoices`): the call opens for them from the queue and from the visit page. No permission was granted for this.
  - **The patient:** only on M3's confirm page (same signed token, bound to the patient, `no-store`, `noindex`, `Referrer-Policy: same-origin` as before), only for an **online** booking that **has a link**, is **confirmed or arrived**, not cancelled, no-show or done, and only from **15 minutes before its start until 3 hours after it** (`JOIN_OPENS_BEFORE`, `JOIN_CLOSES_AFTER` in `appointments/online.py`, with their reason). Before the window the page says when the button will appear, in words and the clinic's time; unconfirmed, no link yet and unpaid each have their own sentence; after the window the page is the same dead page as any dead link. A wrong, forged or expired token gives the one dead page it always did. The link is in the page's HTML only for an open window (the box never holds it otherwise), never in a redirect (the join is a direct link to the meeting service; confirm and cancel-request still answer with a redirect to the page and nothing else), a header, an audit row or a log line.
  - **M3's rule was departed from, narrowly:** the confirm page used to be dead from the minute the booking started and for «arrived», so a call window after the start was impossible. `appointments.online.join_page_alive` keeps the page alive for an **online, confirmed or arrived** booking of the same patient until the window closes. `services.link_alive`, which guards the confirm and cancel-request **posts**, is **not** loosened (tested: both still refuse after the start); after the start the page shows neither button.
  - **Messages:** the reminder of an online booking (a clinic's, M3) says it is an online visit and that the call opens from the same confirm link («الكشف ده أونلاين: هتدخل المكالمة بزر من نفس الرابط قبل الميعاد بقليل» and its English form); without a public address (no confirm link) it says to ask the clinic and promises nothing. **The call link is never written into a message, the outbox, or the text WhatsApp receives** (tested by scanning every outbox column). A shop's reminder and an in-clinic reminder are byte for byte what they were. The outbox masking of the confirm link (M3) applies as before.
- **Paying before the call (off by default; «الكشف الأونلاين مدفوع مقدّماً»):** when on, the patient's button shows only if the booking is **covered**: a deposit on it (S5, held or applied; a refunded or forfeited one does not count) of at least its price, **or** its invoice posted with nothing left to pay (`payments.services.outstanding(invoice) <= 0`); a free visit is covered. Otherwise the page says the visit must be paid first and, **if an S4 pay link already exists** for the booking's invoice (new, unexpired, still payable), shows it; the public page never makes a link (it reads the rows directly: `payments.live_link` would expire and audit on a GET). The staff's button is not gated by payment (the booking's page shows a note while it is unpaid). **What the code makes impossible:** `appointments.services.bill` (and the insured billing) finishes the booking when it makes the invoice, and a finished booking has no call; an S4 pay link needs a posted invoice. So the invoice branch of «covered» and an existing pay link never coexist with an open booking: **in practice, paying before the call is an S5 deposit at the counter**. The rules are written and tested at the rule level (price 300.00: no deposit, 100.00, 299.99 and 300.00; a posted invoice paid 300.00 and one with 300.00 due; an existing pay link, a stale one) but the flow the plan imagined (post the consult invoice, send the pay link, then call) cannot happen without changing billing, which this task may not. Several deposits are not added together (the service holds one at a time); a customer payment applied later by the ledger's FIFO does not show on the invoice and does not count.
- **Online booking by the patient (off by default; «اسمح بحجز كشف أونلاين»):** the public booking page (M4) asks «في العيادة ولا أونلاين» on the details step, for a medical business and when the setting is on; a posted mode is ignored otherwise. **It offers every visit:** marking visits one by one would need a new column (`BookableService`), which this task may not add. An online booking by the patient has no link until the staff make one, and the done page says so (it never shows a link).
- **Other places:** the kind is offered on the agenda and the booking form in a medical installation only (an installation that is not medical never offers it; a booking already online keeps the choice). The settings screen (`/appointments/online/`, linked from the agenda; medical only, needs `sales.create_sales_invoice` and `settings.view_settings` as the booking settings do) works whether or not the «الحجز أونلاين» capability is on. The clinic demo gets two online bookings today (`settings_core/demo_online.py`, called from `prepare_demo`): one confirmed, a few minutes ahead, with a generated link (inside the patient's window), one later with no link yet.
- **Files:** `appointments/models.py`, `appointments/migrations/0004_online_consultation.py`, `appointments/online.py` (new: every rule), `appointments/views_online.py` (new), `appointments/views.py`, `appointments/public.py`, `appointments/booking.py`, `appointments/views_booking.py`, `appointments/services.py` (one hook in `reschedule`), `appointments/urls.py`, `medical/views.py` (the queue and the visit page), `messaging/services.py` (the online sentence), `settings_core/display_labels.py`, `settings_core/vocabulary.py`, `settings_core/demo_online.py` (new), `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py`, templates `appointments/online_settings.html` (new), `agenda.html`, `detail.html`, `confirm_public.html`, `book_public.html`, `book_done.html`, `medical/queue.html`, `medical/visit_form.html`, `static/hesba/css/booking.css` (`?v=bk-002`), tests `appointments/tests_online.py`.
- **Tests:** `appointments/tests_online.py` (56): the migration on a database with bookings and `makemigrations --check`; a link is unguessable, different each time, built from the setting's base, never holding the patient, phone, number or booking; pasted `http://`, `javascript:`, credentials, over-long, spaces and quotes refused with the exact Arabic and English messages; set, replace and remove audited without the link; a finished, cancelled, no-show or not-online booking refused; staff visibility (doctor, manager, a user without the right: no URL in the agenda, booking page, week page and JSON, queue and visit page; 403 on the three POSTs; another entity's booking 404); the patient's page at 16 minutes before, 15 minutes before, the start, start + 2 h 59 m and + 3 h, and 3 h 1 m after (dead), absent when unconfirmed, cancelled, done, no-show, not online or with no link, its headers, no confirm or cancel after the start, both posts still refusing, the dead page identical for a wrong token; prepaid (no deposit, 100.00, 299.99, 300.00, refunded, free, a posted invoice paid and unpaid, an existing and an expired pay link, the page changing nothing); the reminder (online sentence, no call link in any outbox column, English, no public address, a shop untouched); the public choice (off, on, a posted mode ignored, a shop); the screens in both languages and the scanners; the settings screen (saves, checks, audits, permissions, medical only); money counts identical across every action; the doctor's and the patient's buttons carry the same address; the demo.
- **Risks:**
  1. **A link the patient forwards opens for whoever has it during the window**, and a link pasted or made for a long-lived room works until someone replaces it. The window limits the patient's page, not the meeting service's room: anyone holding the room link (a screenshot, a forwarded message from the doctor's own device) can enter. Replace the link to close a room.
  2. **The meeting service's own terms and sign-in apply** (the public Jitsi server's sign-in for the first person in a room, its availability, its privacy policy); Hesba does not control them. A clinic that treats patients' health data should point the base at its own server or paste links from a service it has a contract with. The room name carries the clinic's name in clear (to the meeting server); nothing about the patient does.
  3. **No recording, no waiting room controlled by Hesba:** the doctor cannot admit a patient from Hesba; whoever has the link joins as the service allows. The doctor still records the visit by hand.
  4. The settings are installation-wide, not per entity.
  5. The confirm page is now open to a patient after the start for an online booking (to 3 hours); its content is still the clinic, the day, the time, the service, the kind and the call box, and nothing else.
  6. Migration numbering with another open branch (another `appointments` migration renumbers).
  7. The demo's two bookings are seeded relative to the moment the demo is built; a demo opened hours later shows the first one past its window (the page still says so correctly).
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Public Jitsi or the clinic's own server as the default? (The public server, because it needs nothing; the settings screen says plainly that it asks the doctor to sign in and that the clinic may point at its own server or paste its own link.)
  2. May the doctor make or replace the link, or only reception? (Reception and managers only, with the booking permission; the doctor uses it. The doctor role has no booking permission and no agenda.)
  3. The window: 15 minutes before to 3 hours after the start? (Yes, two constants.)
  4. Should «paid first» also accept a posted unpaid invoice with a pay link, or a payment link made for the call? (No: it reads what exists; in practice a deposit. Making a link for an online visit would need a change to billing or a new flow.)
  5. Should the patient be able to choose online for some visits only? (No: all visits when the setting is on; per-visit marking needs a column on `BookableService`.)
  6. Should an online booking with no link yet warn reception on the agenda? (Not built: the booking page says it has no link, and the patient's page says it is not ready.)
  7. Should the staff's own button also wait for the window or the payment? (No: staff may open it whenever the booking is open, so the doctor can start early.)

## HG-097 — Restaurants R4: kitchen stations, one kitchen ticket per station, and the kitchen screen (new table `restaurant.KitchenStation` and four columns)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/r4-kitchen-screen` under Ahmed's delegation, awaiting his review. T2: one new table and four columns in one migration (`restaurant/0004_kitchen_stations`; the plan's `0008` was written before R12 and R1a, which have not merged, so it takes the next free number and renumbers on rebase if one of them lands first). No new permission, no change to `pay()`, posting, stock movement, cashbox, ledger, average cost, closing, `sales/pos.py`, the permission core or any report calculation. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «5. مطاعم وكافيهات», item 18 «شاشة مطبخ (KDS) ومحطات». The roadmap's R-ids are taken by other work, hence "Restaurants R4".
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words: nothing.** Sending to the kitchen, marking a dish ready, finishing a ticket, recalling it and seeing a void all write the station's own marks and nothing else: no sale, no stock movement, no cashbox movement, no customer-ledger row, no journal entry, no plate made. A test counts every one of those before and after sending, marking ready, finishing, recalling and voiding, and the five R13 characterization tests pass unchanged (`restaurant/tests_pay_characterization.py`).
- **Why:**
  - The kitchen got one printed ticket for the whole order, for every station alike: a bar and a grill printed the same paper, and nobody could say a dish was ready.
  - Checklist item 18 asks for a kitchen screen and stations (kitchen, bar, sweets). Nothing routed an item to a place.
- **The schema (the Hard Gate):**
  - `KitchenStation`: `name_ar`, `name_en`, `sort`, `active`, `is_default`, `entity` (a record made without one belongs to the main entity, as a cashbox does), and `categories`, a ManyToMany to `master_data.Category`.
  - `KitchenTicket.station` (FK, null, PROTECT) and `KitchenTicket.done_at`. A ticket made with no station set up, and every ticket made before this, has no station.
  - `OrderLine.ready_at` and `OrderLine.void_reason` (255). `void_reason` moves R13's reason from the audit row to the line so the order page and the kitchen's card can show it; the audit row is unchanged.
  - Existing rows get null/blank values; nothing is backfilled and nothing has to be.
  - Why no existing table serves: nothing routes an item to a place, and a ticket had nowhere to say who it was for or when it finished.
- **What was built:**
  - **Routing** (`restaurant/stations.py`, `services.send_to_kitchen`): one ticket per station, each with its own sequence (still unique per order, numbered from the highest so far). An item goes to the station holding its category, or the nearest parent category a station holds; whatever no station holds goes to the default station; with no station set up at all, or no default and nothing matching, the lines stay together on one station-less ticket, exactly as today. `send_to_kitchen` still returns the first ticket (its `batch` holds all of them), so every caller works unchanged.
  - **Print:** the send redirects to the first ticket with `upto=` the last sequence, and the print page renders each ticket as its own page (`break-after: page`), with the station's name on it. Reprinting one ticket from the order page prints that one.
  - **The kitchen screen** `restaurant:kds` (`?station=`) and its JSON feed `restaurant:kds_feed`, polled every 5 seconds (paused while the tab is hidden; an expired session or a lost connection is said on the screen). A card per ticket: the table or order type, its age from the server's clock (amber after 8 minutes, red after 15; the colours are the existing `--hs-*` tokens), tap a dish for ready (tap again for not ready), tap the card for done (every dish not ready is stamped with the one time), «رجّع» brings back the last ticket finished within 2 hours exactly as it was. Taps go to `restaurant:kds_act` (POST, CSRF), which answers with the fresh feed. All text is drawn as text, never markup.
  - **A void after firing** shows on the card struck through with its reason and flashes until the kitchen taps it («تمام، شفته»). A finished card with a void it has not seen comes back on the screen. A cancelled order's card says so until the kitchen finishes it.
  - **On the floor:** the order page shows «جاهز» on each line the kitchen marked ready and the reason on a voided line; the tables board marks a table (and an open takeaway or delivery) with the number of ready dishes.
  - **Station setup** `restaurant:stations`: a list and a form on one page, like the tables' setup (seen with `sales.view_sales_invoices`, changed with `master_data.manage_items`). A category belongs to one station (refused otherwise, naming both); one default per entity (the last one chosen); a station with unfinished tickets cannot be switched off. Create and change are audited (`create_station`, `change_station`).
  - **Demo:** the restaurant, café and fast-food demos get «المطبخ» (default) and «المشروبات» (the drinks). Their drinks now sit in their own menu category (`DEMO-CAT-BAR`, from the catalog's new `bar` field, so a top-up that re-seeds the items keeps them there; the café's own category is now «المأكولات»), and two tables are in service with cards on both screens: one fresh, one amber, one red with a dish voided after firing and a drink ready.
- **Default decisions (change any of them and say so):**
  1. **Who sees the screen:** `restaurant.take_orders` (owner, manager, cashier, waiter), no new permission or role. A user who may only view invoices gets a 403 on the screen, the feed and the taps; the plan's open question 7.
  2. **Entity:** orders and tables carry no entity until R12, so stations do. Sending routes only to the working entity's stations (the main entity's when working across the group), the screen, the feed and the taps are scoped by the ticket's station, another entity's station is a 404, and **a ticket with no station belongs to the main entity** (HG-034's «no entity means main»). A branch that wants the screen sets up its own station, even just a default one.
  3. **«Seen» has no column of its own.** The four columns the task named do not store that the kitchen saw a void, so on a voided line `ready_at` means «seen»: voiding clears it (so a dish voided after it was ready still shows as new), and the kitchen's tap sets it. The fact that it had been ready is not kept (the audit row stays exactly as R13 pinned it). If a separate `void_seen_at` is wanted it is one more nullable column.
  4. **Done and recall:** done stamps every live line not ready and every unseen void with its own time; recall undoes exactly that (a dish the kitchen tapped itself earlier stays ready); a finished ticket's dishes cannot be un-tapped one by one.
  5. **What the screen shows:** tickets sent in the last 24 hours that are not finished (plus finished ones with an unseen void), whether or not the order is still open (a takeaway paid at the counter is still cooked). The age colours are fixed constants (8 and 15 minutes), not a setting.
  6. **The board's «جاهز» mark** counts the dishes marked ready on a live order. There is no «served» step yet, so it stays until the table is paid.
  7. **Station setup:** the same permissions as the tables' setup (seen with the invoices' view right, changed with `master_data.manage_items`).
- **Not built, on purpose:**
  - **«اتعمل فعلاً؟» on a void after firing** (T3: it makes plates through `kitchen._produce` and writes them off through `adjust_stock`, so it moves stock and cost). The void reason is stored and shown only; a dish cooked and then voided still never leaves stock, as HG-094 said. This is the follow-up (R4b), and it needs its own Hard Gate.
  - A separate kitchen permission or role, direct network printing (ESC/POS), a «served» step, per-station age thresholds, sound on a new ticket.
- **Files:** `settings_core/tests_watch2.py` (the restaurant demo test now expects two tables in service and the bar's second ticket), `restaurant/models.py`, `restaurant/migrations/0004_kitchen_stations.py`, `restaurant/stations.py` (new: routing, setup rules, the screen's queries and taps), `restaurant/services.py` (`send_to_kitchen`, `void_line` keeps the reason and clears the mark), `restaurant/views_kds.py` (new), `restaurant/views.py` (the send's message and print redirect, the print page, ready counts on the board), `restaurant/urls.py`, `restaurant/demo.py` (new), `templates/restaurant/kds.html`, `stations.html` (new), `ticket.html`, `order.html`, `board.html`, `static/hesba/css/kds.css` (new), `restaurant.css`, `static/hesba/js/kds.js` (new), `reports/demo_catalogs.py` and `reports/management/commands/seed_demo_business.py` (the catalog's `bar` field and the drinks' category), `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py` (`_floor`: stations and the two tables in service), `restaurant/tests_kds.py` (new).
- **Tests:** `restaurant/tests_kds.py` (24): a burger and a juice make two tickets and each station's feed carries only its own line; with no station there is one default ticket as before and the screen still shows it; what no station holds goes to the default station; a parent category routes its children; the setup rules; ready, done and recall stamp and clear the right times; the order page shows «جاهز» and the board marks the table; a void after firing (after the dish was ready, and after the card was finished) shows with its reason until seen and writes nothing else; a cancelled order's card; a paid takeaway still shows and a day-old ticket drops off; the screen, feed and taps give a 403 without `restaurant.take_orders` and the taps are POST only; setup is seen with the invoices' view right and changed with `manage_items`; routing, feed, taps and setup never cross entities (another entity's station, ticket and line are 404); the new styles use defined tokens and no colour literals, and the script writes no markup. Every test that sends or marks counts sales, payments, cashbox movements, stock movements, journal entries, customer-ledger rows and production runs before and after.
- **Risks:**
  1. **Branches before R12.** A ticket's entity is its station's. A branch with no station of its own cannot see its tickets on the screen (they belong to the main entity), and a branch's order sent while working across the group goes to the main entity's stations. R12 should give orders an entity and route by it.
  2. **No «served» step**, so a table keeps its «جاهز» mark until it is paid, and the kitchen cannot tell a plate was taken away.
  3. **A cooked-then-voided dish is still not written off** (above), so stock and cost of sales still miss it; the screen now at least tells the kitchen to stop.
  4. **One tablet per station in a browser.** A station's printer is that device's default printer; a tablet that goes to sleep stops polling and catches up on wake.
  5. **The demo catalogs changed:** the café's category is renamed «المأكولات» and the restaurant, café and fast-food drinks moved to a second category. Anything that asserted the old names would fail; the demo scanners pass.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Open the screen to the floor roles, or a new kitchen permission and role? (The floor's `restaurant.take_orders`; a role is a permission-core change.)
  2. A «served» step, so the table's «جاهز» mark clears when the waiter takes the plate? (Not built.)
  3. Should the kitchen's own «seen» for a void get its own column? (No: it reuses the line's ready stamp.)
  4. The age colours at 8 and 15 minutes, per station? (Fixed, the same for every station.)
  5. «اتعمل فعلاً؟» with the waste write-off: build it next as R4b? (Not built here; it is T3.)

## HG-098 — Manufacturing F1: a sales order makes a production order (make to order), and a sales order carries a delivery date (`manufacturing.ProductionOrder.sales_order_line`, `orders.SalesOrder.deliver_by`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/f1-make-to-order` under Ahmed's delegation of 7 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2: two new nullable columns, no posting, no new permission. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «7. تصنيعي», item 12 «أوامر بيع وتصنيع حسب الطلب» (أساسي), PR F1. Written as «Manufacturing F1» (a bare «F1» is also a finding id in this file). The numbers HG-095 to HG-097 belong to other open branches; this one took the next free number.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing moves and no number changes. «اعمل أمر إنتاج» opens an ordinary production order through the unchanged `orders.create_order`; it posts nothing. Tests count stock operations, stock movements, journal entries and lines, cashbox movements and operations, sales invoices, production runs and material issues before and after the service call and after the screen's POST (and after a ledger rebuild): all identical, and the trial balance stays balanced. The production order then walks its usual stages, issues its materials, absorbs labour and overhead and finishes exactly as before; the sale is the ordinary invoice of the sales order. Pinned end to end: SO for 30 cakes at 25.00 (VAT off), three batches of 95.00 = 285.00 of materials, 90.00 labour and 60.00 overhead planned, total 435.00, 14.5000 a cake; invoicing the 30 books sales 750.00, cost of goods sold 435.00 and gross profit 315.00, the ledger balanced and reconciled.
- **Protected items touched:** two columns, both nullable, with no data to migrate: `manufacturing/migrations/0004_make_to_order.py` (`ProductionOrder.sales_order_line`, a FK to `orders.SalesOrderLine`, `PROTECT`) and `orders/migrations/0003_deliver_by.py` (`SalesOrder.deliver_by`, a date). Two files because the columns sit in two apps; `makemigrations --check` is clean. Posting, stock movement, cashbox, ledgers, average cost, `produce`'s quantities and cost, sales invoice posting, the permission core and report calculations are untouched (`manufacturing/services.py`, `manufacturing/orders.py` and `ledger/` have no diff).
- **Why no existing table serves:** `ProductionOrder.customer` names a person, not what was ordered, and nothing ties a quantity of an order line to the units being made, so nothing could stop making the same order twice. `SalesOrder.valid_until` is the quotation's validity, not a delivery date.
- **What was built:**
  - `manufacturing/make_to_order.py`: `uncovered`, `make`, `sync_due`, `open_for`, `line_states`, `locations_for`, `recipe_for`.
  - **The action** is POST-only `manufacturing:make_for_sale` (`/manufacturing/orders/for-sale/<line>/`), reached from a small form on the sales order page: quantity (prefilled with what is not covered, editable down, never above), the place, planned labour and overhead. It needs `inventory.adjust_stock` (403 without) and `sales.view_sales_invoices` (403 without), the quotations capability on (404 otherwise) and a sales order inside the entity being worked in (404 otherwise). It locks the sales order, so a double tap cannot make it twice. It refuses an order that is not confirmed, an item with no active recipe, and a line with nothing left to make («الكمية دي بتتصنع بالفعل»). Cancelling the production order frees its quantity.
  - **The sales order page** (`orders/detail`) gets a «الإنتاج» column when the manufacturing module is on and the document is an order: each linked production order with its number, quantity and stage (links only for whoever may see production), what is not covered yet, «اتصنع 30 · في المخزن 30», a red note when the stock was sold before it reached the customer, and the form. A user with sales rights but no `adjust_stock` (the cashier) sees the column, never the form. A trade shop has no column.
  - **The delivery date** `SalesOrder.deliver_by`: set in the quotation's details form, and changeable on an open order with its own small form (`services.set_deliver_by`, audited, never before the quotation date). It shows on the sales order page, on the production order page and on the board card. A production order still planned or on the floor follows the sales order's date (`sync_due` rewrites its existing `due_date`), so the board's late flag, the home table and the activity panel keep reading `due_date` unchanged; a finished one keeps the date it had.
  - **The production screens:** the board card and the order page say «لطلب SO-… · العميل» with the delivery date, and link to the sales order for whoever may open it.
  - **Cancelling a sales order** with production orders still planned or on the floor shows a notice before, and a warning after naming them; it does not cancel them.
- **Default decisions for the owner (built, change on request):**
  1. Finished goods are **not reserved** for the customer (there is no reservation anywhere in the system). The line shows what was made and what is in stock, and says so when the till sold them first. The warning counts what was made and not yet invoiced against stock at the production order's place.
  2. A product with several active recipes uses the first active one by code, shown on the form.
  3. What a line still needs made = the quantity left to invoice, and never more than the order less what is already in production (`min(left, ordered − live production)`). The plan's own formula (ordered − invoiced − live production) counts a made unit twice once it is invoiced: 20 of 30 made and invoiced would show 0 left to make instead of 10. The invoiced units are taken to be the made ones first.
  4. The production place is the sales order's own entity (an order with none belongs to the main entity): its default location, or the one chosen among that entity's active locations; never the session's entity.
  5. The delivery date can be set on an open quotation and changed on a confirmed order, because it is usually agreed after the customer accepts.
  6. Only a **confirmed** order makes production orders (not a closed one).
  7. The delivery date is not printed on the sales order document yet.
- **Files:** `manufacturing/models.py`, `manufacturing/migrations/0004_make_to_order.py`, `manufacturing/make_to_order.py` (new), `manufacturing/order_views.py`, `manufacturing/urls.py`, `orders/models.py`, `orders/migrations/0003_deliver_by.py`, `orders/services.py` (`deliver_by` in the header, `set_deliver_by`), `orders/views.py`, `templates/orders/detail.html`, `templates/orders/_production_cell.html` (new), `templates/manufacturing/orders.html`, `templates/manufacturing/order.html`, `static/hesba/css/operations.css` (`mfg-sale-*`, tokens only), `templates/operations/base.html` (stylesheet version), tests `manufacturing/tests_make_to_order.py` (30) and one in `orders/tests.py`.
- **Tests:** `manufacturing/tests_make_to_order.py` (30): one production order of 30 cakes (3 batches) with the customer, the date and the link, uncovered 0; a second tap refused and cancelling frees 30; quantity down but never up (0, negative, text, 31, 21 after 10) and the 20 that remain; 10 + 20 covers 30; a quotation and an item with no recipe refused; the first active recipe by code; planned labour 90.00 and overhead 60.00 (negative refused); a place of another entity refused; finish and sell (285.00, 435.00, 14.5000, 750.00, 435.00, 315.00, ledger balanced and reconciled); 20 made and invoiced leaves 10 still to make; made 30 and in stock 30, then 10 after the till sells 20 to someone else, with the warning on the page; nothing posted (counts, stock and ledger) for the service and for the screen's POST; the date on the sales order, the production order and the board, in both languages; a past delivery date flags the order late; a new date follows a planned order and not a finished one, and clearing it clears it; the date rules (before the quotation date, not a date, cancelled order); the date saved from the quotation and kept by a save that does not send it; the date saved from the order page; the column and the button for the owner (prefilled quantity), none once covered, the stage words as it walks and finishes; the cashier sees the column and the production number, never the button, and gets 403 from the action; no recipe means no button; no column on a quotation; no column without the manufacturing module; the screen reports what it refused and refuses GET; cancelling the sales order warns and leaves the production order planned; a stock order shows no sale; another entity's sales order is a 404 on the page and on the action, the branch's own order is made at the branch's store, and its production order is a 404 from the main entity; a stock keeper without sales rights gets 403; quotations switched off closes the action. `orders/tests.py`: a trade shop has no column and keeps the date.
- **Risks:** (1) Not reserved: the till or another invoice can sell the goods first; the warning says so, nothing stops it. (2) The «invoiced units are the made ones first» assumption can offer a few units too many when part of a line was delivered from older stock and the rest made. (3) The two migrations renumber if another `orders` or `manufacturing` migration lands first. (4) The «الإنتاج» form is a `<details>` inside the line's cell, not a new screen; Ahmed has not approved the look (screenshots in the PR). (5) The home board's table and the print of the sales order do not show the sales order number or the delivery date. (6) The unchanged `create_order` still stores the planned labour and overhead once; they cannot be changed after the order is opened (F12).
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Should goods made for an order be held for that customer? (No; the line warns when the stock was sold first.)
  2. Which recipe when a product has several? (The first active one by code, shown.)
  3. May the delivery date be changed after the customer accepted? (Yes, on an open order; production orders still on the floor follow it.)
  4. Should closing or cancelling a sales order cancel its open production orders? (No; it warns and leaves them.)
  5. Print the delivery date on the sales order? (Not yet.)

## HG-099 — E6 completion certificate and its public verify page (`education.Certificate`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/e6-completion-certificate` under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2 (a new table, and a public page that names a student). Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «4. تعليمي», item 18 «شهادة إتمام (تدريب)», PR E6.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing moves. Issuing, printing, revoking and opening the verify page write no sales invoice, cashbox movement, ledger row, journal entry, stock movement or outbox row (pinned by counts before and after, on the service and on the screens). The module only **reads** attendance (`attendance.rate`), held class sessions, marks (`exams.student_marks`) and the dues list (`billing.dues`, to warn); a test scans it for posting, billing, messaging and write calls and finds none. No attendance or marks calculation was changed.
- **Protected items touched:** the migration only: `education/0009_certificates` (one new table and one partial unique constraint; no existing table, column or row is read or changed; it follows `0008_exams_and_marks`, so a branch that merges later renumbers; the plan said `0010` because it expected E3 first, which has not landed). Posting, stock, cashbox, ledgers, average cost, report calculations, closing, attendance and exam calculations and the permission core are untouched. **No new permission and no seeded role.**
- **Why no existing table serves:** printing a sheet without a record cannot be verified or revoked, and nothing in the app records that a certificate was issued, to whom, or that it was withdrawn. `Enrollment` is the right owner (it is one student in one group over a span) but cannot hold a number, a revoke or the frozen figures.
- **Table** (`education/models.py`): `Certificate`: `enrollment` (PROTECT), `number` (unique, `CRT-<year>-<5 digits>`), `issued_on`, `hours` (decimal 6,2), `attendance_rate` (whole percent, may be empty), `grade_text` (40 characters, may be empty), `issued_by`, `revoked_at`, `revoked_by`, `revoke_reason`. **A partial unique constraint: one live (not revoked) certificate per enrolment**, so a double click or two people at once cannot make two. No entity column: a certificate belongs to its enrolment's group's entity, as an exam does (every read goes through `services.scoped(StudyGroup.objects)`). **No column was added that the plan did not list** (see the decisions below for what that costs).
- **Rules** (`education/certificates.py`):
  - **Where:** training and language centres only (`levels.is_levels_install()`); every other education install answers 404 on every screen and a service refusal.
  - **Who:** `master_data.manage_parties` (owner, manager, accountant) issues, revokes, lists and prints. A cashier or any user who may only read student files gets **403** on every screen and POST (the list shows grades, which a cashier may not see since HG-091); a teacher gets 403 too. Guard order is the exam screens': not a training or language install (404), then the record in the entity being worked in (404), then the permission (403).
  - **Finished:** the enrolment was stopped (a student who left, or who was moved up a level by `levels.move_up`, which stops the old enrolment), **or** the group's term has ended (its last day included). An enrolment still running in a group that has not ended is refused with «الطالب لسه مكمّل في المجموعة ومدتها ما خلصتش…» (exact text pinned in both languages). An enrolment with **no held class** in its span is refused with «مفيش حصة اتعملت للطالب في المجموعة دي، فمفيش حاجة تتشهد بيها.».
  - **Span:** from the later of the enrolment's start and the group's start, to the day the enrolment stopped, else the group's last day, never past the group's last day or the day of issue.
  - **Hours:** held (not cancelled) class sessions of the group in the span × the group's class length ÷ 60, half-up to 0.01. **10 classes × 90 minutes = 15.00 hours**; 5 classes of a student who joined late = 7.50; 3 classes × 50 minutes = 2.50.
  - **Attendance rate:** `attendance.rate` over that student's marks in that group and span (late counts as attended, whole percent): 9 attended of 10 held = **90**; 7 present + 2 late of 10 = 90. A cancelled class is neither held nor counted.
  - **Frozen at issue:** hours, rate and grade are stored, so correcting attendance later never changes a certificate already handed out.
  - **Warnings, never a block** (owner question 7): attendance under 75% (70% warns; **exactly 75% does not**), or the payer has a balance on the dues list (`billing.dues`). The first issue is stopped with the warning and the text «علّم "أصدرها برضو"»; the second, with the box ticked (service: `acknowledged=True`), issues. Nothing is written by the refused first step. The audit row records `warned`.
  - **Grade:** a suggestion, never a condition. The issue screen offers the percentage of the student's score on the group's latest **published** final exam (`exams.student_marks`; 17 of 20 gives «85.0%», the newest of two finals wins, 7.5 of 10 is 75.0%), and the manager can change or empty it. An **unpublished** exam is never offered: HG-091 keeps an unpublished mark the centre's own, and a certificate's grade is shown on a public page. A quiz, an absence from the final, or a final with no score offers nothing. At most 40 characters.
  - **Issue once:** an enrolment with a live certificate returns that one (screen: «للتسجيل ده شهادة سارية بالفعل، دي هي.»); two clicks make one row and one audit row. The number comes from `services._create_numbered` (the series `CRT-<year>-`, retried when taken: a test makes the first number taken and checks the retry moves on).
  - **Revoke:** a reason of at least 3 characters is required; the **row is kept**; revoking twice is refused; the action is audited with the reason. An enrolment whose certificate was revoked can be certified again with a **new number** (the old number still answers «revoked»).
  - **Audit:** issuing writes the printed name, course, group, hours, rate, grade, the span and whether it was warned; revoking writes the number and reason.
- **The sheet** (`/education/certificates/<id>/`, staff only): A4 landscape (`@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:0}`, one page in a real print to PDF, checked in Arabic and English, live and revoked), Arabic or English by `?lang=` (a button switches), the business's name and its logo **only when one is set** in company details (no Hesba logo is drawn or invented), the student's name, the course and the group's level, the period, the hours, the grade when there is one, the number, the issue day, a signature line and a QR to the verify page. A revoked certificate prints a «ملغية» / «REVOKED» mark across it. Drawn in container units, so the same layout shows as a miniature on a 390 px phone (the facts, status, verify link and revoke form sit above it as readable text) and fills a desktop and the page. Colours come from the `--hs-*` tokens only (navy, gold as accent, `--hs-gold-wash`); no colour literal and no `rgba()` (a test scans the stylesheet).
- **The verify page** (`/verify/c/<token>/`, public, `education/public.py`):
  - **Token:** `signing.dumps({"c": id, "n": number}, salt="hesba.education.certificate.v1")`: its own salt (a share token never opens it and it never opens a share page: pinned both ways), **no expiry** (paper does not expire), and `read_token` checks the row's number against the token's. It is not guessable in sequence: the printed running number alone opens nothing (`/verify/c/CRT-…/` is 404), and a token needs the `SECRET_KEY`. **No unguessable-code column was needed or added.**
  - **It shows exactly:** the number, the student's name, the course (with the level when the group has one), the period, the day of issue, the grade if the sheet prints one, the issuing business (the group's own entity when the installation has several, else the company's name, as `appointments/public.py`) and the status. **It never shows** a phone, the guardian, the date of birth, the school, notes, the stage, the group, the teacher, the hours, the attendance rate, the exam, or **the reason for a revoke** (asserted with marker strings in both languages; a revoked certificate says «ملغية» and the number still answers).
  - **Every failure is one plain page** (forged, altered, another salt, a number that is not the row's, an unknown row): the same 404 body, pinned equal across all of them, with only the installation's name and contact.
  - **Headers on every response, errors included:** `Cache-Control: no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: same-origin`. GET and HEAD only (everything else 405, with the same headers). **Throttled per address** in Django's cache (per process, as the lab page): 60 requests a minute, and 30 requests that found nothing an hour, then the plain page with status 429; a real certificate still opens for a guesser who has used up their misses. Opening the page writes no audit row and no row of any kind.
  - **The QR's address** is built from `settings.PUBLIC_BASE_URL` only (never the request's host, the standing rule of `printing.share`). Without it the sheet prints **no code** and the manager sees one line saying why.
- **Files:** `education/models.py`, `education/migrations/0009_certificates.py`, `education/certificates.py`, `education/public.py`, `education/views_certificates.py`, `education/urls.py`, `education/views.py` (the tab's flag and word: two lines), `config/urls.py` (one public route), templates `education/certificates.html`, `certificate_issue.html`, `certificate.html`, `certificate_verify.html`, `_tabs.html` (one tab), static `hesba/css/education_certificate.css` (`?v=e6-1`), tests `education/tests_certificates.py`. `student.html` and `group.html` are **not** touched (the queue of finished students is on the issue screen).
- **Tests:** `education/tests_certificates.py` (68 at this writing). **Rules:** 10 × 90 = 15.00 hours and 9 of 10 = 90%; late counts, a cancelled class does not; the half-up hours; the span from the later start; a stopped enrolment's own end; the term's last day; the exact refusals (still enrolled, no class held) in both languages; the 40-character grade; who may issue and revoke; an unknown enrolment; the 70% warning and the second step with nothing written by the first; exactly 75% not warning; a guardian who owes (a real posted fee invoice) warning; the grade (unpublished never offered, 17/20 = 85.0%, quiz / absent / unscored nothing, the newest final, typed text and an empty box); issue twice gives one certificate and one audit row; distinct numbers and a taken number skipped; the audit content; revoke (reason, once, row kept, reason in the audit); re-issue after a revoke with a new number; the database's one-live-per-enrolment constraint; the ready queue; another entity; no money, stock or message and the module's imports. **Token:** reads back, is not the number, forged / altered / another salt / wrong number / unknown row / odd payloads read as nothing, no expiry, the two salts do not cross, the address from `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` only. **Public page:** valid in both languages, no grade line without a grade, revoked without its reason, nothing beyond the sheet (guardian, phones, school, notes, stage, group, teacher, birth date in three forms, hours, rate), every dead link the same body, GET/HEAD only with private headers on the 405, both throttles and another address unaffected, the business per entity, nothing written. **Screens:** the tab for a manager only, 403 for cashier and teacher and a login redirect for anonymous on every page and POST, the issue queue and its group filter, the preview with 15 hours / 90% and the grade offered, the exact refusal on the screen, issuing and the second click, the warning's two steps through the screen, the list and its filters, the sheet in both languages with its QR token opening the public page, a revoked sheet with its mark and the reason shown to staff only, a refused empty reason, no code without `PUBLIC_BASE_URL`, another entity's certificate / enrolment / group 404 with a positive control, no money, the stylesheet and template scanners, the premium-tone word scan, the customer-voice rules of `reports.tests_customer_voice` applied to what a parent or student reads, and a check that no Arabic string of the feature starts or ends with a Latin letter, `0009` on a database already holding students, a group, an enrolment and attendance, and `makemigrations --check`.
- **Where this differs from the plan (`EDUCATION_PLAN.md`, E6):** (1) migration `0009`, not `0010`. (2) The plan's verify page lists hours; the PR's brief did not, so hours are **not** on the public page (they are on the sheet). (3) The plan's «issued when…» became «finished» (stopped, or the term ended, its last day included). (4) The plan said «grade text from E2's final exam if any»; only a **published** final is offered and the manager decides, because the page is public. (5) The plan's token had no expiry rule; none is applied. (6) A tutoring centre is out (the plan's question 7 default is training and languages).
- **Risks:** (1) **The sheet's dates are not stored.** The plan's table has no period columns and none was added, so the period printed on the sheet and the verify page is derived from the enrolment and the group (capped at the day of issue): editing the group's term dates later, or stopping an enrolment that was certified at term's end on an earlier day than the term's last, can move the printed period of an old certificate. Hours, rate and grade are frozen. Two date columns (`period_from`, `period_to`) would fix it and are the recommended follow-up. (2) **A renamed student, course or business shows the new name** on the verify page and on a reprint; the name is not snapshotted (no column). The issue audit row keeps the name as it was printed. (3) **Rotating `SECRET_KEY` kills every printed QR** (every share link too). (4) The throttle is per process (the lab page's rule), so with several workers the limits are per worker. (5) A certificate is paper once printed and can be shown to anyone; revoking is what makes the page say so. (6) No pass mark: a certificate can be issued for a low grade; the grade is only printed. (7) No bulk issue for a whole group (one click per student from the queue).
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Training and language centres only? (Yes; a tutoring centre or a nursery has none.)
  2. Warn, do not block, below 75% attendance or with fees owed? (Yes, 75% a constant, the second step ticks «أصدرها برضو».)
  3. Who issues and revokes? (Managers: `manage_parties`; no new permission, a dedicated one later in the way of HG-084 if wanted.)
  4. Is a certificate's grade taken from E2? (Offered from a **published** final exam and editable; an unpublished mark is never offered.)
  5. Hours: all classes held while the student was enrolled, not only the ones attended? (Yes, as the plan; the rate says how many were attended and is not on the public page.)
  6. May an enrolment stopped early (a drop-out) be certified? (Yes if a class was held; the warning shows low attendance. The alternative is to require the term to have ended.)
  7. A pass mark below which no certificate is issued? (Not built.)
  8. Show the period and name as frozen? (Not frozen: needs `period_from`, `period_to` and a name column, a schema change beyond the plan; see risks 1 and 2.)
  9. Bulk issue for a whole group, and sending the certificate to the guardian by message? (Not built; a message would carry a link to a page that names a minor, which needs its own decision.)

## HG-100 — Manufacturing F11: a production order puts exactly its good units into stock, and a run whose goods have left cannot be cancelled (two live defects; no schema)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/f11-production-defects` under Ahmed's delegation of 7 Nov 2026, **awaiting his approval before merge**. T3: it changes the quantity that enters stock from a production order (and so that order's unit cost), and it refuses some run cancellations that pass today. No table, no column, no migration (`makemigrations --check` is clean), no new permission. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «7. تصنيعي»: F11 has no checklist item; it protects item 4 (actual cost) and item 6 (scrap). Written as «Manufacturing F11» (a bare «F11» may be a finding id elsewhere). HG-097 and HG-099 are reserved by other open branches.
- **Both defects were proved first** on `develop` as it stood, by tests written before any fix (`manufacturing/tests_f11.py`, first commit, marked as expected failures until the fix): 99.999 and 99.996 units in stock for orders of 100; and the cancel that left 5 cakes worth 102.50, 20.50 each.
- **Defect 1, units lost to batch rounding. What changes in quantity and money:**
  - An order's batches are its quantity ÷ the recipe's output, kept to three decimals (`orders._batches`). The finish used to put `recipe output × batches − scrap` into stock, so the rounding of the batches came back as missing units.
  - **Worked example A (batch of 3):** an order for 100 rusks, 3 kg of flour a batch at 20.00. 100 ÷ 3 = 33.333 batches; flour issued 3 × 33.333 = 99.999 kg = 1,999.98. **Before:** 99.999 units entered stock at 20.0000. **Now:** 100.000 units at 19.9998 (1,999.98 ÷ 100). The value entering stock is 1,999.98 both times.
  - **Worked example B (tray of 12):** an order for 100, 6 kg of flour a tray, labour 60.04, overhead 40.00. 8.333 trays; flour 49.998 kg = 999.96; total 1,100.00. **Before:** 99.996 units at 11.0004. **Now:** 100.000 units at 11.0000.
  - **Scrap follows the same rule:** the run's `scrap_quantity` is now the order's quantity − its good units. Before, it was `recipe output × batches − good units`: 4 defects in example A were recorded as 3.999 of scrap (now 4.000), and an order for 2 with a batch of 3 (0.667 of a batch, 2.001 "planned") recorded 0.001 of scrap that never was (now 0.000).
  - **What does not change:** the materials issued (recipe × the rounded batches, at the first stage), their cost, work in progress, labour and overhead absorbed, the formula `cost_round((materials + labour + overhead) ÷ good units)`, and every order whose quantity is a whole number of batches (the HG-036 figures are identical: 20 cakes at 14.5000, 19 at 15.2632; `manufacturing/tests_hg036.py` is untouched and passes). A plain run from the recipe page, the restaurant kitchen (`restaurant/kitchen.py::_produce`) and the demo seed never say an output, so they still make recipe × batches, byte for byte (`restaurant/tests_kitchen.py` untouched and passing; a plain run of 33.333 batches still makes 99.999, pinned).
  - **How:** `manufacturing.services.produce` takes a new keyword `output` (default `None` = the old expression). `manufacturing.orders.finish` passes `output=good_units(order)` and `scrap=order quantity − good units`. A said output of zero or less is refused with the existing «مفيش ولا وحدة سليمة» message.
  - **Runs already posted are not rewritten.** A finished order that came in 0.001 short stays as it is; a stock adjustment corrects it if anyone cares.
- **Defect 2, cancelling a run whose goods have left. What changes:**
  - `cancel_run` reverses the product first through the engine's `cancel_stock_operation`, at the run's own unit cost. The engine refuses only when the location holds fewer units than the run made.
  - **Worked example:** run 1 makes 20 cakes at 14.50 (290.00). 15 are sold (cost of sales 217.50). Run 2 makes 20 at 16.00 (320.00). Stock: 25 cakes worth 392.50, **15.70** each. **Before:** cancelling run 1 passed, took 20 out at 14.50 (290.00) and left 5 cakes worth 102.50, **20.50 each**, for cakes that cost 14.50 and 16.00. **Now:** it is refused, nothing moves (25 cakes, 392.50, 15.7000; no stock row, no cancelled operation, no audit row), and the message says what to do instead. Cancelling run 2 (nothing has left since it) passes and leaves 5 cakes worth 72.50 at 14.5000.
  - **The rule** (`manufacturing.services._left_since`, in `manufacturing`, not in the engine): a run cannot be cancelled when its product has an outgoing stock movement at the run's location written **after** the run's output movement: a sale, a purchase return, a transfer out, an adjustment out, or use in another run. "After" is the order the rows were written in (the movement's id), not their dates: cakes sold this morning do not stop the noon run being cancelled, and a sale typed in later with yesterday's date does.
  - **Two kinds of outgoing rows do not count,** because they are not the product leaving. (a) A reversal row: it takes back what one incoming operation put in, at that operation's own cost. Without this, of two runs of the same product only one could ever be cancelled, since cancelling either writes an outgoing row after the other. (b) The row of an adjustment or transfer that has itself been reversed since: the units came back whole, at the cost they left at (a baker who made dough, used it in a bread run, then cancels the bread run and after it the dough run). The plan's wording had neither exception; both are pinned by tests.
  - **The message,** exact: «مينفعش تلغي التشغيلة دي، عشان في كمية من «كيكة» خرجت من المخزن بعد ما التشغيلة اتسجلت (بيع أو تحويل أو تسوية أو استخدام في تشغيلة تانية). لو الرصيد محتاج تصحيح، اعمل تسوية مخزون عادية.» / "This run cannot be cancelled: some “كيكة” has left the location since the run was recorded (a sale, a transfer, an adjustment or use in another run). To correct the stock, record an ordinary stock adjustment."
  - **Locking:** `cancel_run` locks the product's item row (the engine's own `lock_items_for_stock_check`) before it checks, so a sale cannot slip in between the check and the reversal. Sales posting and the engine take the same lock.
  - **What does not change:** `inventory/services.py` (the engine, the movement and cost rules, `cancel_stock_operation`) is not edited. A run with nothing gone cancels exactly as before (product out, every material back). Cancelling an order on the floor (`orders.cancel`) is untouched: it has no output. `cancel_run` keeps its signature.
- **A second person's approval (FB-27):** a new action `manufacturing.cancel_run` («إلغاء تشغيلة إنتاج» / "Cancel a production run") in `approvals/actions.py`, code only, and `approvals.gate.held` in `manufacturing.views.run_detail`, wired as the other held actions are: nothing is held until the owner ticks it; whoever may approve cancels directly; anyone else's cancel waits and nothing moves (stock rows, operations, the product's quantity, value and average, journal entries and totals, the run's status and the audit log are compared before and after); on approval it runs once, in the requester's name; the approver is never the requester. A cancel the service would refuse anyway (no reason, already cancelled, goods gone) is said on the page at once and no request is sent; goods sold while a request waited make the approval fail with the same message and nothing moves. The document's scope is `entities.scope.PRODUCTION_RUN` (`location__entity`, the path the run page already uses), a new constant.
- **Protected items touched:** the quantity entering stock from an order's finish, and so its unit cost (`produce`, `finish`); when a run may be cancelled (`cancel_run`). Not touched: the inventory engine, average-cost logic, sales and purchase posting, the ledgers and the projector, the permission core (no new code; the action asks `inventory.adjust_stock`, as the page does), report calculations, closing.
- **Files:** `manufacturing/services.py` (`produce`: the `output` keyword; `_left_since`, `check_cancellable`, `cancel_run`; one message in two languages; the module's rules text), `manufacturing/orders.py` (`finish`), `manufacturing/views.py` (`run_detail`), `approvals/actions.py`, `entities/scope.py` (one constant), `manufacturing/tests_f11.py` (new), `docs/AGENT_HARD_GATES.md` (this entry and dated notes under HG-030 and HG-036), `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md` (a dated note; no percentage change). No template changed: the refusal shows in the run page's existing error box. No existing test was edited.
- **Interplay with Manufacturing F1 (HG-098):** `make_to_order` reads a finished order's `run.output_quantity` to know what it covers. That figure is now exact: an order for 100 with a batch of 3 used to leave 0.001 "still to make" on its sales order line and now leaves 0; with 4 defects it leaves exactly 4. Cancelling its run (where the rule allows it) frees the line again; once the customer was invoiced the run stays and so does what it covers. `manufacturing/tests_make_to_order.py` is untouched and passes.
- **Tests:** `manufacturing/tests_f11.py` (53), every money scenario on the manufacturing chart and again on a restaurant bakery's chart with the module on: 100 with a batch of 3 → 100.000 units, 1,999.98, 19.9998, the output's journal entry 1,999.98 = 1,999.98; a tray of 12 → 100.000 at 11.0000 with 100.04 absorbed; 4 defects → 96.000 good and 4.000 scrap at 20.8331; an order smaller than a batch → no scrap; exact orders identical (290.00, 14.5000; 15.2632); a plain run unchanged (99.999 at 20.0000); `produce(output=…)` and its refusal; the two-run example refused with 25 / 392.50 / 15.7000 unchanged and the exact message in both languages; run 2 cancelled (5 / 72.50 / 14.5000); a plain run cancelled as before; a transfer out, an adjustment out, use in another run and a back-dated sale each block; what left before the run does not; two runs cancelled in either order; an outgoing adjustment reversed since does not block; a cancelled sale still blocks; the run page says why; the approval tests above; the make-to-order scenarios above. After each money scenario: trial balance balanced, the reconciliation rows compared, and raw materials, work in progress, finished goods, absorbed cost and cost of sales asserted to the piastre.
- **Risks and what is left open:**
  1. **The stock operations screen is not covered.** The rule lives in `cancel_run`. Reversing the run's output adjustment itself from «عمليات المخزون» (`inventory.views.operation_cancel` → `cancel_stock_operation`) still goes through the engine alone and reproduces the worked example (5 cakes at 20.50, the run still «posted»). It is pinned by a test as a known gap. It was possible before this change and the brief kept the engine and its screen out of scope. Proposed follow-up, the gift-card precedent (HG-071, D4): that screen refuses to reverse an operation that belongs to a production run and points to the run's page.
     - **Note, 13 Nov 2026 (see HG-102):** closed by Manufacturing F11b. The screen now refuses an operation that belongs to a production run or to a production order and links to that document's page; the test that pinned this gap (`manufacturing/tests_f11.py`) now tests the refusal. One part stays open and is pinned there: a business with the manufacturing module switched off (HG-102, question 1).
  2. **A cancellation that passed yesterday may be refused today,** and the user is sent to an ordinary stock adjustment, which goes out at today's average cost and books to stock loss, not back to the materials.
  3. **Found while testing, not caused by this change:** on a chart without work-in-progress and absorbed-cost accounts (a restaurant bakery with the module on), `ledger.reports.reconciliation()` reports the cost of sales out by exactly what is on the floor less the labour and overhead absorbed (HG-036's fallback puts both in 5101). Stock, cash and the parties reconcile. The tests state the difference instead of hiding it (−100.00 for an order with 60.00 labour and 40.00 overhead). Unchanged by F11.
  4. The four-decimal unit cost can still leave a piastre in work in progress (13,000.00 over 780 units); that is Manufacturing F12's subject, unchanged here. The examples above land exactly.
- **Default decisions for the owner (built in brackets; change on request):**
  1. Should the stock operations screen also refuse to reverse a run's own operations? (Not built: the engine and its screen were out of scope. See risk 1.)
  2. Is run cancellation its own tick on the approvals screen, or should ticking «عكس تسوية أو تحويل مخزون» hold it as well? (Its own tick, off until the owner ticks it, as every action starts. An owner who already holds stock reversals must tick «إلغاء تشغيلة إنتاج» too.)
  3. A sale that was cancelled afterwards: does it still stop the run being cancelled? (Yes. A cancelled invoice brings the units back as a return, and the rule does not read that as the sale never having happened. A reversed adjustment or transfer does not block.)
  4. A transfer to another branch keeps the value inside the group; should it block? (Yes: the units are no longer where the run made them.)
  5. Should the 0.001-unit shortfalls of runs already posted be corrected? (No: nothing posted is rewritten.)
  6. The action's code is `manufacturing.cancel_run`, following the `app.action` codes already in `approvals/actions.py`; the plan called it `cancel_production_run`, which is the name of the function that runs it.

## HG-101 — E3 guardian portal: a public page that shows minors' data (`education.GuardianLink`)

HG-100 is reserved by another open task and is not in this tree: the gap is on purpose.

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/e3-guardian-portal` (cut from `claude/e6-completion-certificate`, so the migration is `0010` after E6's `0009`) under Ahmed's delegation of 10 Nov 2026, awaiting his review. T2 by size, **T3 by privacy**: a new table, and an unauthenticated page that names children and shows their attendance, marks, nursery days and fees. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «4. تعليمي», item 16 «تطبيق أو بوابة لولي الأمر», PR E3.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing moves. Making, opening, answering, revoking and the pay button write no sales invoice, payment, cashbox movement, ledger row, journal entry, stock movement, outbox row or `PaymentLink` change (counts and every link's status and amount are pinned before and after, and the audit rows added are exactly the three portal actions). The portal **only reads** attendance, exams and marks, nursery days, the dues figure and the live pay links; its writes are, **per visit** (a GET after the pass), the link's `last_opened_at` and one audit row (a test counts the INSERT/UPDATE statements: exactly those two); **per answer**, only the row's `wrong_answers` counter (a wrong answer: one guarded UPDATE, left raised; a right answer: the same counter taken and given back, two UPDATEs, net zero; a format error, a forged link and the question page write nothing; also counted by a test). No calculation of fees, invoices, posting, stock, cashbox, ledgers, attendance or marks was changed, and `payments`, `messaging`, `exams.py`, `attendance.py` and `billing.py` are **not edited**.
- **Protected items touched:** the migration only: `education/0010_guardian_links` (one `CreateModel` and one partial unique constraint; no existing table, column or row is read or changed). No permission core change, **no new permission, no seeded role**, no setting except `EDU_PORTAL_DAYS` (default 30).
- **Why a table, and why no existing one serves:** the centre must be able to switch a link off at once (a custody dispute, a wrong number, a lost phone), and a stateless signed token cannot be revoked (the lab's, the certificate's and the share link's all are). `Student`, `Customer` and `PaymentLink` cannot hold «this guardian's portal link, in this entity, made by this person, revoked at…».
- **Table** (`education/models.py`): `GuardianLink`: `payer` (PROTECT), `nonce` (unique, random), `entity` (PROTECT, never empty: the student's entity, else the main one), `created_by`, `created_at`, `revoked_at`, `last_opened_at`, and (added at review) `wrong_answers` (small integer, default 0: the lifetime counter, see defence 5). **A partial unique constraint: one live (not revoked) link per payer**, so two people clicking at once cannot make two. The only column the plan did not list is `wrong_answers`, added at review so the lifetime limit survives restarts and is shared by every worker. Rows are never deleted: the audit still names a revoked link.
- **The defences, by number** (modelled on `medical/public.py`, HG-085; `education/portal.py` and `education/views_portal.py`; all in `education/tests_portal.py`):
  1. **Own salt.** The token is `signing.dumps({"l": id, "n": nonce, "f": fingerprint}, salt="hesba.education.portal.v1")`. A share, lab or certificate token never opens it and it never opens theirs (pinned with all four salts). *Test:* `test_every_dead_link_shows_the_same_page_with_the_same_status`.
  2. **The row decides.** A link opens nothing when it is revoked, replaced by a newer one, past `EDU_PORTAL_DAYS` (checked from the **row's `created_at`** and from the signature's timestamp, so a token signed again later does not extend the life), has a wrong nonce or another link's id, belongs to an archived payer, or whose payer's phone or WhatsApp number changed (a keyed HMAC of both numbers in the token, never a plain hash: plan §5 «a change of the payer's phone kills the link»). *Tests:* `test_revoked_and_replaced_links_fail_like_a_forged_one`, `test_an_expired_link_fails_by_the_signature_and_by_the_row`, `test_a_changed_payer_phone_or_an_archived_payer_kills_the_link`.
  3. **One live link per payer, atomically.** `make_link` runs in a transaction, locks the payer row (`select_for_update()`, no joins, so no nullable-join problem on PostgreSQL), revokes the older links, creates the new one; the partial unique constraint is the backstop. «ابعت تاني» revokes the previous one, **across entities** (the brief's wording; per-entity is an owner question). *Test:* `test_the_database_allows_one_live_link_per_payer_and_a_new_one_revokes_the_old`.
  4. **Second factor, and nothing before it.** The **full birth date** (day, month, year) of **any** active child of the payer in the link's entity (changed at review from the birth year, which has about 15 plausible values); only when **none** has a birth date, the last 4 digits of the payer's phone (at least 7 digits on file). A mix (one child with a date, one without) uses the dates alone. The question page has three numeric boxes in one row, day, month, year, in the same left-to-right order in Arabic and English (`inputmode="numeric"`, `autocomplete="off"`, 48 px tall, no date picker); Arabic-Indic and Persian digits are read. An answer that is not a real calendar date, or whose year is outside 1900 to this year, is a **format error**: the same 400 page with a format message, **not counted**; a real date that does not match is a wrong answer and is counted. Before the answer the page names only the centre: no child, group, date or amount (marker strings). Every candidate date is compared (`hmac.compare_digest`, no early exit). *Tests:* `FactorTests`, `test_a_valid_link_shows_only_the_centre_and_the_question`, `test_a_badly_typed_answer_is_a_format_error_and_is_not_counted`.
  5. **Count before compare; the lifetime limit is in the row.** **8 wrong answers in the link's whole life** are counted in `GuardianLink.wrong_answers` by one atomic guarded UPDATE (`wrong_answers < 8` in the WHERE; zero rows updated means the link is spent), taken **before** the answer is compared; a right answer gives its slot back by a guarded decrement (never below 0); a slot refused by a short window is given back too. The row survives a deploy or restart and every gunicorn worker sees it, so the limit is 8 for the link, not 8 per worker. A new link is a new row at 0; revoking or replacing never resets an old row. The three short limits stay in Django's default cache, which is **per process** (the booking, pay and lab pages' precedent): 5 wrong answers per 15 minutes per link, 50 per hour per address, and 60 page loads a minute per address, each slot also taken before the compare. A spent link refuses even the right answer and is the one plain page (a browser that already passed keeps its 30 minutes). *Tests:* `ThrottleTests` (the clock frozen, windows moved explicitly, real limits), `test_the_lifetime_limit_is_in_the_row_so_a_restart_or_another_worker_does_not_reset_it` (the cache is emptied: still dead), `test_an_answer_writes_only_the_counter_and_a_visit_writes_the_open`, and `LifetimeRaceTests`: **20 real threads, exactly 8 compared**, the row ending at 8 (a `TransactionTestCase`; it ran on PostgreSQL with real concurrent connections and on SQLite, where writers take turns).
  6. **A pass cookie that belongs to one link.** Signed with its own salt, value = the link's identity digest, `HttpOnly`, `SameSite=Strict`, path = this link's address, 30 minutes, never the Django session (a staff login in the same browser neither opens nor is touched).
