The content view **requires** it; link A's cookie under link B's name, or holding A's identity under B's name, or unsigned, opens nothing; after a revoke or expiry it opens nothing either. *Tests:* `CookieTests`.
  7. **Private headers on every response**, errors, redirects, 405 and 429 included: `Cache-Control: no-store`, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`, `Referrer-Policy: same-origin`. The pay button is `rel="nofollow noreferrer"` so the portal's address never reaches the pay page. No resource is loaded from another site. *Test:* `HeaderTests`.
  8. **One identical failure page** (forged, altered, other salt, revoked, replaced, expired, another payer's id or nonce, wrong fingerprint, archived payer, no child left, no factor left): the same 404 body (normalised for the token and CSRF value), the same status. A POST to such a link gets that page, not the question. A valid link after a wrong answer says «wrong» and nothing about what is on file; the same answer for any family. **How the brief's «a POST with a wrong answer never reveals whether the token is valid» is read:** a forged token gets the plain 404 page and a valid link answered wrongly gets the 400 question page, as in the lab page (HG-085), because «one identical failure page» and «the question page for a live link» cannot both be literal; what is never revealed is anything about what is on file (which child, which date, which kind of mismatch), and every dead state (revoked, replaced, expired, spent, moved, another payer's) looks exactly like a forged token. Only someone who already holds a genuine link can tell it is live.
  9. **Entity scoping.** An anonymous request has no current entity, so every read runs inside `working_in(link.entity)`: children, groups, exams, invoices and the dues figure are that entity's alone. A branch child is not on a main-entity link and the reverse; a link whose entity holds no child of the payer is the plain page. *Test:* `test_the_links_of_another_entity_show_that_entitys_children_only`, `test_a_multi_entity_install_reads_the_links_entity_alone`.
  10. **Never shown** (marker strings absent from the HTML after the answer, in Arabic and English): `Student.notes`, school, stage, relation, date of birth (in any format, year included), the payer's name and phone, a pick-up person's ID number and phone, who brought a child, teacher pay and rate, the room, `Attendance.note`, the class topic, `Exam.notes`, `ExamMark.note`, `DayRecord.note`, `Enrollment.notes`, an **unpublished** exam and its score, an exam with no mark entered, another family's child, name, invoice or phone, internal links and ledger. *Test:* `test_nothing_internal_and_nothing_of_another_family_is_ever_drawn`.
  11. **Audit without secrets.** `create_guardian_link` (actor, payer, entity, children count, factor kind, days), `revoke_guardian_link` (why: `revoked` or `replaced`), `open_guardian_portal` (**actor None, no IP, no device**; failed tries are throttled, not audited). The token, the nonce and the address are asserted absent from every audit row. *Test:* `test_the_audit_names_the_link_but_never_the_token_or_the_nonce_or_an_address`.
  12. **Only a manager makes or revokes** (`master_data.manage_parties`; owner, manager, accountant). Cashier and stock keeper: no card on the student file, 403 on both POSTs and on the services; signed out: the login. Guard order is the exam screens': not an education install (404), the student or link in the entity being worked in (404), then the permission (403). CSRF is enforced on the answer and on both staff actions; the staff result page is `no-store`.
  13. **The WhatsApp text holds a greeting and the link and nothing else** (no name, date, group, mark or amount; the link on its own line; the business's name mid-sentence), built from `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` only. Without it nothing is made and the old link is left alone. No outbox kind in v1: the centre taps the `wa.me` link itself.
- **Rules of the content** (all inside `working_in(link.entity)`, nothing written): each **active** child of the payer, in name order. Groups: the child's active enrolments in active groups, with days, time and the teacher's name. Attendance: the last 30 days, today included, rate by `attendance.rate` (equal to `attendance.student_summary`), held classes, the dates of absence and of lateness, oldest first; a cancelled class does not count. Marks: **published** exams of the last 12 months the child was on the roster of that day, newest first, with the report card's arithmetic (17/20 = 85.0%, 7.5/10 = 75.0%, average 80.0%; an absent exam listed and left out of the average; **equal to `exams.report_card` when every exam is published**, tested). Nursery days: the last 14 days, arrival, leaving, the pick-up person's name. Fees: the **dues figure** (`billing.dues`, the staff dues screen's own number: invoices of 600.00 and 450.00 with 200.00 paid at the desk **owe 850.00**, both when the 200.00 is unsettled and when it is settled on one invoice), and each open posted fee invoice with what it still asks, allocated the way `paylinks.Board` does (a desk payment lowers the account, not an invoice's `remaining_due`, so the balance is spent newest invoice first: 400.00 + 450.00; asserted equal to the board's own amounts). A «ادفع» button only on an invoice with a **live, payable** pay link that already exists.
- **Where this differs from the plan (`EDUCATION_PLAN.md`, E3) and from the code it named:** (1) migration `0010`, not `0009`. (2) `exams.report_card` / `_student_rows` need a staff user and do not filter on `published`, so the portal has its own read with the same roster rule and the same arithmetic (parity tested); `exams.py` is untouched. (3) `payments.services.live_link` sweeps expired links (an UPDATE and an audit row), which an anonymous page must not do: the portal filters `status=new` and a future `expires_at` and asks `is_payable`; a test shows an expired link stays `new` and nothing is written. (4) `attendance.student_summary` returns absences only, capped at 10, so late and absent dates are read directly (never the mark's note). (5) `paylinks.Board.load` is gated by the viewing user's rights and does not run when payments are off, so its allocation is reproduced in `portal._fees` and pinned equal to it by a test. (6) The plan's «the centre's InstaPay handle text if configured» **is not built**: no such setting exists (E4a did not add one) and a new setting is outside this PR. (7) `contact_buttons.html` uses staff `op-*` classes that the public base page does not load, so the contact card uses the public pages' own `cl-btn`. (8) Plan §5's «a change of the payer's phone kills the link» is a fingerprint in the token, not a column.
- **Risks (privacy of minors first):**
  1. **Closed at review: the weak factor and the per-process lifetime counter.** The first build asked for a birth year (about 15 plausible values, 3 to 6 in a nursery, and any child's year opened the link) and counted the 8-answer lifetime limit in each worker's own cache, so on the real deployment (gunicorn with 2 or 3 workers, no `CACHES` setting) the limit was really 16 to 24 and started again after every deploy, over a 30-day link. Now the factor is a full birth date and the limit is in the row. **What remains:** a forwarded link plus a birth date the holder knows (a relative, an ex-partner) still opens the portal until the centre revokes it, which is the cost of any knowledge factor; the three short windows are still per process (5 per 15 minutes per link is therefore up to 5 per worker, and 50 per hour per address likewise), which only affects the speed of guessing inside the 8-per-life cap; and a birth date a family has printed on a social post is not secret.
  2. **The phone-only mode protects nothing when the number is wrong** (the recipient owns those digits), and 4 digits are 10,000 values against 8 tries. The centre is told so on the student card and on the made page, in both languages. It is advice, not a block. The recommended practice is to enter a child's birth date first.
  3. **A WhatsApp chat keeps the link** on the guardian's phone and any linked device for 30 days. Revoke is the cure; there is no automatic reminder to revoke when custody changes. Moving a child to another payer removes them from the old portal at once (tested).
  4. **The total is the dues figure for the whole account**, so an old non-fee balance (an opening balance, a shop sale to the same customer) is included and explained in one line, not itemised. `billing.dues()` ages every customer on each open (a single query per kind, as the staff dues screen does), which is fine for a centre's size but is the heaviest call on the page; the 60-a-minute limit applies.
  5. **Pick-up people's names are shown** (as the plan says); their ID numbers and phones never are. Invoice rows name the payer's own active children the invoice covers.
  6. **Marks are published per exam by the centre** (HG-091); publishing is the decision to show a child's mark on this page. Taking it back hides it at once.
  7. **No retention rule** for audit rows of opens, one per open (no de-duplication, as the lab's).
  8. **The lab result page (HG-085) has the same two weaknesses** (a birth-year factor and a lifetime counter in the per-process cache) and is **not changed here**; it gets its own follow-up.
    - **Note, 13 Nov 2026 (see HG-106):** closed. The lab result page now asks for the full birth date and counts its lifetime limit in a database row, as this portal does.
  9. The seat/«المحتوى» pages of the plan (E8/E9) and any message kind are **not** here; a future link-bearing outbox kind must join `messaging.services.LINK_KINDS` and be masked.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Who receives the portal? (The paying guardian only; pick-up people get none.)
  2. One live link per payer across entities, 30 days (`EDU_PORTAL_DAYS`), made by managers only? (Yes; the plan says one live link per payer. A link per entity is a later option.)
  3. Is the full birth date of any child the factor, with the phone's last 4 digits only when no child has a birth date? (Yes: changed at review from the plan's birth year; see risk 1.)
  4. How long do marks and attendance reach back? (Attendance 30 days; published exams 12 months; nursery 14 days.)
  5. Show the whole account's dues figure, or only fee invoices? (The dues figure, with a one-line note when other amounts are in it.)
  6. The centre's InstaPay handle on the portal? (Not built: no setting exists.)
  7. May the guardian make a pay link from the portal? (No: staff-made links only; a parent-made link needs `PaymentLink.created_by` nullable, HG-070's area.)
  8. Audit every open, or once per visit? (Every open.)
  9. Show the pick-up person's name? (Yes, never the ID number or phone.)
- **Files:** `education/models.py`, `education/migrations/0010_guardian_links.py` (edited in place at review: it was not merged anywhere, so the `wrong_answers` column is in the same migration), `education/portal.py`, `education/views_portal.py`, `education/urls.py` (two staff routes), `education/views.py` (the student page's card: two lines), `config/urls.py` (one public route `p/edu/<token>/`), `config/settings.py` (`EDU_PORTAL_DAYS`), templates `education/portal.html`, `portal_dead.html`, `portal_made.html`, `_portal_card.html`, `student.html` (one include), static `hesba/css/education_portal.css` (`?v=e3-1`), tests `education/tests_portal.py`. E6's files are untouched except the one-line additions above.
- **Found by Codex on the PR, fixed there:** a pay link made before a desk payment still asks for the old amount. The pay-link board calls such a link stale and withholds it (`paylinks.Board.load`: `live.amount > ask`); the portal offered it. The portal now shows the pay button only when the link's amount is no more than what its invoice still asks after the account's balance is allocated; nothing is cancelled or swept from the public page. Not changed here, and older than this PR: a guardian who still holds that earlier link from WhatsApp can open the pay page with it until staff cancel it (`payments.services.is_payable` reads the invoice's own remaining amount, not the account); that belongs to HG-070's area and is a question for the owner.
- **Tests:** `education/tests_portal.py` (72 at this writing). Token and the one plain page (11 failure kinds, GET and POST); the database constraint and replacement; expiry by signature and by row; phone change, archived payer, custody moved; entity (a branch child, a branch link); the factor matrix (either child's full date, Arabic-Indic and Persian digits, a leading zero, another family's date, one field off, day and month swapped, the phone's digits, a mixed family, neither; format errors not counted; the three boxes' order and attributes); every throttle with the clock frozen and the real limits (8 / 5 / 50 / 60), the lifetime counter in the row (survives an emptied cache, a replaced link keeps its count, the new row starts at 0), 20 threads comparing exactly 8, a re-signed token, what an answer writes; the pass cookie (missing, attributes, 30 minutes, of another link, forged, after revoke, staff login untouched); the headers on every response including failures; no foreign resource; CSRF; the marker strings absent (Arabic and English); unpublished exams hidden and shown on publishing; the report card parity; attendance parity with `student_summary`; nursery; fees (850.00 twice, 400.00 + 450.00 equal to the board, fully paid, other dues, the pay button only where a link is live, expired link not swept, gateway off); nothing posted; write statements of one visit; staff (make, once-only address, WhatsApp text, 403s, revoke, audit with no secrets, phone-only warning, neither factor, no base URL, inactive student, another entity, other installs); the words (customer-voice scan, no Arabic line starting or ending with an English word, both languages complete); the stylesheet (tokens only); the migration on a database that already holds payers and students; `makemigrations --check`. Also run: `education` in full on SQLite and PostgreSQL, `printing`, `payments`, `medical.tests_lab_link`, `reports.tests_route_auth`, `reports.tests_findable`, `reports.tests_demo_names`, `reports.tests_customer_voice`, `settings_core.tests_table_cards`, `settings_core.tests_tokens_adoption`, `config.tests_referrer_policy`.

## HG-102 — Manufacturing F11b: the stock operations screen leaves a production run's and a production order's operations to their own page (closes HG-100's known gap; no schema)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/f11b-run-operations-guard`, cut from `claude/f11-production-defects` (HG-100, PR #395, not merged yet) under Ahmed's delegation, **awaiting his approval before merge**. T3: it changes from where a stock operation may be reversed. No table, no column, no migration (`makemigrations --check` is clean), no new permission. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «7. تصنيعي»: no checklist item and no percentage change; it protects item 4 (actual cost). HG-101 is reserved by another open branch.
- **The gap was proved first,** by three tests written before any fix and marked as expected failures until it (`manufacturing/tests_f11b.py`, first commit). The screen «التحويلات والتسويات» (`inventory.views.operation_cancel`) reversed any adjustment through the engine alone, so a run could be taken apart one operation at a time:
  1. **The run's output, after a sale and a refill** (HG-100's own example): run 1 makes 20 cakes at 14.50 (290.00), 15 are sold, run 2 makes 20 at 16.00 (320.00): 25 cakes worth 392.50, 15.70 each. Reversing run 1's output from the screen took 20 out at 14.50 and left **5 cakes worth 102.50, 20.50 each**, for cakes that cost 14.50 and 16.00, with run 1 still «posted» and nothing of it in stock. `cancel_run` has refused exactly this since HG-100; the screen did not.
  2. **One material of a run:** a plain run of one batch takes 2 kg of flour (40.00), 1 kg of sugar (30.00) and 10 boxes (25.00) and makes 10 cakes at 9.50 (95.00). Reversing the flour's operation from the screen brought the 2 kg back (200 kg again) while the 10 cakes stayed at 9.50, a cost that still holds that flour: stock worth 4,890.00 where 4,850.00 was bought, and the run still «posted».
  3. **A material issued to an order on the floor:** an order for 20 cakes issues 4 kg of flour, 2 kg of sugar and 20 boxes (190.00 in work in progress). Reversing the flour's issue from the screen brought the 4 kg back and left the order «in progress»; its finish then refuses («خامات الأمر ده اترجعت من شاشة المخزون؛ ألغِ الأمر وافتح أمر جديد»), so the order could only be cancelled.
- **What changes:** an operation that belongs to a production run (its output, or one of its materials) or to a production order (a material issued to it) is **not reversed from that screen**. In all three cases above nothing moves now: 25 cakes, 392.50, 15.7000; flour 198 kg; flour 196 kg and the order still finishes at the HG-036 figures (20 cakes, 290.00, 14.5000). The person is told why and is sent to the document's own page, where `cancel_run` (with HG-100's rule) or the order's cancel reverses all of its operations together.
  - **Ownership** is read from the links that already exist: `ProductionRun.output_operation`, `ProductionConsumption.operation`, `OrderMaterialIssue.operation`. Once an order is finished its issued materials are its run's materials too (`produce` uses the same operations), so they belong to the run, which is where they are reversed from then on.
  - **The messages,** exact. For a run: «الحركة دي تابعة لتشغيلة الإنتاج MO-20261111-001، ومينفعش تتعكس من شاشة التحويلات والتسويات. ألغِ التشغيلة من صفحتها، والمنتج بيخرج من المخزن وكل خاماته بترجع مع بعض.» / "This operation belongs to production run MO-20261111-001 and cannot be reversed from the transfers and adjustments screen. Cancel the run from its own page: its product leaves stock and all of its materials come back, together." For an order: «الحركة دي صرف خامات لأمر الإنتاج PO-20261111-001، ومينفعش تتعكس من شاشة التحويلات والتسويات. ألغِ الأمر من صفحته، وكل خاماته بترجع المخزن مع بعض.» / "This operation is a material issue of production order PO-20261111-001 and cannot be reversed from the transfers and adjustments screen. Cancel the order from its own page: all of its materials come back together."
  - **The row:** where «عكس» was, the row of such an operation shows «تابعة للتشغيلة … وبتتلغي من صفحتها.» with the link «افتح صفحة التشغيلة» (`/manufacturing/runs/<pk>/`), or «مصروفة لأمر الإنتاج … وبتتلغي من صفحته.» with «افتح صفحة الأمر» (`/manufacturing/orders/<pk>/`); in English "Open the run" / "Open the order". No new style: the existing `op-muted` class and the table's link colour.
- **Where the check lives, and why there:** in a new module `manufacturing/guards.py` (`owners`, `guarded`, `before_stock_operation_reversal`), asked by the **view** `inventory.views.operation_cancel` right after the entity check (so another entity's operation is still a 404) and before the form, the approval hold and the engine. This is the layer the precedent used: the cashbox operations screen refuses an expense's, a courier settlement's, a payroll's, a gift card's (HG-071, D4) and a cheque's operation in its view, before `held`, and the engine below is called directly by the owning document. A crafted POST meets the same check, whatever it sends and whoever sends it. The engine (`cancel_stock_operation`) was not given the rule: the run's and the order's own cancellation call it directly, so a rule inside it would need a way round it, and that is an edit to protected code which nothing here needs. Only two places reverse an operation a person names (the view, and the approval that runs it later); the other callers of the engine are the owning documents themselves (`manufacturing/services.py`, `manufacturing/orders.py`, `inventory/transfer_requests.py`).
- **A second person's approval (FB-27, «عكس تسوية أو تحويل مخزون»):** the path exists (`approvals.actions._reverse_stock_operation`). The view refuses before anything is held, so no request is sent for such an operation. A request that was **already waiting** when this ships (the operations existed before) asks the same guard when it is approved: it fails with the reason, shown in Arabic on the approvals screen, and nothing moves. An ordinary adjustment is held and reversed on approval as before.
- **Restaurant kitchen runs (what the code says):** `restaurant/kitchen.py` makes plates through `manufacturing.services.produce`, so a preparation is an ordinary `ProductionRun` and its operations show on the stock operations screen with «عكس» like any adjustment. The restaurant has **no cancel of its own** for a preparation: the only screen that cancels a run is the run's page under `/manufacturing/`, and a restaurant has the manufacturing module off (it is not offered to restaurants, except optionally to a bakery), so `settings_core.module_gate` closes that page with a 403.
  - **Built:** the rule holds wherever the owning page can open (`guards.pages_open`, the module gate's own answer). A restaurant bakery with the module on, and any kitchen in a business that has it on, is protected like a factory: the screen refuses and links to the run, and the run's page cancels the preparation whole (plates out, every ingredient back). **With the module off, the screen works as before,** because refusing there would send the person to a closed page and leave a wrongly typed preparation with no way back except loss-and-gain adjustments. A factory cannot reach this: the module is required for the manufacturing activity and cannot be switched off (tested).
  - **So one part of the gap stays open, pinned by a test:** in a restaurant with the module off, 3 plates at 11.00, 2 sold, the recipe grows to 14.00 a plate, 3 more made: 4 plates worth 53.00, 13.25 each; reversing the first preparation's output from the screen leaves **1 plate worth 20.00**. See question 1.
- **What does not change:** `inventory/services.py` is not edited: the engine, `cancel_stock_operation`'s arithmetic, costs, averages, posting and the ledger projector are as they were, and a test pins what the engine alone still does (5 cakes, 102.50, 20.5000 when called directly). `cancel_run`, `check_cancellable`, `_left_since`, `orders.cancel` and their pages are not edited: the run page cancels a run whole (10 cakes, 95.00, 9.5000 → 0, 0.00, 0; flour 198 kg / 3,960.00 → 200 kg / 4,000.00, sugar 19 / 570.00 → 20 / 600.00, boxes 90 / 225.00 → 100 / 250.00, averages 20.0000, 30.0000, 2.5000 throughout), still refuses run 1 of the two-run example with HG-100's message and cancels run 2 (5 cakes, 72.50, 14.5000); the order page cancels an order on the floor and every material comes back (4,850.00 of raw materials, work in progress 0). An adjustment or a transfer that no run and no order made is reversed from the screen as before (flour 193 kg / 3,860.00 → 198 kg / 3,960.00; a transfer of 30 kg back to 200 kg), and the engine's own refusals are still said. Entity scope, permissions (`inventory.transfer_stock` / `inventory.adjust_stock` on the screen and in the engine) and the held actions are untouched.
- **Protected items touched:** from where a stock operation that belongs to a production run or order may be reversed (the screen and the approval of its reversal). Not touched: the inventory engine and its movement and cost rules, average-cost logic, sales and purchase posting, the ledgers and the projector, the permission core, report calculations, closing, models and migrations.
- **Files:** `manufacturing/guards.py` (new), `inventory/views.py` (`operation_list`: the owner of each row; `operation_cancel`: the guard), `approvals/actions.py` (`_reverse_stock_operation`: the guard), `templates/inventory/operations.html` (the link in place of the form), `settings_core/ui_messages.py` (the two Arabic messages), `manufacturing/services.py` (the module's rules text only), `manufacturing/tests_f11b.py` (new), `restaurant/tests_kitchen.py` (a new class), `manufacturing/tests_f11.py` (one test, below), `docs/AGENT_HARD_GATES.md` (this entry and a dated note under HG-100), `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md` (one dated line).
- **Existing test edited (one):** `manufacturing/tests_f11.py::CancelAfterSaleTests::test_the_stock_screens_own_reversal_is_not_covered_yet` pinned the gap by calling the engine directly and expecting 5 cakes at 20.50. It is now `test_the_stock_screens_own_reversal_is_refused_and_names_the_run`: it posts to the screen and expects the refusal with 25 cakes, 392.50, 15.7000 unchanged. What the engine alone does is still pinned, in `tests_f11b`.
- **Tests:** `manufacturing/tests_f11b.py` (31), with the clock held on a Wednesday at noon: the three gap scenarios, on the manufacturing chart and again on a restaurant bakery's chart with the module on; the engine alone, unchanged; the exact message in both languages for the output and each material of a run, and for an order's issue; the row's link, its target and that the target opens, with the reversal form left only on an ordinary adjustment beside them; a finished order's materials belong to its run; a crafted POST (as the form sends it, with nothing in it, with a stray page number) by the owner and by a stock keeper, and a GET, with stock rows, cancelled operations, every item's quantity, value and average, journal entries and totals and the audit log compared before and after; an operation of a cancelled run; the approvals path (nothing sent, a waiting request fails with the reason and nothing moves, an ordinary adjustment still held and reversed); the run page's and the order page's own cancellation with quantity, value and average asserted as Decimals before and after, on both charts, each ending with the trial balance balanced and the control accounts asserted to the piastre; an order refused at the screen still finishes at the HG-036 figures; an ordinary adjustment and an ordinary transfer; another entity's operation is a 404 and its run page too, and the same request from the whole group gets the new message; the module off and on again. `restaurant/tests_kitchen.py::KitchenRunOperationTests` (3): the three kitchen cases above.
- **Risks and what is left open:**
  1. **A business with the manufacturing module off** (a restaurant's kitchen) keeps the old behaviour, wrong average included. See question 1.
  2. **Transfer requests** (`inventory/transfer_requests.py`) also own stock operations (what was sent, received and lost on the way), and each can still be reversed alone from this screen. Same shape, not touched here: the brief was production runs and orders. A project's material issue is a single operation and the project's cost reads only posted ones, so reversing it from the screen stays consistent.
  3. **Operations reversed from the screen before this change stay as they are:** a run left «posted» without its output, or an order whose issue was reversed (it still says so and asks to be cancelled). Nothing posted is rewritten.
  4. A person who may reverse stock operations but may not open the manufacturing pages (`inventory.view_stock`) is refused here and cannot follow the link. No seeded role is in that position (the stock keeper holds both).
  5. Seen on the 390 px screenshot, not caused by this change and not touched: the reversal form inside a row's card is wider than the card, and its labels are cut at the edge.
- **Default decisions for the owner (built in brackets; change on request):**
  1. With the manufacturing module off, should the screen refuse a kitchen run's operations too? (No: it works as before there, since the run's page is closed and nothing else cancels a preparation. Proposed follow-up: «إلغاء التحضير» on the dish's page, calling `cancel_run` with HG-100's rule; then this exception is removed in one line, `guards.pages_open`.)
     - **Note, 14 Nov 2026 (see HG-107):** closed. «إلغاء التحضير» is on the dish's page, and the screen now refuses a kitchen preparation's operations with the manufacturing module off and sends the person to the dish's page. The old behaviour stays only where neither page can open (both modules off after use).
  2. Where does the person land after the refusal? (On the operations list with the message, as on the cashbox screen; the row carries the link to the run or the order. Not sent straight to the run's page.)
  3. A request for approval that was already waiting for such an operation: withdraw it automatically, or let it fail when approved? (It fails when approved, with the reason, and nothing moves.)
  4. A finished order's issued materials: the order's page or the run's? (The run's: that is where they are reversed from once the order is done; the order's page says to cancel the run.)
  5. Should the rows of operations that are already cancelled also link to their run or order? (No: the link stands only where «عكس» used to be.)
  6. Should transfer requests get the same protection? (Not built; see risk 2.)

## HG-103 — Contracting C1: variation orders and extra works (`projects.VariationOrder`, `projects.VariationLine`, `Project.original_value`, `BoqLine.variation`, and one new owner type in the attachments registry)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/contract-c1-variation-orders` under Ahmed's delegation, awaiting his review. **T3**: it changes the base progress certificates bill from (the bill of quantities and the contract value), so it needs his approval before merge. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «6. مقاولات», item 14 «أوامر تغيير وأعمال إضافية» (أساسي), PR C1. HG-101 and HG-102 are not on `develop` (HG-100 is, from Manufacturing F11); this entry takes the next free number and leaves the gap rather than renumbering anything.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing moves by itself, and no past bill is rewritten. A variation order writes no invoice, no payment, no stock, cashbox, customer-ledger or journal row, and touches no certificate. What it does change is **what a later certificate may bill**: an approved order moves quantities in the bill of quantities and creates new items, and the certificate engine (unchanged `create_certificate`) then bills that new work at the project's own retention and advance-recovery rates. On a project billed as a lump sum, approval raises or lowers the typed `Project.contract_value`.
  - Pinned before and after, to the piastre, on the reference contract (1,000,000.00: A 400 م³ × 1,500.00, B 2,000 م² × 200.00; retention 5%, recovery 10%, advance 100,000.00), VAT off and VAT 14%:
    - certificate 1 gross 200,000.00, retention 10,000.00, recovery 20,000.00, net 170,000.00 (VAT on: invoice 228,000.00, net 198,000.00);
    - certificate 2 gross 250,000.00, retention 12,500.00, recovery 25,000.00, net 212,500.00 (VAT on: 285,000.00 / 247,500.00);
    - after both: certified 450,000.00, retention held 22,500.00, recovered 45,000.00, advance left 55,000.00, due now 382,500.00 (VAT on 445,500.00), the owner's account 350,000.00 (VAT on 413,000.00), receivable 382,500.00, retention receivable 22,500.00, customer advances −55,000.00, sales −450,000.00, cash 100,000.00.
    - **Approving VO-1 (+20 م³ on A, −100 م² on B, a new item C 1,000 م² × 80.00 = +90,000.00) leaves every one of those figures identical**, and the two certificates' lines, rates and amounts byte for byte; only the contract value moves, 1,000,000.00 → 1,090,000.00, and with it what is read from it (the billed share 45% → 41%, and what is left of the contract).
    - Certificate 3 on 500 م² of C: gross 40,000.00, retention 2,000.00, recovery 4,000.00, net 34,000.00 (VAT on: VAT 5,600.00, invoice 45,600.00, net 39,600.00).
    - **The advance is never recovered twice:** billed to the original 1,000,000.00 the advance is recovered in full (100,000.00, advance left 0.00); the 90,000.00 of extra work that follows recovers **0.00**, retains 4,500.00 and is net 85,500.00. The recovery cap (`min(gross × rate, the advance left on its date and later, gross − retention)`) is untouched.
  - A characterization suite written **before** any change (`projects/tests_variation_baseline.py`, 10 tests, green on `develop` 3f021d0) pins all of the above for a project with no variation order; it is unedited on this branch (sha256 `8c0cf1d6a0f1760a261316b0161b70586df8099ef0b7fdee6ee3e40eeab1b886`) and still green.
- **Protected items touched (each the smallest change, with its reason):**
  1. **`projects/migrations/0004_variation_orders.py`:** two new tables, and two **nullable** columns on existing tables (`Project.original_value`, `BoqLine.variation`). No data migration, nothing read or rewritten. It follows `0003_site_reports`, so whichever open branch that adds a `projects` migration merges second renumbers.
  2. **`contract.save_boq_line` and `contract.delete_boq_line`:** the lock. Once the contract is **under way** — a live certificate (draft or posted), or an approved variation order — a new item, another quantity, another rate or a deletion is refused with «المشروع عليه مستخلصات أو أوامر تغيير معتمدة؛ تعديل الكميات والفئات وإضافة البنود ومسحها يتم بأمر تغيير من «أوامر التغيير».»; the code, the description and the unit stay editable, because they are words, not value. Needed because without it the contract value still moves silently and the original figure is lost, which is the whole point of the item. **This changes behaviour an existing test pinned** (see below).
  3. **`services.save_project`:** the typed `contract_value` can no longer be changed once an order is approved (same reason: the original plus the approved orders must stay the contract value). Everything else on the form stays editable.
  4. **`insights.time_share`** (a report calculation): the planned end now carries the days approved orders added, so the «متأخر عن الجدول» flag stops shouting after an approved extension of time. One call to `variations.planned_end`; with no approved extension the figure is exactly what it was.
  5. **`attachments/services.py`:** one new owner type (`projects.variationorder`) plus its messages. No `attachments` migration and no schema change; the existing owners behave exactly as before and the whole attachments suite is green.
  - **Untouched:** posting (sales and purchases), the ledger projector, the customer and supplier ledgers, cashbox, inventory and average cost, closing, `create_certificate`, `position`, retention and advance arithmetic, and the permission core. **No new permission code and no seeded role.**
- **Why no existing table serves:** `BoqLine` holds the current state only — no history, no approval, no document. The audit log is not a paper the owner signs and cannot keep unapproved work out of a certificate. Nothing anywhere holds the original contract value once the bill of quantities moves.
- **Tables and columns:**
  - `VariationOrder`: project (PROTECT), `number` (**unique per project**), `order_date`, `title`, `reason`, `status` (draft / approved / rejected / cancelled), `extension_days`, `lump_amount` (NULL except on a project without a bill of quantities), `value` (what it added, fixed at approval), `approved_on`, `owner_reference`, `decided_by`, `decided_at`, `decision_note`, `created_by`, `created_at`, `updated_by`, `updated_at`.
  - `VariationLine`: order (CASCADE), `position`, `boq_line` (PROTECT, NULL for a new item), `code`, `description`, `unit` (copied, so the order reads the same later), `quantity_change` (+ or −, 3 decimals), `rate`, `amount`.
  - `Project.original_value` (Decimal, NULL) and `BoqLine.variation` (FK, NULL, PROTECT: the order that created the item).
- **`original_value` has no data migration, unlike the plan.** The plan asked for a fill from today's bill-of-quantities total. Built instead: **NULL means «no order was approved, so the original is the contract value»**, and the column is written **once**, at the first approval, from the value as it stood then. For every project already under way, the lock makes that the same figure the plan's fill would have written, with no migration touching rows. The honest cost: a quantity raised silently **before** this PR is already inside that «original», and no data can recover the true signed figure. A project migrated forward reads `original_value` NULL and its bill of quantities unchanged (pinned by a migration test).
- **Rules (`projects/variations.py`):**
  - a **draft** changes nothing: its new items do not exist, so no certificate can bill them;
  - **approval** runs in one transaction with the project row and the items locked: each changed item's quantity moves, each new item becomes a `BoqLine` marked with the order, and on a lump-sum project the contract value moves by the amount. The arithmetic must close (original + approved orders = the contract value) or the whole approval is undone;
  - a line's value is **its item's amount after less its amount before**, never the rounded product of the change, so the items always add up to the contract value to the piastre (pinned with awkward decimals);
  - an **omission** may not take an item below what certificates already billed, nor below zero; checked when the order is drafted **and again at approval**, because a certificate may be made in between (pinned: a refusal leaves the bill of quantities and the new item untouched);
  - a **rate is never changed**: work at another rate is a new item («A-2 خرسانة مسلحة بفئة معدّلة»); earlier certificates keep their lines, rates and amounts;
  - an **approved, rejected or cancelled order is never edited, decided again or deleted**: a mistake is corrected by another order, and numbers are never reused;
  - limits: at most 60 lines, an extension of 0–3650 days, a title of 160 and a reason of 3,000 characters; the order date is today or earlier and the owner's approval is dated on or after it and not in the future;
  - the project must be **open**, and its money must be in the entity being worked in.
- **Who (existing codes only):** **sees** the register, an order and its print: `projects.view_projects` or `sales.view_sales_invoices` (the project screens' own rule). **Drafts, edits, attaches the signed copy and cancels a draft:** `projects.record_progress` or `sales.create_sales_invoice` — the site engineer drafts. **Records the owner's approval or rejection:** `sales.create_sales_invoice` only; a site engineer never approves (403 on the POST, and no button). A stock keeper holds neither view right: 403 everywhere.
- **Entities:** unlike C2a/C2b's documents, a variation order **is** narrowed to the entity that holds the project's money, as the certificate screens are, because it changes the billing base: another entity gets 404 on an order, its edit, its print and its files, an empty register with no «أمر تغيير جديد», no original/revised line in the header, and a refusal on every write (drafting, editing, approving, rejecting, cancelling, and attaching or deleting the signed copy). The project page and its documents stay un-narrowed as HG-095 explains. The signed copy follows its order: from another entity the project's own file and delete URLs answer 404, and the registry refuses attaching and deleting (`can_attach` / `can_delete` call `variations.in_reach`). **Leftover risk:** the signed copy is `group_wide` like the project's other files, so the generic **read** URL `/attachments/file/<pk>/` still serves it by id to anyone with the project right in another entity; the generic delete is refused by the same rule.
- **The signed copy:** an attachment owned by the order (JPG/PNG/WEBP or PDF, the usual 5 MB per file, **6 files and 12 MB per order**). **The approval is refused without one.** Files are added and removed while the order is a draft; once it is decided the copy is the record and is kept (adding and deleting both refused in words).
- **Audit rows:** `module="projects"`, `object_type="projects.VariationOrder"`: `save_variation_order` (create and edit), `approve_variation_order` (with the contract value before and after, the original value, the approval date and how many signed copies), `reject_variation_order`, `cancel_variation_order`. They keep the figures and the item codes and **never the title or the reason**. The attachments service writes its own rows for the signed copy.
- **Screens:** a «أوامر التغيير» tab: the register (the original, the approved orders, the revised value, what is awaiting approval and the extension of time, then the orders newest first with value and status); the form (new and edit: an «زيادة / نقص» chooser beside each item of the bill of quantities, rows for new items, the reason and the days, or one amount on a lump-sum project); one order with its lines, what the contract becomes, the signed-copy card and the approve / reject / cancel forms; and the A4 print with the three signature lines (المقاول، الاستشاري، المالك) and the status across a draft. The project header shows «الأصلية … · أوامر التغيير +…» under the contract value once an order is approved, and the bill of quantities marks each item an order added with «أمر تغيير n», shows the lock note and offers «أمر تغيير جديد». Arabic and English, phone (390 px, controls ≥ 44 px, the save row sticking above the tab bar) and desktop; tokens only, `projects.css` bumped to `?v=c1-vo1` everywhere it is pinned.
- **Existing tests edited (all four for this change, none weakened):**
  - `projects/tests_contract.py` — `test_a_certified_item_keeps_its_rate_and_cannot_shrink_below_or_be_deleted`: «more quantity is fine» and «an uncertified item can go» after a certificate are now refusals with «يتم بأمر تغيير», and the total stays 200,000.00 (it became 120,000.00 before). **This is the behaviour change the plan's owner question 1 predicted.**
  - `projects/tests_documents.py` — the group-wide list is now three owner types.
  - `projects/tests_c2_ui.py` — the 0003 migration test reads through the 0003 model state (today's models carry 0004's columns), and the stylesheet tag.
  - `reports/tests_demo_catalogs.py` — the demo project's bill of quantities is 6 items, the sixth added by the seeded order.
  - `reports/tests_insights.py` — the builder's dashboard tile reads the billed share against the **revised** contract value, so the demo's figure is 32%, not 35%. Nothing in the tile changed; the demo's contract did.
- **Files:** `projects/models.py`, `projects/migrations/0004_variation_orders.py`, `projects/variations.py` (new), `projects/views_variations.py` (new), `projects/contract.py` (the lock, two messages, `under_way`), `projects/services.py` (the value lock, one message), `projects/insights.py` (`time_share`), `projects/access.py`, `projects/documents.py`, `projects/urls.py`, `projects/views.py`, `projects/contract_views.py` (tab words, the header figures, the BOQ marks), `projects/demo.py`, `attachments/services.py`, templates `projects/variations.html`, `variation_form.html`, `variation.html`, `variation_print.html` (new), `_tabs.html`, `_head.html`, `boq.html`, `static/hesba/css/projects.css` (`?v=c1-vo1`, tokens only), `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py` (one call), tests below.
- **Tests:** `projects/tests_variation_baseline.py` (10, written first, unedited), `projects/tests_variations.py` (38) and `projects/tests_variation_ui.py` (32): 80 new. **Rules:** a draft changes nothing (every money table, the bill of quantities, the position, the owner's account and both certificates identical before and after) and its new item cannot be billed; a draft is edited in place and its lines replaced; numbers run per project and the database refuses a duplicate; fifteen refusals in words (title, date, the future, the days, a quantity that is not a number, an item twice, an unknown item, below zero, the new-item rows, an empty order, an amount on a project with a bill of quantities); blank and zero rows dropped; who may draft; a closed project; the audit row with figures and never the text. **Approval:** the bill of quantities written, the original value kept, both certificates and the whole ledger unchanged; certificate 3 on the new item; the raised and the cut item billed to their new limits; the recovery cap across a variation; an omission refused at drafting and again at approval; a certified rate never changed and a new line at the new rate billable; the items adding up with awkward decimals; the signed copy and the approval dates; an approved order never edited, approved again, rejected or cancelled; the copy kept once decided; only the office decides; a rejected order on the register changing nothing; the approval audited with the contract before and after; a second order starting from the revised value; another entity refused. **VAT 14%:** certificates 1 and 2 unchanged by the approval, certificate 3 at 45,600.00 / 39,600.00 and the ledger. **The lock:** free before the first certificate, every value change refused after, words still editable, withdrawing the draft frees it again, an approved order locks it too, an item named on an order not deleted, a draft read at the rate as it stands at approval. **Lump sum:** +50,000.00 on 300,000.00 and billed, an omission never below what is billed, items refused, the typed value and a late bill of quantities locked. **Time:** an approved 30 days moving `time_share` from 65 to 57 and clearing the behind flag, a rejected one moving nothing, a project with no dates. **Migration:** 0004 follows 0003, five operations and no data migration, an existing project and its bill of quantities untouched, `original_value` NULL. **Screens:** the empty register and its message, the register's figures and statuses read from the one element that holds each, the header line, every project page linking to the tab, a closed project read-only; the form writing a draft, the direction chooser making a change negative, a refusal in Arabic with what was typed, the edit form, the lump-sum form; the order page's lines and totals, the approval needing the copy and then moving the contract, reject and cancel, the copy uploaded, opened and deleted through the project, a wrong file and another project's order refused, the print; 403 for a stock keeper on every screen and POST, a reader seeing but not acting, the engineer drafting and cancelling but refused on approve and reject, another project's order id 404 on every screen, another entity seeing none of it; both languages with no English-opening Arabic sentence and no Arabic in the English pages, dates in words, named controls, the shared row roles; and the scanners (no pre-written `data-label`, tokens only, every template pinning the new stylesheet tag, `makemigrations --check` clean).
- **Demo:** `prepare_demo` adds one approved variation order to the contracting demo's project that has a bill of quantities: +10% on the second item, a new item «تجهيزات إضافية للموقع العام» at 65,000.00 and 14 days more time, with a signed copy attached, approved two days ago with «محضر اجتماع رقم 7». It goes through the same service the screens use, is idempotent and moves no money.
- **Two things worth seeing in the diff that are not the feature itself:** the variation form calls a bill-of-quantities line `boq`, not `item`, because a repo-wide scanner refuses `{{ item.unit }}` in any template (Codex #233: a master-data item's unit is read through `unit_label`; a bill-of-quantities line's unit is free text and is printed as typed). And the demo's new order moves one dashboard figure (above).
- **Risks:** (1) The lock is a real change for anyone used to editing the bill of quantities after the first certificate; the page says what to do instead and links to the new order, but it is the behaviour Ahmed should look at first. The existing wording on that tab still reads «البند ده اتعمل عليه مستخلص، فالفئة بتاعته ثابتة», which breaks the no-«بتاع» rule; it is older text and out of this PR's scope. (2) `original_value` for a project already under way is today's total, not the signed one (above). (3) The extension of time only moves the calendar share and the behind flag; there is no schedule yet (C3). (4) A variation order on a **subcontract** does not exist (C11). (5) The owner approves on paper; the public link is C10. (6) The signed copy is reachable by id through the generic attachments URL in another entity (above). (7) Migration numbering with another open branch that adds a `projects` migration. (8) The screens have not been seen by Ahmed; screenshots at 390 px are in the PR.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (the default built in brackets):**
  1. After the first certificate, must every quantity raise, rate change, new item and deletion go through a variation order? (**Yes**, and a draft certificate already counts; before the first certificate the bill of quantities is free. The code, the description and the unit stay editable at any time.)
  2. Variations on a lump-sum project: one amount that raises or lowers the contract value? (**Yes**, and the extra work is billed as a lump-sum certificate as today.)
  3. Is the owner's approval recorded by staff with the signed copy attached, or given by the owner through a link? (**Staff record it**, and the approval is refused without a copy attached; the link is C10.)
  4. Does an approved variation change the advance or the rates? (**No**: the advance stays as received, the same recovery rate and cap, the same retention rate. Extra work above the original contract recovers nothing more.)
  5. Who approves? (**The office only**, `sales.create_sales_invoice`; the site engineer drafts, edits and cancels a draft. No new permission code.)
  6. Should an approved order be editable or reversible? (**No**: it is never edited or deleted; a correction is another order. Nothing «un-approves».)
  7. Should a variation order be visible from another entity, read-only? (**No**: 404, as a certificate is, because it changes the billing base.)
  8. Limits on the signed copy: 6 files and 12 MB per order? (**Built so**, under the global share, with the 5 MB per file kept.)
  9. Should the original contract value of projects that already moved be corrected by hand? (**Not built**: the first approval writes today's total as the original. A correction screen is a follow-up if he wants one.)
  10. Should an approved extension of time move the project's own `end_date`? (**No**: the end date stays as signed and the extension is shown and read beside it, so the signed date is never lost. C3 will read the same figure.)

## HG-104 — E9 group content: links to videos, files and online classes shared with one study group (`education.GroupContent`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/education-e9-group-content` (cut from `develop` at `58c983d`, so the migration is `0011` after E3's `0010`), awaiting Ahmed's review. **T2**: one new table, one screen card and one extra block on the guardian portal. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «4. تعليمي», item 20 «حصص أونلاين ومحتوى», PR E9.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** nothing moves. Adding, editing, hiding, showing and removing content, and a guardian reading it, write no sales invoice, payment, cashbox movement, ledger row, journal entry, stock movement or outbox row (counts are pinned around a whole run). No message is sent: the centre shares the content on its own channels, and Hesba holds a title and an address.
- **What the table is:** `education.GroupContent`, one row for one thing shared with one study group: `group` (PROTECT), `kind` (`video`, `file`, `online` = an online class), `title` (up to 160), `url` (up to 500 characters; plain text, **not** a `URLField`, so the only validator is the one below), `note` (optional, up to 255), `starts_at` (optional; the start of an online class), `published` (default on), `active` (default on: the soft removal), `created_by`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. One index on `(group, active, published)` serves both queries (the staff list and the guardian's). **Its entity is its group's**, as an `Exam`'s is (every read goes through `group__in=services.scoped(StudyGroup.objects)`): there is no entity column of its own that could drift from the group's. Nothing is hard-deleted.
- **Protected items touched:** the migration only: `education/0011_group_content` (one `CreateModel`; no existing table, column, row or migration is read or changed; a test runs it on a database that already holds a group). No permission core change, **no new permission, no seeded role, no setting**. `education/portal.py` gains one read (below); the portal's defences (HG-101) are unchanged.
- **Why a table, and why no existing one serves:** a link has to be listed, hidden, removed and shown to the right guardians. `ClassSession` is one meeting on one day, not a shelf of material; `Exam` is a paper with marks; nothing else holds «this title and this address for this group».
- **Link safety (the security core).** Hesba never fetches, embeds or proxies the address: a guardian's browser opens it in a new tab. So the only risk the address carries is what a tap on it does. One function, `education.content.clean_url`, decides, and the screens, the guardian portal (which checks every row again before drawing it) and the tests all use it. **Accepted:** `https://` and a real host (dotted ASCII labels with a letters-only last label, or punycode), an optional port, any path, query and fragment, at most 500 characters; surrounding whitespace is trimmed, and only the scheme is lower-cased (a video id in a path is case-sensitive). **Refused:** `http:`, `javascript:`, `data:`, `vbscript:`, `file:`, `ftp:` and every other scheme; an address with no scheme (`//host`, `example.com`); `https:host` with no slashes; a user name or password (`user:pass@host`), and any `@` in the host part; a backslash (a browser reads it as «/», the parser does not); a space, a tab, a line break, a control or invisible character anywhere, a bidi override included (it can make an address read as another site in a right-to-left app); an IP address, `localhost` or a bare name as the host; a bad or out-of-range port; a non-ASCII host (look-alike letters); and anything over 500 characters. Every link is drawn with `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"` (the portal answers with `Referrer-Policy: same-origin` as well). Titles, notes and addresses are always auto-escaped (a title with `<script>` and an address with a quote both come out inert; tests read the row and the portal section). The audit rows carry the title, the kind, the flags and **the host only**, never the whole address (an address can carry a share key).
- **Who changes it (staff):** the same people as the exam screens: whoever may take the group's attendance, **a manager or the group's own teacher** (`attendance.can_take`). That is the permission that guards entering a group's exams; editing the group itself is `master_data.manage_parties`, which would have shut the teacher out of what the teacher is meant to do. No new permission. Guard order is the exam screens': not an education install (404), then the group or row in the entity being worked in (404), then the permission (403). Hiding, showing and removing are **POST only** (a GET is a 405) and every change is CSRF protected. Screens: a card «محتوى المجموعة» on the group page (and a page of its own, linked from today's classes, for a teacher who may not open the school's lists) listing kind, title, date, shown or hidden, with add, edit, hide or show, and remove (a «confirm removal» button behind «remove…»).
- **What the portal shows, and to whom:** in `education.portal.content()`, after the second factor, each child's section gains «محتوى المجموعة»: the rows that are `published` **and** `active`, of groups where **that child has an ACTIVE enrolment** (and that are themselves running, as the portal's groups card lists only those), inside the link's entity. A sibling in another group, a child whose enrolment was stopped, another family's child, a hidden row, a removed row, a closed group and another entity's group each show nothing. An online class shows «حصة أونلاين», its date and time, and a «ادخل الحصة» button; one whose `starts_at` is **more than a day in the past** is no longer shown to a guardian (staff still see it), and one with no start time stays. Nothing appears before the second factor, and the question page, the dead page and the 404 are unchanged. **Reads only:** the portal's visit still writes exactly two rows (the link's `last_opened_at` and one audit row); the content costs **two extra reads for all the children together** (the children's active enrolments, then the rows), never one per item or per child.
- **Risks:**
  1. **Hesba does not host or protect the content.** Whoever has the YouTube or Drive address can open it, and a forwarded guardian page forwards the buttons. The centre must share each item as «unlisted» (YouTube) or «restricted to specific people» (Drive) and not as public; the staff screens say so in one line. Hesba cannot check how the other site shares it.
  2. **An address can change after it is saved.** The other site can delete the video, change its sharing or reuse the address; Hesba stores the text it was given and does not look again. An online class's address is as good as the meeting behind it.
  3. **A teacher's new item is shown at once** (`published` defaults on). Publishing an exam's marks is a manager's decision (HG-091); for a recorded video the centre's own teacher is trusted to share it. A manager or the teacher can hide it in one tap. Open question 1.
  4. **No retention rule and no hard delete:** a removed row stays in the table (and its address with it) for the audit's sake.
  5. The check is on the **text**; it cannot know that a perfectly shaped `https://` address leads somewhere harmful. A teacher who pastes a bad address is a staff-trust matter, as for any text a teacher types.
- **Not built (on purpose, and said so in the plan):** no file upload, no storage, no video hosting, no embedded player, no thumbnail or preview, no download counter, **no «who watched» tracking** of any kind, no messaging or WhatsApp sending of the content, no student accounts or student logins, no reminder before a class, no calendar entry, no ordering by hand, no categories, no new dependency and no paid service. The make-up class and seat booking (E8) are separate.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. May a group's teacher publish content straight to guardians, with no manager's approval? (Yes, as built: same people as the exams, shown at once; a manager-only publish would be a one-line change to `content.set_published` and the default.)
  2. How long does an online class stay in the guardian's list after it starts? (One day, `content.ONLINE_GRACE`.)
  3. Should a closed (stopped) group's content stay visible to the guardians of its children? (No: the portal's groups card lists active groups only, and the content follows it.)
- **Tests:** `education/tests_content.py`: the link rules (every accepted and refused shape above, the exact length limit, English and Arabic messages, a row that breaks the rules is not drawn whatever put it in the table); the staff rules (a new row, audit without the address, bad input, longest title and note, an online class's time in the centre's own zone, a closed group, who may and may not, soft hide, show and remove, nothing moves); the staff screens (the card for a manager and the group's teacher only, today's link, a row's words, escaping, hide, show and remove from the screen, the form keeping what was typed, English); the guards (not an education install 404, another entity's group and row 404 before the 403, a person who may not 403 on every screen, a teacher with no student-file permission, POST only, CSRF, unknown id); the portal (only published and active items of the child's active groups, a stopped enrolment, a closed group, another entity, the one-day rule at its exact boundary, the words and buttons per kind, every link with `rel="noopener noreferrer"` and `target="_blank"`, escaping, nothing before the factor, English, two writes and one content read per visit, nothing else added); the table (columns, index, the migration on a database that already holds a group, `makemigrations --check`). Also run: `education` in full on SQLite and PostgreSQL, `reports.tests_route_auth`, `reports.tests_crawl_matrix`, `reports.tests_customer_voice`, `settings_core.tests_table_cards`, `settings_core.tests_tokens_adoption`, `config.tests_referrer_policy`.


## HG-105 — E8 makeup classes and seat booking: a student attends one class of another group of the same course (`education.MakeupSeat`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/education-e8-makeup-seats`, **stacked on E9** (`claude/education-e9-group-content`, HG-104: the migration is `0012` after E9's `0011`), awaiting Ahmed's review. **T2**: one new table, one rules module, one booking page and two small cards, a guest list on the register, one branch in the card scan, and one extra block on the guardian portal. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «4. تعليمي», item 19 «حصة تعويضية وحجز مقعد», PR E8.
- **What it changes in money terms, in plain words:** **nothing, and that is the hard line of the item.** A makeup seat is **not** attendance: it writes no `Attendance` row and no `ClassSession`, it makes no fee charge, no invoice and no teacher payment, and nothing in `billing.py`, `pay.py` or `insights.py` reads the table (none of the three files is edited; a test reads their source and finds neither «MakeupSeat» nor «makeup»). Booking, cancelling, marking a guest attended or missed, scanning a guest's card and a guardian reading the list write no sales invoice, payment, cashbox movement, ledger row, journal entry, stock movement or outbox row (counts are pinned around the whole run).
- **What the table is:** `education.MakeupSeat`, one row for one student in one class of one group on one day, in place of a class of their own group: `student` (PROTECT), `home_group` (PROTECT: the group they are enrolled in and missed), `missed_on` (optional date: the home class day being made up), `group` (PROTECT: the group whose class they attend, the **target**), `day` (the target's class day, indexed), `status` (`booked`, `attended`, `missed`, `cancelled`), `note` (optional, up to 255), `created_by`, `created_at`, `updated_at`. Two database objects: the index `edu_makeup_group_day_idx` on `(group, day)` (the capacity count, the register's guest list and the portal read by it) and the **partial unique constraint** `education_one_live_makeup_seat_per_class` on `(student, group, day)` where `status` is `booked`, `attended` or `missed`: one student cannot hold two live seats in the same class, and a cancelled seat frees its slot (a test creates two cancelled rows for the same slot and proves a booked, an attended and a missed row each refuse a second live one, on SQLite and on PostgreSQL). **Its entity is the target group's**, as an `Exam`'s and a `GroupContent`'s are: every read goes through `group__in=services.scoped(StudyGroup.objects)`, there is no entity column that could drift, and the rules require the student, the home group and the target to be in one entity. Nothing is hard-deleted: «cancelled» is the soft state.
- **Protected items touched:** the migration only: `education/0012_makeup_seats` (one `CreateModel`, after `0011_group_content`; no existing table, column, row or migration is read or changed; a test runs it on a database that already holds a group and a student). **No existing model, no permission core, no new permission, no seeded role, no setting.** `education/billing.py`, `education/pay.py` and `education/insights.py` are not edited. `education/attendance.py` is not edited (`take`, `sheet_version`, `can_take` and `lock_group` are used as they are). `education/scan.py` gains one branch (below); `education/portal.py` gains one read (below).
- **Why a table, and why no existing one serves:** a seat has to be counted against a room, listed on another group's day, cancelled and shown to a guardian, without being a mark. `Attendance` is a mark in the group's own class and is exactly what per-class fees bill and `scan.touched` reads; `ClassSession` is a held class and is what a teacher paid per class is counted on; `Enrollment` would make the student a member of the other group (billed, on its roster, in its capacity count for good). Nothing else holds «this student, in that group's class on that day, in place of that one».
- **The rules (`education/makeup.py`, the service layer; the views are thin):**
  1. **Booking** (`makeup.book`, one transaction): the student has an **active enrolment in `home_group`** and is active; `group` is a **different, running group of the same course** in the same entity; the student is **not already actively enrolled in `group`** (they would be counted twice); `day` is **not in the past** (today is allowed) and is a day the group really meets (`attendance.meets_on`: its weekdays and its dates); **a class cancelled on the register cannot be booked**; `missed_on`, when given, is one of the home group's class days; the note is at most 255 characters; the student has no live seat in that class already; and the room is not full. Each refusal is its own plain message, in Arabic and English. The booking form lists the classes of the next **14 days: today and the 13 after it**.
  2. **Capacity.** A group with `capacity` 0 is unlimited. Otherwise `free = capacity − active enrolments − live seats that day` (live = booked, attended or missed: only a cancelled seat frees its place; a guest marked «missed» still held the room for that class). One function, `makeup.free_seats`, gives the number to the booking form («فاضل 3 مقاعد» / «مكتمل», the full ones disabled) and to the booking itself, so what the page shows is what is enforced. The booking **locks the target group's row first** (`attendance.lock_group`, the lock `attendance.take` and a scan hold) and counts and inserts under it; cancel and mark take the same lock before the seat's own row, so two writers never wait on each other in opposite orders. The partial unique constraint is the backstop under the lock (an `IntegrityError` becomes the «already booked» message, inside its own savepoint).
  3. **Cancelling** frees the place: a **booked** seat becomes `cancelled`; cancelling a cancelled seat again is not an error and writes nothing; a seat already marked attended or missed cannot be cancelled (it keeps its row). **Marking**: a guest is marked `attended` or `missed`, and either can be corrected to the other; a cancelled seat, a class still to come and a class cancelled on the register are refused; marking the same status again writes nothing.
  4. **Audit:** every change writes one `AuditLog` row, `module="education"`, `object_type="education.MakeupSeat"`, with the seat's student, groups, day, status and note before and after: `book_makeup_seat`, `cancel_makeup_seat`, `attend_makeup_seat`, `miss_makeup_seat`, `scan_makeup_seat`. **None of them is `scan_attendance` on a `ClassSession`**: that pair is what `scan.touched` reads to flip a register into its «who was not scanned counts as absent» mode, which would change per-class billing when the teacher closes the class (a test pins that a guest's scan leaves `touched` empty and the register's digest unchanged).
  5. **Who** (`attendance.can_take`; **no new permission**): booking and cancelling need whoever may take attendance for the **home group or the target group**; marking the guest, and the guest list, need whoever may take the **target** group's attendance. A manager (`master_data.manage_parties`) for any group; a teacher (a user linked to an employee) for their own groups; nobody else. The booking page is offered to a person who could book some pair for this student (`makeup.can_use`).
- **Screens (guard order as the exam and content screens: not an education install 404, then the record in the entity being worked in 404, then the permission 403; every change POST only, CSRF protected, and the redirect built from the seat, never from the request):**
  - **The student's page:** a card «حصص تعويضية» (day and time joined by «، », the group, «من مجموعة …», the status, the note, «إلغاء الحجز» for a booked seat) and «احجز حصة تعويضية».
  - **The booking page** (`/education/students/<id>/makeup/`): choose the home group (chosen for you when there is one) and optionally the missed day; then radio rows of the other groups' classes in the next 14 days, each with its free seats, a full class disabled with «مكتمل», one the student already holds disabled with «محجوزة بالفعل»; an optional note; one button.
  - **The register and today's classes:** a section «ضيوف الحصة (تعويضي)» listing the live guests of that class on that day (name, «من مجموعة …», the note) with «حضر» and «ماحضرش». It sits **outside** the attendance form (no nested forms; a guest's button can never submit the register), is absent when the class has no guest, shows no buttons when the class was cancelled, and is shown only to whoever may take that group's attendance. **The guest is not a roster row and not a mark, and the register saves exactly as before**: the sheet's digest and its save do not change with a booking (tests).
  - **The card scan (`scan.mark`):** the answer for a student with a **booked (or marked) seat in a group the scanner may take today** that the old rules would have refused or not offered is now ok with the chip **«حصة تعويضية»** and the seat attended; a rescan says «again»; a seat marked «missed» is corrected to attended. It is a branch only: a student with a class of their own is marked there as before, with two classes in the window the scan asks which (the seat's group is one of the choices), a student with no seat gets the old refusal word for word, a cancelled class answers «cancelled». **The branch writes the seat and its own audit row and nothing else**: no `Attendance` row and **no `ClassSession`** (a scan of a guest before anyone else in a class would otherwise create a held class, which per-class teacher pay counts). The scan page's script and stylesheet are unchanged (it draws the chip from the label the server sends).
- **What the portal shows, and to whom:** in `education.portal.content()`, after the second factor, each child's block gains «حصص تعويضية» **when there is something to show**: that child's **booked** seats from today onward (in a running group) and their **attended or missed** seats of the last 30 days, in the link's entity: day and time (joined by «، »), the target group's name, and the status in nouns (a boy and a girl read the same line): «حصة محجوزة», «حضور», «غياب». A coming seat whose class was cancelled on the register says «الحصة اتلغت. تواصل مع المركز.». Never a cancelled seat, a seat older than 30 days, a past seat nobody marked, a note, the home group, another family's child, a sibling's seat under the wrong child, or another entity's. Nothing appears before the second factor. **Reads only:** the visit still writes exactly two rows (the link's `last_opened_at` and one audit row) and the seats cost **one read for all the children together** (a second only to see which coming classes were cancelled); a past booked seat is **never turned into «missed» by being looked at** (a test).
- **Proof that billing and teacher pay are untouched (the characterization tests were written first, in `education/tests_makeup.py`, class `MoneyLineTests`):** a per-class course at 50.00 with two groups, one paid **10% of its fees** and the other **80.00 for each class held** (the two paths that read the register), four students, three recorded classes (one student absent on one). The figures are taken before any seat: `billing.due_lines` and `preview` (150.00, 100.00, 150.00, 150.00 = 550.00), `pay.teacher_rows` (units and due for each group), `insights.group_report` (rows and totals), `attendance.student_summary` and `group_rates`, `billing.billed`, `billing.dues`, and the row counts of `Attendance`, `ClassSession`, `FeeCharge`, `SalesInvoice`, `CashboxMovement`, `JournalEntry`, `TeacherPayment`, `GroupPayTerm`, `OutboxMessage`, `Enrollment`, `Student` and `StudyGroup`. Then seats are booked, attended, missed, corrected and cancelled **on days where the target group has no class recorded** (so a class opened by mistake would show), and every figure is identical. The same is done again after `bill_month(post=True)` (the month billed: 550.00 on four invoices, one per payer; teacher A 10% of the 400.00 billed at home = 40.00, teacher B 3 classes at 80.00 = 240.00) and again after both teachers are paid. A **negative control** holds one real class in the target group and shows the same comparison notices it (teacher B's units 3 → 4, 240.00 → 320.00); another test scans guests first thing in a class and shows the pay figures do not move. No existing figure would have changed, so there is **no owner question about a figure that moves**; the questions below are about what a seat does not do.
- **Risks:**
  1. **A seat is not billed and not paid.** A student who attends a makeup class pays nothing for it, and the home group's per-class fee for the class they missed is not billed (they have no mark there); a teacher paid per class held gets nothing for hosting a guest and nothing is taken from the home teacher. For monthly and whole-course fees (most centres) this is exactly right; for per-class fees and per-head pay it is a policy question (below).
  2. **`services.enroll` checks capacity without counting seats.** An enrolment made after a booking can take the place a booked guest was promised, and the room is then over its capacity on that day. `services.py` is not edited by this item (protected area); the booking itself never overfills, and the next booking for that day is refused. Proposed follow-up: count the live seats of the group's coming classes in `enroll`, or refuse a new enrolment that would push a day over.
  3. **A class cancelled on the register after a booking leaves the seat «booked»** (`attendance.take` is not edited). The register shows the guest without buttons, the guardian's list says the class was cancelled, the seat cannot be marked, and a person who may cancel it can; but nothing cancels it by itself.
  4. **A past seat nobody marks stays «booked»** forever (nothing changes a seat by being looked at). It holds its place for that past day, which no longer matters, is not shown to the guardian and shows on the student's card as «محجوز».
  5. **Stopping the home enrolment later does not cancel the seat**; the seat stays for the class booked, which is attended or not as usual.
  6. **A teacher with no student-file permission** can mark guests from the register and today's classes (their own groups) but not open the student's page, so they can book only through someone who can; the booking page itself is not gated on `view_master_data`.
  7. **No waiting list:** a full class is simply disabled. The guest's card works at the door only when the seat is booked for that class and day (the 30-minute window before the start applies as for any scan).
  8. **Today is bookable at any hour:** `day` is compared with today's date, not with the class's start or end time, so the booking page also offers today's class after it ended (the guest can then only be marked, never scanned in outside the window). Not widened here; proposed follow-up: compare the class's end time when `day` is today.
  9. **Chips beside a child's name are nouns** («حضور», «غياب», «محجوز», «اتلغى») so they read the same for a boy and a girl; only the two register buttons use the verbs «حضر» and «ماحضرش», as the plan words them.
- **Not built (on purpose, and said so in the plan):** guardians booking online (guardians cannot book; the portal only lists); a fee, an invoice or a charge for a makeup class; a teacher's pay for hosting a guest; a waiting list; automatic suggestions of a class; WhatsApp or any other message about a booking; makeup seats across **different courses**; a seat for a group of another entity; a guest counted in the group's attendance rate, in a certificate's hours or in the dashboard's counts; a seat in a class on a past **day** (refused) — the rule compares days, not hours, so a class that already ended earlier **today** can still be booked (see risk 8); editing a seat (cancel and book again).
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. **Should a makeup class ever be billed?** (No, as built: a seat is free and bills nothing. A fee per makeup class would be a new rule in `billing.py`, which this item does not touch.)
  2. **Should it count for a teacher paid per head or per class held?** (No: the hosting teacher's per-class pay counts classes the register recorded as held, never guests, and a per-class fee counts the student's marks in their own group only. If the centre pays a teacher for a guest, that is a new rule in `pay.py`.)
  3. **May a student make up in a group of another course or another level of the same course?** (No: same course only, as the plan says; the level is not compared, so a level-1 student may take a level-2 group's class of the same course. Say if the level must match: one comparison in `makeup.book`.)
  4. Should a seat be refused when the home class was not missed yet and the student attended it? (No check: `missed_on` is optional and only has to be a class day of the home group.)
  5. May a guardian cancel a seat from the portal? (Not built; the portal only reads.)
- **Tests:** `education/tests_makeup.py` (a frozen Wednesday noon, every group meeting every day, no weekday or time-of-day dependence): the rules (a seat and its audit, who may book, **each refusal** with its message: same group, another course, another entity, not enrolled, stopped enrolment, inactive student, already in the target, closed group, a past day, a day the group does not meet, a cancelled class, a missed day the home group does not have, a bad date, a long note; the window is today and the 13 days after; one live seat per class and the database's own constraint, including a booked, an attended and a missed row each refusing a second live one while cancelled ones do not); capacity (the count, a cancel frees a place, a marked seat still holds it, a group with no capacity is unlimited, the form and the booking use one count, who sees which candidates); statuses (who marks, corrections, the audit trail, refusing a future class, a cancelled seat and a cancelled class, idempotent cancel, an attended seat cannot be cancelled, a seat never changes by being looked at); **the money line** (above, including the negative control and a scan-first run); **eight bookings at once** on a class with two free places never exceed it (real concurrent connections on PostgreSQL, where a second test also reads the statements and pins that the group row is locked before the insert; on SQLite the in-memory database cannot take concurrent writers («database table is locked») and has no row locks, so the same eight bookings run one after the other in the same test, which proves the count and not the race, and a cancel among bookings ends within the room); the screens (the student's card and its words, the date and time joined by a comma and never a dot, escaping, the booking page's options, free seats and disabled full class, one seat left, an unlimited group, the choice of home group, the booking by the form and its refusal back to the page, a missed day that cannot add parameters to the way back);
