the register (the section, outside the form and not a roster row, absent with no guest, the digest and save unchanged, the two buttons and the redirect, the target teacher marks and the home teacher cannot, a cancelled class shows no buttons, today's list, English, escaping); the guards (not an education install 404, another entity 404 before the 403, a person who may not 403 on every screen, the home teacher may cancel but not mark, POST only, CSRF, unknown id 404); the scan (the answer, no mark and no class and no `scan_attendance`, a rescan, a missed mark corrected, no seat or another day or a cancelled seat refused as before, only the groups the scanner may take, outside the window, a class of their own and a seat in whichever window holds the time, the «which class?» choice, a cancelled class, a saved register's digest unchanged, JSON in both languages); the portal (the list in its order and window, what is not shown, only that child's, a cancelled class, another entity, nothing before the factor, two writes and one read per visit, no seat or figure changed by a visit, English); the words (no banned word, no gendered line, no Arabic line that starts or ends with an English word, the two languages have the same keys, no date and time joined by a dot); the stylesheet (tokens only, 44 px targets) and the migration (after 0011, one `CreateModel`, on a database that already holds data). Also run: `education` in full on SQLite and PostgreSQL, `reports.tests_route_auth`, `reports.tests_crawl_matrix`, `reports.tests_customer_voice`, `settings_core.tests_table_cards`, `settings_core.tests_tokens_adoption`, `config.tests_referrer_policy`, and `makemigrations --check --dry-run`.
- **Review notes, 13 Nov 2026 (two findings of the automated review, both fixed with a failing test first):**
  1. `makeup._locked` and `makeup.attend_by_scan` locked the seat with a plain `select_for_update()` and `select_related`, which on PostgreSQL also locks the joined student and both groups. Two seats with opposite home and target groups (A to B, B to A) could then each hold their target group and wait for the other's home group, and PostgreSQL would abort one. Both now lock the seat row only (`of=("self",)`); the target group is already locked. `SeatLockTests` reads the SQL on PostgreSQL.
  2. A teacher who takes attendance but has no `master_data.view_master_data` was allowed to book and cancel, yet the only link to the booking page was on the student page (403 for them) and a booking or a cancellation redirected there. The register of a group now has «احجز حصة تعويضية لطالب من المجموعة» under the guests section, outside the register's own form (a list of the roster's names, each opening the booking page with that group as the home group), and the booking page's back link and the redirects go to the student page only for whoever may open it; otherwise to the home group's register, else to today's classes. No access to the student file is granted: the booking page shows the student's name and their groups, which that teacher already sees on the register. `TeacherPathTests`.

## HG-106 — Medical M6 hardening: the lab result link asks for the full birth date and counts its wrong answers in the database (closes HG-101's risk 8; new table `medical.LabLinkCounter`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/medical-lab-link-hardening` (cut from `develop` at `733cc3d`) on 13 Nov 2026, awaiting review by the supervising session and Ahmed. **T2**: a public page that shows patient data. The only protected item is the migration `medical/0016_lab_link_counter` (one `CreateModel`: a new table, no existing row, table, column or migration is read or changed; `makemigrations --check --dry-run` is clean). No posting, stock, cashbox, ledger, report, permission-core, closing or average-cost code is touched, and no existing model changes.
- **What and why:** the patient's lab result page (HG-085) asked for the **birth year** (a few dozen plausible values, and a year is often known to people close to the patient) and counted its **lifetime limit of 8 wrong answers in Django's default cache**. Production runs gunicorn with 2 or 3 workers and has no `CACHES` setting, so every worker counted on its own (a real limit of 16 to 24) and every deploy started the count again. Whoever held a forwarded link could get in by guessing. HG-101 fixed exactly these two weaknesses for the guardian portal and recorded that the lab page still had them (its risk 8). This closes that, with the same design.
- **The factor (change 1):**
  - A file **with a birth date**: the question is «تاريخ ميلاد المريض», answered in **three numeric boxes in one row, left to right in Arabic and English alike: day, month, year** (`inputmode="numeric"`, `autocomplete="off"`, labels «اليوم / الشهر / السنة» and Day / Month / Year, each box `--hs-control-height-lg` = 48 px tall, no native date picker). Arabic-Indic and Persian digits are read.
  - **Format error, not counted** (the same 400 question page with a format message): a date that is not a real calendar date (31 February, month 13, day 0, missing or non-numeric boxes) or a year outside 1900 to this year. **A real date that does not match is a wrong answer and is counted** (3 April is not 4 March). Typos therefore never use the patient's tries and never lock them out.
  - The comparison is `hmac.compare_digest` of the answer as an ISO string against the file's date. The reader is `education.portal.parse_date`, imported (not copied) so the two public pages cannot disagree about what a date is; `education/portal.py` is not edited and its behaviour is unchanged. The import is made inside the function, so the lab page depends on the guardian portal's module only when an answer is read.
  - A file **with no birth date** keeps the **phone's last 4 digits** exactly as before (the 7-digit minimum, Arabic digits, the staff warning «ملف المريض ده مفيهوش تاريخ ميلاد…»). A file with a date never accepts the phone's digits, and the old birth-year answer opens nothing.
  - **The page never shows the birth date or the phone**, before or after the answer, in any shape: the question page, the wrong, format and busy (429) pages carry no value in the boxes and never echo what was posted (checked for ISO, `04/03/1985`, `4/3/1985`, `1985`, `١٩٨٥`, the phone and its last 4 digits, with the random address and CSRF text taken out first); the sheet shows the age that day, as before, never the date.
  - Every word that said «سنة ميلاده / birth year» now says birth date: the question and its labels, the format message, the WhatsApp text (`wa_text_date`), the staff-side send note (`LAB_WORDS["send_note"]`), `no_factor`, and the module docstring. The audit rows carry no factor value (`send_lab_result_link` records patient, day, results count and days), so nothing changed there. `make_link` returns `kind: "date"` in place of `"year"`. Wording: «رابط», «حضرتك», no Arabic line starts or ends with an English word in the lines written here; `reports/tests_customer_voice.py` follows the renamed keys.
- **The counter (change 2):**
  - **Where it lives:** the lab link is a stateless signed token with no row, so one small table was added: `medical.LabLinkCounter` (`link_hash` unique, 64 hex; `wrong_answers` small integer; `created_at`; `updated_at`). `link_hash` is a **keyed** hash (`salted_hmac`, salt `hesba.medical.lab.life.v1`, SHA-256) of the token: never the token, and not the plain SHA-256 that the cache keys and the pass cookie already use. There is **no foreign key and no patient data** in it. A new link (a new token, «ابعت تاني») is a new fingerprint and starts at 0 (pinned).
  - **How it counts:** `public.take` takes the row's slot **before comparing**: `filter(link_hash=…, wrong_answers__lt=8).update(wrong_answers=F("wrong_answers") + 1, updated_at=now) == 1`; zero rows means the link is dead (the same dead page as any invalid link, byte for byte). A **right** answer gives its slot back with `filter(wrong_answers__gt=0).update(wrong_answers=F(…) - 1)`. A refused answer (a full 15-minute window or address hour) gives the row's slot back too, so only answers that were compared are charged and N answers at once never compare more than 8. `used_up` is an `exists()` on the row and never makes one.
  - **How the lazy row is race-safe:** the row is made by the link's first counted answer, never by making the link, a visit, a forged token or a format error. When the guarded UPDATE finds no row, `get_or_create(link_hash=…)` runs inside `transaction.atomic()`: the **unique constraint** on `link_hash` lets only one INSERT win, the loser's INSERT fails inside `get_or_create`'s own savepoint and it reads the winner's row, and then the same guarded UPDATE runs again, so two first answers at once make one row and lose no count. Proven by threaded tests on **PostgreSQL** (20 first answers at once: one row, exactly 8 `ok`; 20 at once with a roomy limit: all 20 in the count; 6 links with 2 answers each: 6 rows) and mutation-checked (a read-then-write counter fails three of them). On **SQLite** those threaded tests are skipped, and the test file says why: its shared in-memory test database answers a concurrent reader with «database table is locked». There the same properties are pinned by sequential tests (20 takes admit exactly 8 and the row says 8; the **lost race is replayed deterministically**: this request's lookup misses, the competing row is committed, this request's INSERT meets the unique constraint and falls back to that row, one row and the count 3 of 2 + 1) and by a test that the database enforces the unique fingerprint.
  - **Cost, honestly:** an answer writes the counter row and **nothing else** (pinned by a test that captures every write statement): the first answer of a link is `UPDATE` (no row), `INSERT`, `UPDATE`; a later wrong answer one `UPDATE`; a right answer two (take, give back). A visit and a format error write nothing; opening the sheet writes only its audit row, as before. No test in the tree pinned a number of write statements for this page.
  - **Survives a deploy:** the count is read from the row, so clearing the cache (a restart, another worker) changes nothing (pinned: the 8th wrong answer, a cleared cache, and the right answer still finds the dead page).
  - **Unchanged:** the short windows (5 wrong answers per 15 minutes per link, 50 per hour per address, 60 page loads a minute per address) stay in the cache, per process; the 30-minute pass cookie; the token, its fingerprint and expiry; the 8 itself.
- **What remains (stated plainly):**
  1. **The short windows are still per process** (and a restart clears them). They are the speed limit now, not the real one: the lifetime limit is exact and shared. With W workers a link can still take up to W × 5 wrong answers in a 15-minute window, but never more than 8 in its life.
  2. **The phone-only mode is weak if the number on file is wrong**: whoever receives the link at that number owns the 4 digits, and 4 digits are 10,000 values against 8 tries. The lab is told so on the screen and in the box, as before; it is advice, not a block. The strong fix is a birth date on the file.
  3. **A forwarded link plus a known birth date opens the result** until the link expires (`MEDICAL_LINK_DAYS`, default 7) or the result, the birth date or the phone changes. A full date is far harder to guess than a year but is known to the patient's family and often to anyone who has seen an ID. A one-time SMS code is still a later step.
  4. **Links already sent before this change keep working** (nothing in the token changed) **but now ask for the full birth date**, and their WhatsApp text still says «سنة ميلاد حضرتك». A patient who types only the year gets the format message.
  5. **Rows are not purged.** One small row (a hash, a count, two timestamps) per link that received at least one counted answer; it is not made without a genuine signed token, so it cannot be inflated by a stranger. A purge of rows older than the link's life is a later step.
  6. The format check reads each box by its digits only (the portal's reader), so `1/4` in the day box reads as 14, and a stray minus sign or dot is ignored; it never accepts a date that does not exist.
- **Not built:** a purge of old counter rows; a shared cache for the short windows; a message kind for an automatic send; an SMS code; a lifetime counter for the share links of other documents (`printing.share`).
- **Files:** `medical/models.py` (`LabLinkCounter`), `medical/migrations/0016_lab_link_counter.py` (new), `medical/public.py` (factor, `parse_date`, `link_hash`, `_life_take`, `_life_give_back`, `used_up`, `take`, `give_back`, words, docstring), `medical/views.py` (`send_note` words and its `tries` value), `templates/medical/lab_link.html` (the three boxes), `static/hesba/css/lab_link.css` (`?v=ll-002`), `medical/tests_lab_link.py`, `reports/tests_customer_voice.py` (the renamed keys), `docs/AGENT_HARD_GATES.md` (this entry, a dated note under HG-085 and one under HG-101's risk 8), `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md` (a dated note under the medical section's footnote ¹; no percentage changed).
- **Tests** (`medical/tests_lab_link.py`, 55; 4 of them run on PostgreSQL only): the date factor (the right date typed as `4/3/1985`, `04/03/1985`, padded with spaces, in Arabic-Indic and in Persian digits and mixed; the wrong dates, including day and month swapped; over twenty shapes of format error, none counted, none making a row; the first and last accepted years are formats, not matches); the three boxes' order, names, `inputmode`, `autocomplete`, labels, `maxlength`, patterns and the 44 px height from the tokens, and tokens-only CSS; the comparison in constant time on the ISO strings; nothing of the patient or the phone on any page, before or after, in any format; the same pages for every file with a date; the phone fallback unchanged (question, Arabic digits, never opens a file with a date, the staff warning); the words (no «سنة ميلاد», no «لينك», «حضرتك», no Latin at a line's edge in the new lines, both languages, the WhatsApp text, the staff note and its closing sentence); the counter row (fields, keyed hash, no row from a visit, a forged token or a format error; the limit survives a cleared cache; a right answer gives its slot back; a refused answer is not charged; a spent link gives the same dead page as a forged one and a new link starts at 0; the lost race; the unique constraint; the write statements); the PostgreSQL threads; and all the earlier HG-085 tests, adapted to the date. `reports.tests_customer_voice` follows the renamed keys.
- **Open questions for Ahmed (default built in brackets):**
  1. Should links already sent be killed, so nobody holds one that says «سنة ميلاد حضرتك»? (No: they keep working and ask for the full date; editing the file's date or phone kills them, and the lab can send again.)
  2. Should a spent link be shown to the lab on the results screen with a «ابعت رابط جديد» prompt? (Not built: the screen does not list sent links, since they are stateless; the send note says a link closes after 8 wrong answers and needs a new one.)

## HG-107 — Restaurants: «إلغاء التحضير», a kitchen preparation is cancelled whole from the dish's page, and the stock screen no longer reverses its operations one by one (closes HG-102's question 1; no schema)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/restaurant-cancel-preparation`, cut from `develop` (`1d4b6dc`), under Ahmed's delegation, **awaiting his review before merge**. T3: it changes from where a kitchen preparation's stock operations may be reversed, and adds a cancellation that moves stock and books journal entries (through `cancel_run`, unchanged). No table, no column, no migration (`makemigrations --check --dry-run`: «No changes detected»), no new permission, no new held action. Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «5. مطاعم وكافيهات»: it protects item 9 («خصم الخامات من المخزن بالوصفة», already present); a dated line, no percentage change.
- **The gap was proved first,** by tests written before any fix (`restaurant/tests_kitchen_cancel.py`, first commit, 26 tests marked as expected failures until the fix; 4 that already passed and must keep passing). `restaurant/kitchen.py` makes plates through `manufacturing.services.produce`, so a preparation is an ordinary `ProductionRun`. A restaurant has the manufacturing module off, so `settings_core.module_gate` closes the run's page (`/manufacturing/runs/<pk>/`, 403) and nothing cancelled a preparation whole; `manufacturing.guards.guarded` therefore let «التحويلات والتسويات» (`inventory.views.operation_cancel`) reverse each of its operations alone. Pinned on `develop` by `restaurant/tests_kitchen.py::KitchenRunOperationTests`: 3 plates at 11.00 (rice 0.200 kg at 30.00 + lentils 0.100 kg at 50.00), 2 sold, the recipe grows to 14.00 a plate (rice 0.300 kg), 3 more made: **4 plates worth 53.00, 13.25 each**; reversing the first preparation's output from the screen took 3 plates out at 11.00 and left **1 plate worth 20.00**, for plates that cost 11.00 and 14.00, with the first preparation still «posted».
- **What changes, with the figures** (the café kitchen of `tests_kitchen`: rice 20 kg at 30.00 = 600.00, lentils 10 kg at 50.00 = 500.00, and 10 cans of cola at 9.00 = 90.00 that nobody touches; the inventory control 1,190.00):
  1. **The dish's page lists its recent preparations** (`restaurant:dish`, view `restaurant.views.dish_ingredients`): the last 10, newest first, each with its number, date, plates, cost a plate (the output operation's unit cost) and who made it, in the entity being worked in (the run page's own scope, `entities.scope.PRODUCTION_RUN`). A posted one that `check_cancellable` accepts has «سبب الإلغاء» (required) and «إلغاء التحضير»; one it refuses has a muted line instead: «مينفعش يتلغي: في أطباق من «كشري كبير» خرجت من المخزن بعد التحضير ده (بيع أو تسوية أو تحويل). لو الرصيد محتاج تصحيح، اعمل تسوية مخزون عادية.»; a cancelled one shows «اتلغى» and its reason; one whose cancel is waiting for approval says so. English throughout.
  2. **A fresh preparation cancelled:** 5 plates at 11.00 (55.00); rice 19.000 kg / 570.00, lentils 9.500 / 475.00. **After:** plates 0 / 0.00; rice 20.000 / 600.00, lentils 10.000 / 500.00, averages 30.0000 and 50.0000 throughout; three reversal rows, each ingredient back at the unit cost it left at; the run «cancelled» with its reason; one `cancel_production` audit row; trial balance balanced, reconciliation clean, inventory control 1,190.00 and cost of sales 0.00 before and after.
  3. **The two-preparation example:** the first is **refused** with HG-100's message («مينفعش تلغي التشغيلة دي، عشان في كمية من «كشري كبير» خرجت من المخزن …») and nothing moves (4 plates, 53.00, 13.2500; rice 18.500 / 555.00, lentils 9.400 / 470.00; inventory control 1,168.00, cost of sales 22.00). The second **cancels**: 1 plate worth 11.00 at 11.0000, rice 19.400 / 582.00, lentils 9.700 / 485.00, inventory control 1,168.00, cost of sales 22.00 (the HG-100 shape: cancelling the later run leaves the earlier units at their own cost).
  4. **A preparation made at payment** (`prepare_shortfall`: 2 plates made on the spot and sold) is refused once sold, nothing moves; inventory 1,168.00, cost of sales 22.00.
  5. **The stock screen,** with the manufacturing module off and the dish's page able to open, refuses each of a preparation's operations (the plates and each ingredient) and sends the person to the dish's page: «الحركة دي تابعة لتحضير «كشري كبير» رقم MO-20261111-001، ومينفعش تتعكس من شاشة التحويلات والتسويات. ألغِ التحضير من صفحة الطبق، والأطباق بتخرج من المخزن وكل مكوّناتها بترجع مع بعض.» / "This operation belongs to the kitchen preparation MO-20261111-001 of “كشري كبير” and cannot be reversed from the transfers and adjustments screen. Cancel the preparation from the dish's page: its plates leave stock and all of their ingredients come back, together." The row shows «تابعة لتحضير «…» … وبيتلغي من صفحة الطبق.» with «افتح صفحة الطبق» / "Open the dish" (`/restaurant/costs/<dish>/`), and that page opens. In the example above the screen now leaves 4 plates, 53.00, 13.2500 untouched.
- **Where the check lives, and why there:**
  - **The cancel** is `restaurant.kitchen.cancel_preparation(run, user, reason, lang, dish=None)`: the two permissions `prepare` asks (`master_data.manage_items` and `inventory.adjust_stock`; the payment's stand-in `restaurant.prepare_at_payment` is never accepted), then the run found again in the entity being worked in as a preparation of that dish (recipe code `DISH-<dish pk>`, product the dish, the dish still on the menu), then `manufacturing.services.cancel_run` **unchanged**. Anything else is «التحضير ده مش من تحضيرات الطبق ده.» and nothing moves.
  - **The view** (`restaurant.views._cancel_preparation`) follows the run page's order (`manufacturing.views.run_detail`): who may (else a message, nothing moves); the run among this dish's preparations here (else **404**: another entity's run, another dish's, a run that is not a kitchen preparation, a missing or garbage id); `check_cancellable` first, so a cancel that would be refused is said at once and never sent for approval; then `approvals.gate.held` with **the same held action, `manufacturing.cancel_run`** («إلغاء تشغيلة إنتاج»), so an owner who holds run cancellations holds preparations too and a restaurant gets no way round a factory's approval; then the cancel. A GET moves nothing. The dish page itself still needs `inventory.view_cost`, as before.
  - **The approval** reuses the action unchanged except its link: `approvals.actions` gives `manufacturing.cancel_run` the url function `manufacturing.guards.run_page` instead of the fixed `/manufacturing/runs/<pk>/`. That url is also what `approvals.services._still_allowed` asks the module gate about on approval, so with the fixed path every approved restaurant request would have failed «approval_module_closed». `run_page` returns the run's page wherever the manufacturing pages are open (factories: unchanged, `tests_f11` still pins it), and the dish's page for a kitchen preparation where they are closed and the dish's page can open. On approval `_cancel_production_run` runs `cancel_run` in the requester's name, as for a factory. `approvals/services.py` and `approvals/gate.py` (the approvals core) are not edited.
  - **The stock screen's guard** (`manufacturing.guards.guarded`): where the manufacturing pages are open, exactly as before (the run or the order, linked to its page). Where they are closed, an operation of a run that is a kitchen preparation of a dish on the menu, with the restaurant pages open, gets an owner of kind `dish` (its refusal and link above). The kitchen knowledge stays in `restaurant/kitchen.py` (`preparation_dishes`, `dish_page`, `dish_pages_open`), imported lazily. The approvals path for a waiting stock reversal (`approvals.actions._reverse_stock_operation`) already asks the guard, so it follows the same rule: such a request now fails on approval with the dish message and nothing moves.
  - **Where neither page can open** (both modules off after use, e.g. a business that changed activity) the screen keeps reversing a preparation's operations one by one, as before, so nobody is sent to a closed page. The wrong average is possible there, as it was. The restaurant module (`tables_orders`) is required for the restaurant activity and cannot be switched off from settings, so this needs a change of activity.
- **What does not change:** `inventory/services.py` (the engine, `cancel_stock_operation`, costs, averages, posting, the projector), `manufacturing.services.cancel_run`, `check_cancellable`, `_left_since`, `produce`, `orders.cancel`, the run and order pages, sales posting, `prepare` and `prepare_shortfall`, the permission core and the seeded roles. A factory and a restaurant bakery with the module on behave byte for byte as under HG-102 (`manufacturing/tests_f11b.py` and `tests_f11.py` untouched and passing). An ordinary adjustment in a restaurant is still reversed from the screen (rice 18.600 / 558.00 → 19.600 / 588.00).
- **Protected items touched:** from where a kitchen preparation's stock operations may be reversed (the screen and the approval of a stock reversal), and a new caller of `cancel_run` (the dish's page). Not touched: the inventory engine and its movement and cost rules, average-cost logic, sales and purchase posting, the ledgers and the projector, the permission core, report calculations, closing, models and migrations.
- **Files:** `restaurant/kitchen.py` (the cancel and its helpers, messages), `restaurant/views.py` (the list, the cancel action, words), `templates/restaurant/dish_ingredients.html` (the list; stylesheet `?v=resto-hg107`), `static/hesba/css/restaurant.css` (two rules, tokens only; the `?v=` bumped in its four templates: `dish_ingredients.html`, `board.html`, `order.html`, `stations.html`), `manufacturing/guards.py` (`guarded`, `run_page`, the `dish` owner and its English message), `approvals/actions.py` (the action's url function), `settings_core/ui_messages.py` (the Arabic message), `templates/inventory/operations.html` (the row's dish branch), `restaurant/tests_kitchen_cancel.py` (new), `restaurant/tests_kitchen.py` (two tests, below), `docs/AGENT_HARD_GATES.md` (this entry and a dated note under HG-102), `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md` (one dated line).
- **Existing tests edited (two, one more than the brief allowed; both pinned the behaviour this closes):** in `restaurant/tests_kitchen.py::KitchenRunOperationTests`, (1) `test_with_manufacturing_off_the_wrong_average_is_still_possible` (the 1 plate at 20.00) is now `test_with_manufacturing_off_the_wrong_average_is_refused`: the screen refuses with the dish message and 4 / 53.00 / 13.2500 and the stock rows are unchanged; (2) `test_with_manufacturing_off_a_preparation_is_still_reversed_from_the_stock_screen` asserted that with the module off the screen lists no owner and reverses all three operations; that is exactly what now stops, so it became `test_with_neither_page_open_a_preparation_is_still_reversed_from_the_stock_screen` (the restaurant module flag switched off as well), which pins the kept fallback. The third test of the class (module on → the run's page) is untouched.
- **Tests:** `restaurant/tests_kitchen_cancel.py` (30), the clock held on a Wednesday at noon: the list (newest first, cap of 10, another dish's not listed, the fields, Arabic and English, a refused one's muted line, a cancelled one's reason, no button for who may not cancel); the money scenarios above with quantity, value and average of the plates and of every ingredient (and the untouched cola) as Decimals before and after, each ending with the trial balance balanced, the reconciliation clean and the inventory control and cost of sales to the piastre; the English refusal; the service alone; refusals with stock rows, operations, every position, the runs, journal entries and totals, the audit log and the approval requests compared before and after (no reason, blank reason, already cancelled, a GET, another dish's run, this dish's run on another dish's page, a plain run of the same product, a missing, non-numeric and empty id, the service refusing each); permissions (cashier and waiter: 403 on the page and refused by the service; cost view without stock adjustment: refused; the payment stand-in: refused; a cook with both permissions and the cost view cancels from the page; a stock keeper and a manager are accepted by the service and the page stays 403 to them for lack of the cost view; the owner); entity scope (from a branch the list is empty, the POST is a 404, the service refuses inside `working_in(branch)`, nothing moves); approvals (nothing held until ticked; held: nothing moves but the request and its audit row, asking twice asks once, the inbox links to the dish page and it opens, approved: done, in the requester's name, figures as above; a refusable cancel is said at once and not sent; plates sold while it waited: the approval fails and nothing moves; a waiting stock reversal of a preparation fails with the dish message); the stock screen (refused with the link in both languages for each of the three operations, the list shows the link and no reversal form, the link opens; the HG-102 example no longer possible; an ordinary adjustment still reversed; with the module on, still the run's page and the action's url is the run's page; neither page open: reversed as before). `restaurant/tests_kitchen.py::KitchenRunOperationTests` (3): above.
- **Risks and what is left open:**
  1. **At approval only `inventory.adjust_stock` is checked again** (the action's own permission list), not `master_data.manage_items`: a requester who lost «إدارة الأصناف» while the request waited still has it carried out. Same as a factory's run cancel; changing the action's permission list would change the factory's behaviour too.
  2. **Neither page open** (both modules off after use): the screen reverses one operation at a time, wrong average included, as before; see above.
  3. **A dish taken off the menu** (made inactive) is no longer a page: its preparations fall back to the screen's old behaviour where manufacturing is off, and the dish's page cannot cancel them. Switching it back on restores both.
  4. **A stock keeper or a manager** holds both permissions `prepare` asks but not the cost view that the dish's page asks, so they cannot reach the cancel (nor `prepare`, which the engine refuses without the cost view). Unchanged access rule; noted for the owner.
  5. The muted line on the dish's page uses the kitchen's own words; a crafted POST of the same refused preparation gets HG-100's run wording («التشغيلة») from `check_cancellable`, which is not edited.
  6. Preparations reversed from the screen before this change stay as they are (a run left «posted» without its plates). Nothing posted is rewritten.
- **Default decisions for the owner (built in brackets; change on request):**
  1. Which preparations are listed? (The dish's last 10 in the entity being worked in, newest first, cancelled ones included with their reason.) A preparation older than those is not out of reach: the stock screen's row and the approvals inbox link to `/restaurant/costs/<dish>/preparations/<run>/`, the same page with that preparation listed first (review finding, 14 Nov: eleven preparations with nothing sold and the newest ten cancelled left the first one cancellable but unlisted, while the screen refused its operations). A run that is not this dish's preparation in this entity is a 404 there.
  2. Who may cancel? (Whoever `prepare` allows: `master_data.manage_items` and `inventory.adjust_stock`, on a page that needs `inventory.view_cost`; never the payment's stand-in.)
  3. Does a held run cancellation also hold a preparation's cancel? (Yes: the same tick, «إلغاء تشغيلة إنتاج»; no new action. The approvals screen links to the dish's page.)
  4. Is a preparation whose plates were partly sold cancelled for the rest? (No: HG-100's rule, refused whole, with the advice to record an ordinary stock adjustment.)
  5. Where neither page can open, should the screen refuse too? (No: it reverses as before, so nobody is sent to a closed page.)
  6. Where does the person land after a refusal on the screen? (On the operations list with the message; the row links to the dish's page.)

## HG-108 — Restaurants R12: tables and orders belong to a branch, and a table's bill posts to its own branch's store and cashbox (`DiningTable.entity`, `Order.entity`; migration `restaurant/0005_branches`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/restaurants-r12-branches`, stacked on `claude/restaurant-cancel-preparation` (HG-107, PR #410), under Ahmed's delegation, **awaiting his review before merge**. T3: two new columns, a changed unique constraint, a data migration over existing rows, and a change to which store and cashbox a table's bill posts to and where the plates made at payment are made. No new permission, no new table. `makemigrations --check --dry-run`: «No changes detected». Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «5. مطاعم وكافيهات», item 13 «فروع كتير في لوحة واحدة» (جزئي → موجود; dated re-measure block).
- **The gap was proved first,** by tests written before any fix (`restaurant/tests_branches.py`, first commit: three passing tests that pinned today's behaviour, and the branch rules as expected failures). Two entities, the main one (hall «HALL», cashbox «TILL») and «فرع المعادي» (hall «BR-HALL», cashbox «BR-TILL»), each stocked with rice 0.200 kg / lentils 0.100 kg per plate of a large koshary made in the kitchen (11.00 a plate, sold at 60.00) and cans of cola at 9.00 (sold at 20.00); VAT off. On the code before R12:
  1. The owner in whole-group mode billed 2 koshary and a cola (**140.00**) choosing the branch's cashbox: the pay form offered **every** entity's cashbox, the invoice took the **main** hall (`selling_location` = HALL) with the branch's cashbox, the 2 plates were produced in HALL (rice there 20.000 → **19.600**, the branch's rice untouched at 10.000), and the books split: sales **−140.00 in the main entity, 0.00 in the branch**, while the 140.00 cash sat in the branch's till.
  2. A manager who belongs to the branch only saw the main hall's open order on the board and opened its page (200).
  3. The branch could not have a table «1» at all: the form said «فيه طاولة بنفس الاسم» and the database refused (`name` was unique across the installation).
- **What changes:**
  1. **Columns** (`restaurant/0005_branches`): `DiningTable.entity` and `Order.entity`, foreign keys to `entities.Entity` (PROTECT), **NOT NULL**. The unique constraint on the table's `name` becomes `restaurant_table_name_per_entity` on (`entity`, `name`), so every branch has its own «T1» and **the database itself** refuses a second «T1» in one branch (an `IntegrityError` from a raw create, tested). `DiningTable.save()` fills the main entity when none is given, as `master_data.Location.save` does; `Order.save()` fills the table's entity, else the main one (the services always set it).
  2. **Why NOT NULL, unlike the older entity columns** (HG-034's "an empty entity means the main one", kept by locations, cashboxes and kitchen stations): SQLite and PostgreSQL treat NULLs as different in a unique constraint, so with NULL rows two main-entity «T1» would pass the database (two NULL rows, or one NULL and one main). A NOT NULL column plus the fill makes the constraint airtight. `entities.scope` still works unchanged (its "or NULL for the main entity" branch simply never matches).
  3. **An order's branch** is set when it is opened (`services.open_order(..., entity=None)`): a dine-in order takes its **table's** entity; a takeaway or a delivery takes the `entity` given, else the entity being worked in, else the main one. Neither may land outside the entity being worked in («الطاولة دي تبع فرع تاني.» / «الفرع ده مش الفرع اللي شغال فيه دلوقتي.»). It never changes: `move_table` refuses another branch's table («الطاولة دي تبع فرع تاني.»).
  4. **Where a bill posts** (`services._payable`, the R13 seam): the store is **the order's entity's** default selling store (`restaurant.branches.selling_location`, read under `entities.current.working_in(order.entity)`, the M7 pattern of `medical.inpatient._selling_location`), whatever entity the biller works in, and the cashbox must be one of **the order's entity's** («الخزنة دي تبع فرع تاني؛ اختار خزنة من خزن فرع الطلب.» from the service; the screen's crafted-cashbox answer stays «لازم يكون فيه مخزن بيع نشط.» as `tests_shift_void` pins). `kitchen.prepare_shortfall` already takes the store it is given, so the plates made at payment are made in the order's branch's store. `sales.pos.checkout` and everything after it are called unchanged.
  5. **Scope:** the board, the order page (GET and every POST action), the kitchen ticket, the tables setup (list, `?edit=`, POST) see only the entity being worked in (`restaurant.branches`, through `entities.scope`); another branch's order, ticket or table answers **404**, exactly as one that does not exist. A form naming another branch's table (open, move) is treated as naming none. The pay form lists only the order's branch's cashboxes; the cashier's shift cashbox is preselected only when it belongs to that branch, else the branch's default. The restaurant report was already scoped through the invoice's store (HG-034) and now agrees with the order's branch for every new bill (tested: the branch's bill is in the branch's report, 1 order / 140.00, and not in the main one's). The dashboard's floor tiles (`reports.activity_panel._kitchen`: open orders, their value, busy tables) now count the entity being worked in (main: 1 order, 15.00, «1 of 3»; branch: 1, 30.00, «1 of 1»).
  6. **Whole-group mode** (owner, several entities): the board is grouped by branch (a heading per branch, the main one first, each with its tables; a branch with none says «لسه مفيش طاولات في الفرع ده.»); the open takeaway and delivery list gets a «الفرع» column; the takeaway and delivery forms ask for the branch (required; «اختار الفرع.» and nothing is opened when it is missing); opening a table opens it in that table's branch; the tables page gets a «الفرع» column and a branch picker (required for a new table; on an edit it may move a **free** table to another branch: refused with «الطاولة دي عليها طلبات مفتوحة؛ اقفلها الأول.» while an order is open on it). The order page names its branch beside the kind. English throughout.
  7. **A single-branch restaurant looks as before:** every heading, column and picker is gated on `is_multi_entity()`; tested, no `data-branch-group` and no `name="entity"` on the board or the tables page, and a takeaway lands in the main entity.
  8. **Kitchen:** a send is routed to **the order's** entity's stations (`stations.route(lines, entity=order.entity)`), whoever sends it and from wherever; the kitchen screen, its feed and its taps are scoped by the ticket's **order's** entity (`order__entity`) instead of the station's. So a branch without stations of its own now sees its stationless tickets on its own screen (the gap R4's note left "until R12"), and a whole-group send of a branch's order no longer goes to the main entity's stations. The dish page's preparations list (HG-107) stays scoped by the production run's store, which for a plate made at payment is now the order's branch's.
- **The migration and existing rows** (one file): add both columns nullable → `fill_entities` → both NOT NULL → `name` loses `unique=True` → the (`entity`, `name`) constraint. The fill: every table → the main entity (nothing recorded where a table stood); a **paid order with an invoice → the entity of its invoice's selling store** (where its sale, stock and books already are, as `appointments/0002` did; the main one when that store has none); every other order → the main entity. If rows exist and no main entity does, it is made as `entities/0001` seeds it. On PostgreSQL the fill first runs `SET CONSTRAINTS ALL IMMEDIATE`, so the ALTERs that follow in the same migration do not meet "pending trigger events". It is sent through the connection's cursor and only when there are rows to fill, because the demo restart (`config.demo_restart.replay_seed_migrations`) replays every data step with a stand-in editor that carries only `connection`: the first CI run on PostgreSQL failed every demo restart with "'SeedEditor' object has no attribute 'execute'" (the agent had run those tests on SQLite only); fixed, with `DemoRestartFillTests` beside the migration tests. Proved with Django's migration executor (`MigrationTests`, both SQLite and PostgreSQL): from `0004` with two tables, a paid order billed in a branch's store (150.00), a paid takeaway (75.50), an open dine-in order and a cancelled delivery, each with a line of 2.500 × 30.20: after `0005` no table and no order has a NULL entity, the tables and the three unbilled or main-billed orders are the main entity's, the branch-billed one the branch's, the invoices' totals and the lines' quantities and prices are unchanged, a second «1» in the main entity is refused and a «1» in the branch is allowed; the same with the main entity missing beforehand. The reverse is a no-op fill (going back to `0004` works only while no two tables share a name).
- **What does not change:** `sales/pos.py` (`checkout`), sales posting, VAT, the inventory engine and costs, `manufacturing.services.produce`, the cashbox engine, the customer ledger, the projector and the journal rules, `entities` (scope, current, services), the permission core and seeded roles, the shift gate (R13), `insights.period_report` and every report calculation. A bill of a main-hall table posts exactly as before (`MainHallTests`: 140.00 into HALL and TILL, change 60.00 from 200.00, plates made in HALL), and `restaurant/tests_pay_characterization.py` passes **unedited**, every pinned row included.
- **Protected items touched:** models/schema/migrations (two restaurant columns, a changed unique constraint, a data fill); which selling store and which cashboxes a table's bill may post to, and where plates made at payment are produced (the restaurant's own `pay()` choice of arguments, not the engines). A count filter on the dashboard's floor tiles. Not touched: sales and purchase posting, inventory movement and costing, average cost, cashbox movement, customer and supplier ledgers, the permission core, report calculations, closing.
- **Files:** `restaurant/models.py`, `restaurant/migrations/0005_branches.py` (new), `restaurant/branches.py` (new: the rules stated once), `restaurant/services.py`, `restaurant/views.py`, `restaurant/stations.py`, `restaurant/views_kds.py`, `reports/activity_panel.py`, `templates/restaurant/board.html`, `templates/restaurant/_table_card.html` (new: the table card, moved out of the board unchanged so both layouts share it), `templates/restaurant/order.html`, `templates/restaurant/tables.html`, `restaurant/tests_branches.py` (new), `restaurant/tests_kds.py` (below), `docs/AGENT_HARD_GATES.md` (this entry), `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md` (the re-measure block). No stylesheet was edited (existing classes only), so no `?v=` changed.
- **Existing tests edited (one):** `restaurant/tests_kds.py::EntityTests.setUp` opened the branch's order on the hall's table «2» while working in the branch. Under R12 a dine-in order belongs to its table's branch and opening another branch's table is refused, so the branch's order now opens on the branch's own table «1» made in the same `setUp`; the class docstring ("orders carry no entity yet") is updated. Every assertion of the class is unchanged and passes.
- **Tests:** `restaurant/tests_branches.py` (23), the clock held on a Wednesday at noon in Cairo (the two migration tests write their old rows with that fixed date): each branch's «T1», the database refusing a second one in one branch (also one made without an entity, which is the main one's) and the form case-insensitively; a free table moved to the branch, refused while an order is open; the tables page listing and editing its own branch only (404 on another's `?edit=` and POST, a crafted branch ignored for a branch manager) and asking for the branch in whole-group mode; an order's branch from its table or the working entity, refused across branches (open and move); a takeaway and a delivery asking for the branch in whole-group mode; a crafted open on another branch's table or branch opening nothing; **the owner in whole-group mode billing the branch's «T1»**: the pay form lists only `BR-TILL`, the invoice is 140.00 on `BR-HALL` and `BR-TILL`, one cash movement of 140.00 into `BR-TILL`, one production run of 2.000 plates in `BR-HALL`; branch stock rice 9.600, lentils 4.800, cola 4.000, plates 0.000; main hall rice 20.000, lentils 10.000, cola 10.000; the invoice's journal lines all the branch's; sales −140.00 and cost of sales 31.00 (2 × 11.00 + 9.00) in the branch, 0.00 and 0.00 in the main entity, the main entity's trial balance identical before and after, the branch's balanced; a branch manager billing their own table (tea 2 × 15.00 + cola = 50.00 into `BR-HALL` and `BR-TILL`); a crafted pay of the branch's bill into the main till refused from the screen and from the service with nothing moved (invoices, cash, stock, runs, orders); the report per branch; a branch-A manager getting 404 on the branch's order, its ticket and every POST action, with nothing changed; separate boards; the whole-group board grouped by branch (Arabic and English); each kitchen screen with its own branch's tickets and a tap on another's refused; a send routed to the order's branch's station; the dashboard tiles; a single-branch restaurant as before; the migration (two executor tests).
- **Risks and what is left open:**
  1. **Existing multi-branch installs:** every existing table lands in the main entity. The owner moves the branch's free tables from the tables page in whole-group mode (one edit each); until then a branch's staff do not see them.
  2. **Paid orders billed in the main hall before R12 stay the main entity's**, even if the table stood in a branch: their invoice, stock and books are there, and the fill follows the books. Open orders at the time of the migration are the main entity's.
  3. **The shift:** the cashier's open shift is per person, not per branch (R13's gate unchanged). An owner whose shift is on the main till who takes a branch bill into the branch's till is not refused; that cash is not in his shift's expected drawer, the same as choosing another cashbox at the till today (R13's footnote).
  4. **The dish page** (prepare ahead, «جاهز في المخزن») still uses the working entity's selling store, so in whole-group mode it prepares in the main hall, as before. A branch's kitchen prepares ahead while working in its branch.
  5. **Case:** the form refuses «t1» beside «T1» in one branch; the database constraint is exact, so a raw write could still make both.
  6. **Seen while testing, not changed (outside R12):** the seeded manager role holds «تسوية المخزون» but not the cost view, so making a plate at payment answers 403 for a manager (`inventory.services.adjust_stock` asks `inventory.view_cost` to set the plate's cost). R12 does not touch that path (`prepare_shortfall` → `produce` → `adjust_stock`); the manager's test bills tea and a can.
  7. **Restoring a backup made before R12:** `backup_data` says «migrate && loaddata». `loaddata` does not call `save()`, so a pre-R12 dump loaded onto the R12 schema fails on the NOT NULL columns. Restore it onto `restaurant/0004`, then migrate forward (the fill does the rest). The same holds for any later NOT NULL column.
- **Default decisions for the owner (built in brackets; change on request):**
  1. Which entity do existing tables and orders get? (Tables: the main one. Paid orders: their invoice's store's entity. Others: the main one.)
  2. Can a table change branch? (Yes, from the whole group, only while no order is open on it; its past orders keep theirs.)
  3. In whole-group mode, a takeaway or a delivery with no branch picked? (Refused: «اختار الفرع.»; there is no default branch.)
  4. Can an order move to another branch's table? (No.)
  5. Which cashboxes may a branch's bill be paid into? (Only that branch's, active, never a cheque or courier box; the cashier's shift cashbox preselected only when it is that branch's.)
  6. Does the whole-group board show every branch? (Yes, every active branch the person may work in, main first, each with its tables, even an empty one.)
  7. Does the dashboard's floor count follow the working entity? (Yes.)
  8. Does a branch's kitchen see a ticket of its order with no station of its own? (Yes, on its own screen; never on another branch's.)

## HG-109 — Restaurants R1a: add-ons and required choices on a dish; a priced add-on is its own order line under its dish and an ordinary invoice row after it (`ModifierGroup`, `ModifierOption`, `MenuItemModifierGroup`, `OrderLine.parent`, `OrderLine.options`; migration `restaurant/0006_modifiers`)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/restaurants-r1a-modifiers`, stacked on `claude/restaurants-r12-branches` (HG-108, PR #411) and `claude/restaurant-cancel-preparation` (HG-107, PR #410), under Ahmed's delegation, **awaiting his review before merge**. T3: three new tables, two new columns on `OrderLine`, new rows on the table bill (each priced add-on is a sales line with its own VAT, stock and e-receipt item), and the restaurant report's dish table counting dishes only. No new permission. `makemigrations --check --dry-run`: «No changes detected». Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, «5. مطاعم وكافيهات», item 14 «إضافات وأحجام للصنف وكومبو» (**stays جزئي**: combos are R1b; dated note, 67.3% unchanged).
- **Tests first** (`restaurant/tests_modifiers.py`, first commit, before any model existed). A burger at 120.00 (a service) asks «درجة الاستواء» (min 1, max 1: «ميديم», «ويل دن», both free) and offers «إضافات» (min 0, max 2: «جبنة زيادة», a priced add-on that is the item «جبنة زيادة» at 15.00, and «من غير بصل», free). The clock stands still on Wednesday 11 Nov 2026 at noon in Cairo, as in `tests_branches`.
- **What changes:**
  1. **Set-up** (`restaurant/modifiers.py`, the rules stated once): a group has Arabic and English names, `min_choices` (0 = optional), `max_choices` (1 to 20, never below the minimum; also a database `CheckConstraint`), an order and an active flag; an option has names, an optional `item` (PROTECT) and an active flag. A dish is linked to groups in an order (`MenuItemModifierGroup`, unique per dish and group). The page «الإضافات والاختيارات» (`/restaurant/modifiers/`, `views_modifiers.setup`) lists every group with its rule, its options (priced «+15.00» or «مجاني») and the dishes that ask it, and creates or edits a group with its option rows. An option is never deleted: an emptied row or an unticked «متاح» turns it off. A priced option left without a name takes its item's name. Refused, with nothing saved: no name, a name taken, minimum above maximum or maximum outside 1–20, no active option, a minimum above the active options, the same name or item twice, an unknown or inactive item, another group's option id. The dish page gets a box «الإضافات والاختيارات» (its own form, `action=groups`, routed **before** the ingredients' save, which deletes and rebuilds the recipe: tested, the ingredients and the plate cost stay).
  2. **Adding a dish** (`services.add_item(..., options=[...])`, checked by `modifiers.choose` before anything is written): each group's minimum must be met and its maximum respected; a group with no active option asks nothing, and a minimum is never more than the active options. The dish line carries its free choices as text in `options` («ميديم · من غير بصل»); each priced add-on becomes its own line with `parent` = the dish, the dish's quantity, the add-on item's `default_sale_price` **at that moment** (a later price change does not touch it), and the option's name in `options`. **2 burgers with cheese → lines 240.00 and 30.00, order total 270.00.** A dish with no groups, or with optional groups and nothing chosen, is added in one tap and merges with an identical **plain** unsent line exactly as before (the merge now also requires no parent, no options and no add-ons, so a plain «جبنة زيادة» never merges into someone's add-on).
  3. **The family moves together:** changing the dish's quantity changes its add-ons' (2 → 3: 405.00); quantity 0 or removing an unsent dish removes them (CASCADE); voiding a dish the kitchen has follows R13 (selling right and a reason; a waiter gets 403) and voids its add-ons with the same reason, in one audit row that lists them. An add-on is refused on its own («الإضافة بتتغيّر مع طبقها؛ عدّل الطبق نفسه.») for change, remove and void, from the service and from the screen, before the permission check, with nothing changed. There is no line move or re-price path in the restaurant, so «never moved or re-priced on its own» holds because none exists.
  4. **The kitchen:** `send_to_kitchen` routes **dishes only** (`stations.route`) and puts each add-on on its dish's ticket, so a cheese in the bar's category still goes to the grill with its burger (tested: one ticket, the grill's). The printed ticket prints the dish's choices and «+ جبنة زيادة» indented under it, never as a dish line. The kitchen screen's feed folds them into the dish's entry as `extras` (the key is present only when a dish has any, so a plain dish's entry is exactly as before and `tests_kds` is unedited); tapping a dish marks its add-ons ready with it; a voided dish's add-ons are «seen» with it, so the card leaves the screen; a tap on an add-on's id changes nothing. The board's ready count counts dishes only.
  5. **The bill** (the R13 seam `services.bill_rows`, nothing else): rows in family order (each dish, then its add-ons, even when lines interleave). A dish's row is as before, its description its note; an add-on's row is an ordinary row of its item with the description «+ جبنة زيادة (برجر)». `sales.pos.checkout` posts it unchanged. **VAT on:** subtotal 270.00, VAT 33.60 + 4.20 = **37.80**, total **307.80**, one cash movement of 307.80 into TILL, no customer-ledger row (walk-in, paid in full), journal 1101 Dr 307.80 / 2103 Cr 37.80 / 4101 Cr 270.00. **VAT off:** total **270.00**, cash 270.00, journal 1101 Dr 270.00 / 4101 Cr 270.00. A free choice costs 0.00 and adds no row (a well-done burger without onions: one row, 120.00 + 16.80 = 136.80). A voided dish and its add-on never reach the bill. **E-receipt:** one item per invoice row, «برجر» 2 × 240.00 and «+ جبنة زيادة (برجر)» 2 × 30.00.
  6. **Stock:** a kitchen-made add-on is produced at payment by the **unchanged** `kitchen.prepare_shortfall` (it reads every live line): cheese made from 0.030 kg of cheddar a portion, 2 burgers with cheese → cheddar 5.000 → **4.940** (adjustment out 0.060), cheese in 2.000 and sold 2.000 at 6.00 a portion (cost of the cheese row 12.00). `prepare_shortfall` was not edited.
  7. **The restaurant report** (`insights.period_report`, the dish table only): a dish's quantity counts its dish lines only; an add-on's money is added to its dish's row, so the table's money is the same as before R1a and an add-on never appears as a dish (2 burgers with cheese: «برجر» 2.000, 270.00; totals 1 order, 270.00, unchanged).
  8. **The kitchen switch** (`kitchen._copy_as_stock_item`, RESTO-004): a dish that already sold is copied as a new stock item; it now takes its old item's groups, and an add-on option pointing at an old item points at the new one (`modifiers.move_dish`), as its ingredients already did. Without this a burger switched to the kitchen would stop asking its questions, and a switched cheese would be refused as inactive.
  9. **Screens:** the order page's menu tile of a dish that asks opens a choice sheet (`<dialog>`, a bottom sheet on a phone, centred from 720 px): one fieldset per group with its rule («اختيار واحد · لازم», «لحد 2 · اختياري»), radios for a required single choice (one name per group, `required`), checkboxes otherwise (the rest are disabled once the maximum is reached), «+15.00» or «مجاني» beside each, quantity and kitchen note, «ضيف للطلب». A missing minimum is said in the group before sending; the server checks again. Without JavaScript the tile adds the dish plainly and the server's refusal says what is missing. The order's lines show the dish's choices and «+ جبنة زيادة 30.00» under it. 390 px measured in Chrome: page and sheet `scrollWidth` 390, every choice, the close and the add button 44 px high, focus moves to the first choice and back to the tile on close. A board link «الإضافات والاختيارات» for `manage_items`. Arabic and English.
  10. **Sizes (step 5, no schema):** a `variants.VariantGroup` with two or more active sizes on the menu is one tile («لاتيه · من 45.00 · 2 × الحجم») whose sheet asks the size (radios named `item`, so the chosen size is the line's item, as before). A family whose size asks questions of its own keeps a tile per size.
  11. **Demo** (`restaurant/demo.seed_modifiers`, from the end of `prepare_demo._floor`, after every seeded order, so **no seeded figure moves**): a service item «جبنة زيادة» (`DEMO-ADDON-01`, 15.00), the group «إضافات» (0–2: that cheese and «من غير بصل») on the first two dishes, and one required choice on one dish (the restaurant: «درجة التسوية» «ميديم»/«ويل دن» on «كفتة مشوية»; the café: «السكر»; others: «طريقة التقديم»). Through `save_group` and `set_dish_groups`; safe to run twice.
- **The migration** (one file, `restaurant/0006_modifiers`, after `0005_branches`): three new tables; `OrderLine.parent` (nullable FK to itself, CASCADE) and `OrderLine.options` (CharField 255, default ""). Existing rows need no fill: every old line is a plain line with no parent and no options.
- **What does not change:** `sales/pos.py`, sales posting, VAT calculation (`taxes`), the inventory engine and costing, `manufacturing.services.produce`, `kitchen.prepare_shortfall`, the cashbox engine, customer and supplier ledgers, the projector and journal rules, the permission core and seeded roles, the shift gate (R13), R12's branch rules (a branch's order with add-ons posts to its branch's store and till, 270.00 on BR-HALL/BR-TILL, and into the main till it is refused). A dish without groups behaves exactly as before. `restaurant/tests_pay_characterization.py` passes **unedited**.
- **Protected items touched:** models/schema/migrations (three tables, two columns); the table bill's rows (a priced add-on is a new sales line built by the restaurant's own `bill_rows`; the engines that post it are unchanged); a report calculation (`period_report`'s dish table: quantity by dish lines, add-on money folded into its dish; order totals untouched). Not touched: sales and purchase posting, inventory movement and costing, average cost, cashbox movement, ledgers, tax calculation, the permission core, closing.
- **Files:** `restaurant/models.py`, `restaurant/migrations/0006_modifiers.py` (new), `restaurant/modifiers.py` (new: the rules), `restaurant/views_modifiers.py` (new: the set-up page, the dish box, the sheets), `restaurant/services.py`, `restaurant/stations.py`, `restaurant/kitchen.py` (one call in `_copy_as_stock_item`), `restaurant/insights.py`, `restaurant/views.py`, `restaurant/urls.py`, `restaurant/demo.py`, `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py` (one call), `templates/restaurant/modifiers.html` (new), `order.html`, `ticket.html`, `dish_ingredients.html`, `board.html`, `stations.html`, `kds.html`, `static/hesba/css/restaurant.css` (`?v=resto-r1a`), `static/hesba/js/restaurant.js` (`?v=resto-r1a`), `static/hesba/css/kds.css` and `static/hesba/js/kds.js` (`?v=r1a-1`), `restaurant/tests_modifiers.py` (new), this entry, `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`.
- **Existing tests edited:** none.
- **Tests:** `restaurant/tests_modifiers.py` (39): the lines 240.00/30.00; the add-on's price at that moment; a missing required choice (also the one-tap path, Arabic and English) and a maximum exceeded refused with nothing added; never merging (also two identical burgers with add-ons) while plain dishes still merge; a plain add never merging into an add-on; optional groups with nothing chosen merging as before; quantity, remove and void carried to the family; an add-on refused on its own (service and screen); a fired dish's void under R13 for both; the bill with VAT on (307.80, rows, tax rows, cash, ledger, journal) and off (270.00); family order when lines interleave; a free choice; a voided family off the bill; the kitchen-made cheese (0.060 kg); the kitchen switch keeping groups and options; the e-receipt; a branch's order; the report; crafted requests (another group's option, options on a dish without groups, an inactive option, group or add-on item, the same option twice, ids that are not numbers or do not exist, a child line id from the screen, a KDS tap on an add-on); the ticket and the KDS (one station, indented, extras, ready together, a voided dish leaving the screen); the sheet, adding from it and in one tap; the set-up page (manage_items only, a save, a refusal, an edit turning an option off); the dish box (ingredients untouched, cashier refused); styles and script (defined tokens, no colour literals, no `innerHTML`, no banned words); sizes (one tile, a size added, a size with questions keeps its tile); the demo seeding (twice).
- **Risks and what is left open:**
  1. **Free choices are not on the invoice or the e-receipt.** The e-receipt prints a row's description in place of the item's name (`ereceipt.services.build`), so putting «ميديم» on the dish's row would replace «برجر» there. They print on the kitchen ticket, the kitchen screen and the order page.
  2. **The add-on's name is stored as chosen,** in the language of the screen it was added from (as a typed note is), in the child's `options`; renaming the option later does not change lines already taken.
  3. **An add-on item is also on the menu** as a tile of its own (the menu lists every active item; hiding items is the plan's «أقسام المنيو» setting, **not built**: it would make items disappear from existing menus). A service add-on also appears on the dish-costing list as a dish without a recipe.
  4. **Groups are company-wide** (see the decisions): a branch cannot ask a different question of the same dish. The crafted case «an option of another entity» therefore does not apply.
  5. **Kitchen screen ready tap:** tapping a dish marks its add-ons ready with it; there is no separate ready mark for an add-on.
  6. **Promotions** do not apply to the table bill (unchanged), so a «free cheese» offer is not possible yet.
  7. **A group turned off** stops being asked at once, also for a dish already on an open order (lines already taken keep their text and their add-on lines).
  8. **The seeded manager can set up groups but cannot attach them:** the role holds `manage_items` but not `inventory.view_cost` (checked on the restaurant demo: owner both, manager and stock keeper `manage_items` only), and the dish's page opens with `view_cost` (unchanged). The owner attaches groups, or gives the manager the cost view, or asks for an attach box on the set-up page (owner question 2).
- **Default decisions for the owner (built in brackets; change on request):**
  1. Groups per branch or for the whole company? (The whole company, like the menu: items, recipes and sizes carry no entity.)
  2. Who sets up groups and attaches them to dishes? (Set-up page: `master_data.manage_items`. Attaching: the dish's page, which opens with `inventory.view_cost` and saves with `manage_items`, so today the owner; see risk 8. If the manager should attach too, the fix is an attach box on the set-up page, not built.)
  3. A priced add-on's quantity? (The dish's quantity, always: 2 burgers with cheese = 2 cheese.)
  4. Its price? (The add-on item's sale price at the moment it is added; a later price change does not touch lines already taken.)
  5. Do free choices print on the customer's receipt? (No: the kitchen ticket, the kitchen screen and the order page only; see risk 1.)
  6. Two identical burgers with the same add-ons: one line of 2 or two lines? (Two lines: a line with add-ons or choices never merges.)
  7. Does the report count an add-on as a dish? (No; its money counts in its dish's row.)
  8. Sizes under one tile? (Yes, when two or more sizes of a family are on the menu and none asks questions of its own.)
  9. Hide raw ingredients from the menu with a new setting? (Not built; RS-02's existing rule still keeps an ingredient off «الكل».)
  10. Combos? (Not built: R1b.)

## HG-110 — Restaurants R2b: one bill, several tenders; `checkout(..., tenders=...)` pays a bill as cash and a card (each further tender a customer payment settled on the invoice, `SettlementSource.SPLIT`; migration `settlements/0002_split_source`, choices only)

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/restaurants-r2b-split-payment`, stacked on `claude/restaurants-r1a-modifiers` (HG-109, PR #412) and `claude/restaurants-r12-branches` (HG-108, PR #411), under Ahmed's delegation, **awaiting his review before merge**. T3: the shared checkout every activity's till uses (`sales/pos.py`), a new settlement choice, a cancellation listener, and two guard calls in the sales engine. No new permission, no new table or column. `makemigrations --check --dry-run`: «No changes detected». Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, restaurants item 15 from ناقص to **جزئي** (69.2%). Plan: «R2b – Split payment» (12 Nov); where it and the code disagree, the code was followed (see «Where the plan was wrong»).
- **Protected functions edited, exactly:**
  1. `sales.pos.checkout` (diff of `sales/pos.py`: +26 −2, of which 10 lines are new error words in `WORDS`): a keyword `tenders=None`; a block after `cash_due` that runs only when `tenders` is given (`sales.tenders.split` checks them and returns the first tender's cashbox and amount in place of `cashbox` and `tendered`, and the others; `cash_due` is lowered by the others); the walk-in rule's sum gains `+ others_total`; and, inside the transaction after the draft and its serials, `if others: pay_others(...)`. Nothing else in the function moved.
  2. `sales.services.create_sales_return`: one guard call after the gift-card one (`sales.tenders.before_sales_return`).
  3. `sales.services.cancel_customer_payment`: one guard call after the gift-card one (`sales.tenders.before_customer_payment_cancel`).
  Not edited: `post_sales_invoice`, `cancel_posted_sales_invoice`, `record_customer_payment`, `settlements.settle`/`release`, the deposits and gift-card hooks, the projector, the shift summary, the cashbox engine, VAT, stock, costing, ledgers, the permission core, closing.
- **How the single-tender path was shown unchanged:**
  1. **Characterization first** (commit `962c57b`, before `pos.py` was touched; the files are not edited afterwards): `sales/tests_pos_tenders_characterization.py` pins, row by row, what a till `checkout` writes today (every model's row count, invoice header and lines, tax rows, customer ledger, cashbox and stock movements, customer payments and settlements, audit actions, journal entries): cash with VAT on and change (342.00, change 58.00), cash with VAT off (500.00), the card button into the card machine (114.00, no change), a gift-card part with cash for the rest (300.00 = 200.00 card + 100.00 cash, change 50.00), a named customer on credit for the rest (342.00, 100.00 now, 242.00 due), and a short walk-in refused with nothing kept. `restaurant/tests_pay_tenders_characterization.py` adds the table bill into a card machine cashbox (walk-in 233.70 with VAT; a named customer paying 100.00 of 240.00), which R13's `tests_pay_characterization.py` did not cover. All 8 passed before the change and pass **unedited** after it; R13's file passes unedited too.
  2. With `tenders=None` the new code is `others_total = Decimal("0.00")`, so the walk-in sum is the same number, and `if others:` is false; with one tender the call is the plain call with that cashbox and that amount as `tendered` (tested: 400.00 on 307.80 gives change 92.20, no payment, no settlement). The two guards return without effect when the invoice has no split settlement.
  3. Every existing test in the labels run (SQLite and PostgreSQL, below) passes unedited.
- **What changes, with figures** (`sales/tests_pos_tenders.py`, the clock on Wed 11 Nov 2026 noon Cairo; a shirt 150.00 and a service 120.00, VAT 14% per line 21.00 + 16.80, total **307.80**):
  1. **Cash 200.00 and card 107.80, walk-in:** invoice paid_now 200.00 into «CASH» (its own cashbox), remaining_due 107.80; a customer payment `POS-20261111-0001-T2` of 107.80 into «CARD», dated the invoice's day, settled on the invoice (one `InvoiceSettlement`, 107.80, source `split`). «CASH» +200.00, «CARD» +107.80, the walk-in's balance 0.00, `applied_to(invoice)` 107.80 (nothing due). Audit: `create_sales_draft`, `record_customer_payment`, `post_sales_invoice`. Journal: balanced; cash on hand 1101 307.80 (both are cash-kind boxes), receivables 1103 0.00, sales 4101 −270.00, VAT 2103 −37.80. Change 0.00.
  2. **VAT off:** 270.00 as 150.00 and 120.00, the same shape. **Three tenders:** 100.00 cash, 100.00 card, 107.80 bank → `-T2` and `-T3`, banks 1102 107.80. **Gift card + two tenders:** 100.00 card part, 100.00 cash, 107.80 card machine → settlements `gift_card` 100.00 and `split` 107.80, walk-in 0.00. **A named customer** may leave the rest on account through the engine (100.00 + 100.00 of 307.80 → owes 107.80); the table bill does not allow it (below).
  3. **Refused, with every table compared before and after (nothing kept):** 300.00 on 307.80 for a walk-in («البيع الآجل محتاج عميل باسمه», the existing rule); more than the total (also for a named customer); a zero, a negative or a 0.004 tender; an empty list; a repeated cashbox; an inactive cashbox; a cheques, courier or gift-card box (all `HELD_KINDS`, as first or later tender); the card button together with tenders; a closed month.
  4. **The further tenders are recorded and settled before the invoice is posted** (still in the one transaction), because the e-receipt is built and hashed by the posting's own audit row: `ereceipt/tests_split.py` goes through the real issue (164.00 as 64.00 cash + 100.00 card → `"V"`; 100.00 + 64.00 → `"C"`).
  5. **E-receipt:** `ereceipt.services._payment_method` takes the largest tender's cashbox (`sales.tenders.largest_cashbox_id`; a tie goes to the first tender); an invoice paid one way reads its own cashbox exactly as before.
  6. **The table bill** (`restaurant/tests_split_pay.py`, the `tests_branches` set-up with R1a's burger): «ادفع بأكتر من طريقة» on the order page's bill box, a tick that shows 2 to 4 rows (cashbox, amount; one per cashbox of the order's branch, at most 4), the bill with VAT («المطلوب بالضريبة», worked out without writing anything, even for a delivery before its fee item exists) and a running «الباقي» (bill − discount − amounts) that keeps the pay button disabled until it is 0.00; the one-cashbox fields hide meanwhile. Rows start on the shift's drawer, then the card machine. Two burgers with cheese, VAT on, **307.80 as 200.00 into «TILL» and 107.80 into «CARD»**: the order is paid, the invoice keeps R1a's two rows (240.00 and 30.00), «TILL» +200.00, «CARD» +107.80, one `split` settlement 107.80, the walk-in 0.00, the owner's shift `{"sales": "200.00"}` and expected = float + 200.00, books balanced (main: cash 307.80, receivables 0.00, sales −270.00, VAT −37.80). VAT off 270.00 = 150 + 120; three ways with a 7.80 discount (300.00 as 100 × 3); a Maadi bill (R12) as 207.80 «BR-TILL» + 100.00 «BR-CARD» posts to «BR-HALL», the branch's books balanced and the main books unchanged. **The server checks again:** the amounts must equal the invoice's total exactly, for a walk-in and for a named customer («مجموع طرق الدفع لازم يساوي الحساب بالظبط: 307.80», checked against the posted invoice inside `pay()`'s transaction, so everything rolls back); one row only, a zero or negative amount, a repeated cashbox, another branch's cashbox (screen and service), a cheques box or an inactive cashbox → refused with nothing moved; without an open shift (R13) nothing is made or posted. With the box unticked the rows are not read at all and the bill is the one-cashbox bill (400.00 → change 92.20).
- **Cancellation (the listener):** `sales.tenders._on_audit` on `cancel_posted_sales_invoice` cancels each of the invoice's live split payments with `cancel_customer_payment`, on its own date (the invoice's), reason «اتلغت فاتورة … اللي الدفعة دي جزء منها», in the same transaction. **Precedent:** `giftcards.services._on_audit`, HG-071 deviation D3. Cancelling the 307.80 invoice: «CASH» back by 200.00 through the invoice's own cancellation, «CARD» back by 107.80 through the payment's, the walk-in 0.00 (not −107.80), no settlement row left (the deposits hook's `settlements.release` removes it after the payment is cancelled), books all 0.00. A closed month refuses the whole cancellation (the invoice's own period check; nothing moves). **Ordering:** the split payments are found through their settlement rows, which the deposits hook deletes; the listener is connected from `sales.apps.SalesConfig.ready()` and `sales` comes before `deposits` in `INSTALLED_APPS`, so it runs first. If the apps were reordered, the cancellation tests fail.
- **Approval hold:** with `sales.cancel_invoice` held, a clerk's request moves nothing but the request and its audit row; the owner's approval runs the cancellation and both tenders go back (`tests_pos_tenders_cancel`).
- **Walk-in guards (HG-071 D7's rule, for split payments):** a walk-in's split payment cannot be cancelled alone («cancel the whole invoice instead»: it would leave the walk-in owing 107.80); a walk-in invoice paid several ways cannot be returned (a return refunds only `paid_now`'s share from the invoice's cashbox and lowers the customer's due by the rest, which on a walk-in would be a credit nobody can use while the card cashbox stays +107.80): cancel the whole invoice instead. Both messages are in Arabic on Arabic screens (`settings_core/ui_messages.py`). **A named customer's** split payment can be cancelled alone (the invoice is due again, 107.80), and a return behaves as on any partly paid invoice: returning the 136.80 service refunds 88.89 in cash from «CASH» (200.00 × 136.80 ÷ 307.80) and the other 47.91 becomes the customer's credit; «CARD» keeps its 107.80. A walk-in invoice paid one way still returns in cash as before (136.80 in cash, walk-in 0.00).
- **Where the plan was wrong (the code was followed):**
  1. **Shifts have no card group.** `shifts.services.summary` counts only the shift's own cashbox (the drawer), and AN8 keeps the card machine out of every shift. So the shift shows sales 200.00 and the card's 107.80 is in the card cashbox's balance, in no shift, exactly as a card sale at the till today. If a cash tender into the drawer is not the first row, it is a customer payment and so lands in the shift's «collections» group, still inside the expected cash (from the code; not a separate test).
  2. **The bill's total on the page is before VAT** (`Order.total`); the split box needed the VAT-inclusive figure, so `restaurant.services.bill_total` works it out as `checkout` does, without writing (the plain «الإجمالي» line is unchanged).
  3. **The e-receipt is built at posting,** so the further tenders are recorded before the invoice is posted, not after (the plan put them after).
  4. There is no `returns` app: returns are in `sales`.
- **What does not change:** the till screen (`sales/pos.html`, `pos.js`: still one cashbox or the card button); the plain bill; VAT, stock and costing; posting and cancellation of invoices; the projector (a split payment is an ordinary customer payment: Dr the cashbox's account, Cr receivables); the shift engine; the cashbox report (each payment is a `customer_payment` movement in its own cashbox); R12's branch rule and R13's shift rule; R1a's rows; the offline till sync; deposits, payment links and gift cards.
- **Files:** `sales/pos.py`, `sales/tenders.py` (new: the rules, the listener, the guards), `sales/apps.py` (`ready()` connects the listener), `sales/services.py` (two guard calls), `settlements/models.py`, `settlements/migrations/0002_split_source.py` (new), `ereceipt/services.py`, `settings_core/ui_messages.py`, `restaurant/services.py` (`pay(..., tenders=)`, `_split_cashboxes`, `bill_total`), `restaurant/views.py`, `templates/restaurant/order.html`, `static/hesba/css/restaurant.css`, `static/hesba/js/restaurant.js` (`?v=resto-r2b` on every page that loads them), `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`.
- **Existing tests edited:** none.
- **Tests (new):** `sales/tests_pos_tenders_characterization.py` (6), `restaurant/tests_pay_tenders_characterization.py` (2), `sales/tests_pos_tenders.py` (15), `sales/tests_pos_tenders_cancel.py` (12: also the cashbox report CASH 200.00 / CARD 107.80 and both 0.00 after cancelling, the shift closed at float + 200.00 with no difference, loyalty points equal to a bill paid one way, and the invoice page's «Still to collect» 0.00), `sales/tests_pos_tenders_concurrency.py` (1, PostgreSQL only: two split bills at the same moment both post, «-0001» and «-0002», each with its own `-T2` payment; cash 300.00, card 240.00), `ereceipt/tests_split.py` (3, also a one-way card-machine sale still `"V"`), `restaurant/tests_split_pay.py` (18).
- **Risks and what is left open:**
  0. **Found in review, fixed:** the cancellation listener hears every audit row and looked its invoice up with `get`, so an audit row naming an invoice that is not there raised (loyalty's own test of that case failed; the agent's list did not include `loyalty`). It now ignores such a row; `sales.tests_pos_tenders_cancel.StrayAuditRowTests`.
  1. **Listener order** depends on `sales` being before `deposits` in `INSTALLED_APPS` (see above); the cancellation tests guard it.
  2. **Customer ledger order:** the split payment's row is written before the invoice's due row (same date), so a customer statement lists the −107.80 payment one line before the +107.80 invoice; the balance after both is 0.00.
  3. **Collections figures:** each further tender is a customer payment, so reports that count collections by payment date count it there (the invoice's `paid_now` stays the first tender), exactly as a gift-card redemption is counted today. The till's own «مبيعاتك النهارده» card figure reads `paid_now` into the card machine only, so a table bill's card part is not in it.
  4. **No change in split mode:** each row is what is kept; the bill gives no change figure when paid several ways (see decision 3).
  5. **A named customer's split invoice returned:** the card part becomes the customer's credit; there is no refund-to-card path (as for any collection).
  6. **The page's «المطلوب بالضريبة»** is a convenience worked out like `checkout`; the server's exact check against the posted invoice is what counts.
  7. **What else reacts:** every `AuditLog` listener was read (deposits, gift cards, loyalty, e-receipt, R2b's own). None reacts to `record_customer_payment`; loyalty earns on the posted invoice's total once (tested), so a split payment earns nothing more. No messaging hook follows collections.
  8. **The invoice page** shows the split part under the settlements' existing label «مدفوع مقدم (عربون / لينك / كارت)» with «الباقي للتحصيل» 0.00; the label was not changed in this PR.
  9. **Concurrency** was tested for two bills racing for the same invoice number on PostgreSQL; nothing else in the split path takes a new lock (the payment numbers derive from the invoice's unique number).
- **Default decisions for the owner (built in brackets; change on request):**
  1. The e-receipt's payment method for a bill paid several ways? (The largest tender's; a tie goes to the first row. Owner question 8.)
  2. Which tender is the invoice's own «paid now»? (The first row; it starts on the biller's shift drawer.)
  3. Change when paying several ways? (None: the amounts must add up to the bill exactly, and each row is what is kept. The one-cashbox bill still gives change.)
  4. May a named customer leave part of the bill on account when paying several ways at the table? (No: exact, as for the walk-in. The engine allows it for later callers.)
  5. A walk-in bill paid several ways: return part of it? (No: cancel the whole invoice and bill again; every part goes back. A named customer's returns as any partly paid invoice.)
  6. A walk-in's split payment cancelled on its own from the collections screen? (No; with its invoice only.)
