  7. The same cashbox on two rows? (Refused; up to 4 rows, one per cashbox.)
  8. Several tenders on the quick till screen? (Not built in this PR: the engine is ready, the screen is not.)

## HG-111 — Restaurants R2a: «ادفع جزء» splits a table's bill by whole items into parts paid on their own invoices, «رجّع للحساب» puts a part back, «ادمج مع طاولة» merges two tables (`Order.split_from`, `Order.merged_into`, status `merged`; migration `restaurant/0007_split_merge`); the restaurant report and the floor tiles count a bill paid in parts once

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/restaurants-r2a-split-merge`, stacked on `claude/restaurants-r2b-split-payment` (HG-110, PR #413), `claude/restaurants-r1a-modifiers` (HG-109, PR #412) and `claude/restaurants-r12-branches` (HG-108, PR #411), under Ahmed's delegation, **awaiting his review before merge**. T3: two columns and a status choice on `Order`, two guarded branches on the table bill's own pay path (`restaurant.services._payable`, `_close`), and a report-calculation change. No new permission, no new table. `sales/pos.py`, `sales/services.py`, `sales/tenders.py`, posting, VAT, stock, cashbox, ledgers and the permission core are **not edited**: each part is an ordinary order paid by the unchanged `pay()` → `checkout`. `makemigrations --check --dry-run`: «No changes detected». Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, restaurants item 15 from جزئي to **موجود** (71.2%). Plan: «R2a – Split by items, and merge tables» (12 Nov); where it and the code disagree, the code was followed (see «Where the plan was wrong»).
- **Schema (`restaurant/0007_split_merge`, one file):** `Order.split_from` (FK to `Order`, nullable, PROTECT, `related_name="splits"`), `Order.merged_into` (FK to `Order`, nullable, PROTECT, `related_name="merged_orders"`), and the choice `merged` («اتدمج» in `settings_core/display_labels.py`). Existing rows need no fill: every existing order is a whole bill. The one-open-order-per-table constraint is unchanged.
- **What changes, with figures** (`restaurant/tests_split_merge.py`; R2b's set-up: VAT 14% per line, the clock on Wed 11 Nov 2026 noon Cairo; a koshary at 45.00 and a tea at 10.00, both service dishes):
  1. **Split by items.** Table «1» (2 guests) orders 2 × koshary + 3 × tea = **120.00**. Guest A's part, 1 koshary + 1 tea: **55.00**, invoice **62.70** (VAT 7.70); the rest **65.00**, invoice **74.10** (VAT 9.10); together **136.80**, exactly the same bill unsplit (120.00 + 16.80). VAT off: 55.00 and 65.00. The part is a new open order: same kind, waiter, customer and branch (R12: a Maadi table's part bills into «BR-HALL»/«BR-TILL», 51.30), `split_from` set, **no table** while open (the database allows one open order per table), **guests 0** (people are counted on the parent). Once paid it takes the parent's table (`_close`; the constraint covers open orders only); its invoice notes name the parent's table («طلب مطعم R-… (1)»). A part paid with R2b's tenders works (62.70 = 40.00 «TILL» + 22.70 «CARD»).
  2. **By whole plates, never by money (trap 1).** A line is split by a whole count up to its quantity (2 of 3 teas), or moved whole (a 1.5 kg line goes whole or not at all). A fraction of a line is refused with the way to share equally («الأصناف بتتقسم بالعدد، مش بالفلوس… ادفعوه مرة واحدة بـ«ادفع بأكتر من طريقة»»). The plan's «thirds» pin (90.00 → 29.97 + 29.97 + 30.06, VAT 12.61 against 12.60) is therefore a refusal test, not a shown difference: no split can put a piastre on one bill and not the other, because every row of a part is a whole row of the same price and every row's VAT is the row's own.
  3. **Add-ons and the kitchen.** A dish's R1a add-ons follow it in the same count and its free choices are copied: a burger with cheese split 1 of 2 → each side has burger 1 «ميديم» + cheese 1 under it; each invoice is «D-BURG 120.00, A-CHEESE 15.00» in family order, 153.90 each, 307.80 together (R2b's unsplit figure). A whole dish moves its add-on lines with it, pks and all. Moved lines keep their kitchen ticket, ready mark and order time (`created_at`); the ticket stays the parent's, so the kitchen screen and the printed ticket show every line once, under the parent's table («طاولة 1»: koshary 1, koshary 1, tea 3 for a ticket that held koshary 2, tea 3). A part's own ticket (an unsent line sent from the part) names its table's too (`Order.home_table`). A kitchen-made dish split 1 + 1 across two bills takes 0.200 + 0.200 kg rice and 0.100 + 0.100 kg lentils: rice 20.000 → 19.600, lentils 10.000 → 9.800, no plate left in stock, what one bill of two plates takes.
  4. **Rules on the part and its parent:** choosing every live line is refused («كده الحساب كله اتختار؛ ادفع حساب الطاولة نفسه.»; a voided line is no money owed). Refused with nothing changed (every order, line and ticket row compared): a voided line, more than the line holds, a fraction, a negative or non-number, a zero part, an empty part, a line of another order, an unknown line, an add-on on its own, a part of a part, a closed order, a delivery («الدليفري بيتدفع فاتورة واحدة»). A takeaway can be split (its part stays a takeaway without a table). While a part is open the parent is not paid («فيه جزء من الحساب لسه مفتوح (R-…)؛ ادفعه أو رجّعه للحساب الأول.») or cancelled; a part is not moved, merged or cancelled («رجّعه للحساب»). Moving the parent to another table takes its open parts' home along (paid there, the part's table is the new one). With every part paid, the parent is paid or cancelled as any order. R13's shift rule refuses a part's bill without a shift with the till's words and nothing written; a waiter cannot void a part's sent line; a reason is required; the void is audited on the part.
  5. **«رجّع للحساب» (put back):** an open part returns its lines to the parent: a line split off by count folds back into the line it came from (same dish, price, note, choices, ticket, ready state and add-ons), a whole line moves back with its own pk, and the part's own tickets move back numbered after the parent's last. The part becomes `merged` with `merged_into` = its parent. Before/after the split and put back (burger with cheese, koshary with a ready mark, a sent tea moved whole, an unsent tea split 1 of 2): every line, quantity, price, choice, ticket, ready and void state, the tickets and the total compare **equal**, and the bill then pays 467.40 (307.80 + 136.80 + 22.80).
  6. **Merge («ادمج مع طاولة»):** table «1» (2 guests, 2 × koshary = 90.00, ticket 1) and table «2» (3 guests, 3 × tea = 30.00, ticket 1) → **one order of 120.00 with 5 guests** on table «1», its waiter kept; table «2»'s ticket becomes table «1»'s ticket 2 (the numbering stays unique) and its kitchen card says «طاولة 1»; table «2»'s order is `merged` with `merged_into` set, no lines and no tickets left, and **table «2» is free on the board and takes a new order at once**. The report shows 1 order, 120.00, 5 guests. Refused with nothing changed: into a paid, cancelled or merged order, from one, into itself, across branches (R12, either way), a takeaway (either way), nothing chosen. The merged table's parts, open and paid, become the receiving order's parts (otherwise a paid part under a merged order would count its table's guests twice, since they were added to the receiver). Both orders are audited (`merge_orders` on the receiver with before/after guests and total, lines, tickets renumbered and parts; `merged_into` on the other). Both are locked in pk order (`_lock_pair`): two people merging «1» into «2» and «2» into «1» at the same moment → one merges, the other is refused, no deadlock (PostgreSQL test with each lock held 0.5 s; with the lock order unfixed the same test fails with «deadlock detected»).
- **The report calculation change (`restaurant.insights.period_report`), stated precisely:** before, every paid order in the period was an order. After, a bill paid in parts is **one** order: a part adds its sales to every figure, but the order is counted once, at the parent when the parent is paid in the period, else at its first paid part; that one count carries the parent's table, waiter, kind, guests (`root.guests`) and stay (closed − the parent's opening). Hours, kinds, waiters and tables count orders the same way; an hour with only a part's sales is listed with 0 orders. `merged` orders are never paid, so they were and are never counted. **Figures** (the 120.00 split, VAT off, both paid): **before** orders 2, sales 120.00, average 60.00, guests 2 (2 + the part's 0), table «1» 2 orders with an average of 60.00, kinds dine-in 2, waiters 2, hours 2; **after** orders **1**, sales 120.00, average **120.00**, guests 2, table «1» 1 order 120.00 (average 120.00) 2 guests, dine-in 1, waiter 1, hours 1 (measured by running the same case against the code before and after). With only the part paid: before orders 1, 55.00, **guests 0**; after orders 1, 55.00, guests **2**. The merged table with a paid and an open part: before **3** orders, after **1** (120.00, 5 guests, table «1» only). A plain order's figures are identical: every existing report test passes unedited. Dish figures are unchanged (each line counts once wherever it was paid: koshary 2 / 90.00, tea 3 / 30.00).
- **The floor tiles (`reports.activity_panel._kitchen`), a count change:** «طلبات مفتوحة» no longer counts open parts (a split table is 1, not 2; their money is still in «قيمة المفتوح», 120.00) and «اتدفع النهارده» counts a bill paid in parts once (distinct `coalesce(split_from, id)`): before 2 and an average order of 68.40, after **1** and **136.80**. Tables busy and the open value are as before.
- **The board:** an open part is listed under its parent's table card («جزء مفتوح · 55.00»), and under its parent's row for a takeaway, never as a stray takeaway (`services.board()` gives each table row its `parts`). A table that never splits or merges looks as before.
- **Screens** (`templates/restaurant/order.html`, `_table_card.html`, `board.html`, `restaurant.css`, `restaurant.js`, `?v=resto-r2a` on every restaurant page): «ادفع جزء» on an open table's bill opens a bottom sheet (R1a's sheet) listing the live dishes with their add-ons, a − / + stepper each (0 to the line's count, 44 px), the part's running amount before VAT, the rule on equal shares, and «افتح حساب الجزء»; it opens the part's page, the ordinary order page headed «جزء من حساب الطاولة 1» with «حساب الطاولة» back and «رجّع للحساب»; paying a part comes back to the table's bill. The parent lists «أجزاء الحساب»: each part's number, «لسه ما اتدفعش» or «اتدفع» with its invoice number, and its amount. «ادمج مع طاولة» (in «انقل لطاولة / ادمج مع طاولة / إلغاء الطلب») lists the other open tables of the branch. Arabic and English; tokens only, no new colour literal. Screenshots at 390 px (a test-client render opened in headless Chrome): the sheet open with 1 koshary + 1 tea = 55.00, the parent with its open part, the part's page, the board.
- **What does not change:** `checkout`, posting, VAT per line, the invoice rows of an unsplit bill, stock and plate-making (`kitchen.prepare_shortfall` runs per bill on that bill's lines, unchanged), cashboxes, the shift engine, ledgers, the permission core (split, put back and merge are floor actions like moving a table; paying a part needs the selling right as any bill), R12's branch rule, R13's shift and void rules, R1a's families, R2b's tenders. An order that is never split or merged runs the same statements except one extra read in `_payable` and `cancel_order` (is a part open?).
- **Protected items touched:** model/schema/migration (two columns, a choice); the table bill's pay path in `restaurant/services.py`: `_payable` gains `_no_open_part` (refuses while a part is open), `_close` gains one branch (a paid part with no table takes its parent's, `update_fields` + `table`), `_bill_notes` names `home_table`; report calculations (`insights.period_report`, `activity_panel._kitchen`). Not touched: `sales/*`, `settlements`, `taxes`, `inventory`, `cashboxes`, `ledger`, `permissions`, `closing`.
- **Files:** `restaurant/models.py`, `restaurant/migrations/0007_split_merge.py` (new), `restaurant/services.py` (`split_order`, `put_back`, `merge_orders`, `_lock_pair`, guards in `move_table`, `cancel_order`, `_payable`, `_close`, `board`), `restaurant/insights.py`, `reports/activity_panel.py`, `restaurant/stations.py` and `restaurant/views_kds.py` (a part's ticket names its table), `restaurant/views.py`, `templates/restaurant/order.html`, `_table_card.html`, `board.html`, `ticket.html` (+ `?v=` on `stations.html`, `modifiers.html`, `dish_ingredients.html`), `static/hesba/css/restaurant.css`, `static/hesba/js/restaurant.js`, `settings_core/display_labels.py`, `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`.
- **Existing tests edited:** one line, `restaurant/tests_split_pay.py` (R2b, not merged yet): its `?v=resto-r2b` pin is now `?v=resto-r2a`, because this PR changed the stylesheet and script again and bumped the version as required. No figure in any existing test moved; `tests_pay_characterization.py` and `tests_pay_tenders_characterization.py` pass unedited.
- **Tests (new):** `restaurant/tests_split_merge.py` (30, one PostgreSQL only): the split and its invoices (VAT on/off, R2b tenders, Maadi), the kitchen ticket and card, plates made once, add-on families, the crafted refusals, the parent's guards and moving it, the shift and void rules on a part, put back (exact snapshot, a part's own ticket renumbered), merge (figures, board, new order on the freed table, audits, refusals, parts following), the report and the floor tiles, the screens (sheet, part page, parent's parts, board, put back and merge from the page, English, R12 404s and a crafted merge pick), and the merge lock race.
- **Where the plan was wrong (the code was followed):**
  1. The plan let add-ons «and the combo discount» follow in proportion: there is no combo yet (R1b), so only add-ons follow; a combo's discount will need its own rule when R1b lands.
  2. The plan's «thirds» pin is not reachable: a part takes whole items only (trap 1), so it is a refusal test.
  3. `TableMoveLockTests` (named in the task) is on the R12 branch's later commits, not on this stack; the merge lock test copies its style.
  4. The plan's «show the parent's table after paying» needs no condition: a paid part never meets the open-orders constraint, so `_close` always sets it.
- **Risks and what is left open:**
  1. **A parent cancelled after a part was paid:** the cancelled flag on the kitchen card is the order's, so a ticket holding the paid part's lines shows «الطلب اتلغى» too (the money and stock are right; only the card's banner).
  2. **Put back folds a split line into its origin by matching** (dish, price, note, choices, ticket, ready state, add-ons); two genuinely separate identical lines on one ticket (which `add_item` never makes) would be folded into one line of the same total.
  3. **A part is paid without locking its parent** (locking it would invert put back's lock order). If the parent is moved to another table at the very moment its part is paid, the part's table may record the old table; the report counts the bill under the parent's table either way.
  4. **Adding items on a part's page** is allowed (it is the ordinary order page); they are the part's bill, and go back to the table if the part is put back.
  5. **Reports outside the restaurant** (sales by invoice, the cashbox report, shifts) see each part as its own invoice, as they should.
  6. The parent's pay box still shows while a part is open; paying is refused with the part's number.
  7. **PROTECT on the two self-links:** no code path deletes orders through the ORM (the demo restart uses `flush`, the demo's first run swaps a database copy, a backup is restored as a database dump), so a split or merged order never blocks them. A future ORM bulk delete of orders would have to delete parts before parents.
- **Default decisions for the owner (built in brackets; change on request):**
  1. Whose order is a merged order? (The receiving table's: its waiter, customer and table. Owner question 9.)
  2. Which way does «ادمج مع طاولة» go? (The table chosen comes to the one being worked on; its table is freed.)
  3. Merging a table that has parts? (Its parts, open and paid, follow it to the receiving order.)
  4. Splitting a delivery? (No: one invoice; R2b's tenders share it. Dine-in and takeaway can be split.)
  5. A part put back: identical lines merged back? (Yes: a line split off by count folds back into its origin, so the bill is exactly as before.)
  6. Who may split, put back and merge? (Whoever takes orders, the waiter included, as for moving a table; paying a part needs the selling right.)
  7. Paying or cancelling the parent while a part is open? (Refused: pay the part or put it back first.)
  8. A part of a part, or cancelling a part? (No: split from the table's bill; put a part back instead of cancelling it.)

## HG-112 — Restaurants R1b: combos (meal deals): a combo is sold as its chosen components, each at its menu price, with the saving spread over them as line discounts (`Combo`, `ComboSlot`, `ComboChoice`; `OrderLine.discount`, `combo`, `bundle`; migration `restaurant/0008_combos`); the restaurant report gains «خصم الكومبو»

- **Status:** implemented on `claude/restaurants-r1b-combos`, stacked on `claude/restaurants-r2a-split-merge` (HG-111, PR #414), `claude/restaurants-r2b-split-payment` (HG-110, PR #413) and `claude/restaurants-r1a-modifiers` (HG-109, PR #412), under Ahmed's delegation, **awaiting his review before merge**. T3: three new tables, three columns on `OrderLine`, a discount on the table bill's invoice rows, and a report-calculation change. No new permission. `sales/pos.py`, `sales/services.py`, `sales/tenders.py`, posting, VAT, stock, cashbox, ledgers, `promotions` and the permission core are **not edited**: a combo is ordinary invoice rows with `line_discount_amount`, which the sales engine already supports. `makemigrations --check --dry-run`: «No changes detected». Roadmap: `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`, restaurants item 14 from جزئي to **موجود** (73.1%). Plan: «R1b – Combos» (12 Nov); where it and the code disagree, the code was followed (see «Where the plan was wrong»).
- **Schema (`restaurant/0008_combos`, one file):** `Combo` (`item`: one-to-one to `master_data.Item`, PROTECT; `name_en`, `sort`, `active`), `ComboSlot` (`combo` CASCADE, `name_ar`, `name_en`, `sort`), `ComboChoice` (`slot` CASCADE, `item` PROTECT, `upcharge` decimal 14,2 default 0.00, `sort`, `active`; unique per slot and item; a database check `upcharge >= 0`). On `OrderLine`: `discount` (decimal 14,2, default 0.00, a database check `>= 0`: what one unit sells below its menu price), `combo` (FK, nullable, PROTECT), `bundle` (positive integer, nullable: which combo taken on the order, numbered from 1 per order). Existing rows need no fill: every existing line has discount 0.00 and no combo.
- **How the price is spread, and why (the key decision):**
  - the combo's own item (a non-stock service item whose sale price is the combo's price, made by the set-up page as `COMBO-001`, `COMBO-002`…) **never goes on an order or a bill**: `add_item` refuses it («افتحه من المنيو واختار من كل خانة»; trap 7). Its components do, each at its own menu price at that moment;
  - `saving = sum of the chosen components' menu prices − (combo price + the chosen upcharges)`, split in proportion to the menu prices (`combos.spread`): each share is rounded **down** to the piastre, and the piastres left go one each to the largest remainders (the earlier component first on a tie), so the discounts add up **exactly** to the saving and none passes its price;
  - **why:** the dishes report and the cost of sales stay true per dish; VAT stays per component at its own rate; the e-receipt has real lines; a return of one component refunds its real share through the unchanged return path; a T5 offer skips a line that carries a discount (`promotions.services._open`), so a combo never takes a second discount. Selling the combo item at the combo price with components at zero would zero dish revenue, break VAT per component and the e-receipt, and let «10% off» discount it again (trap 7);
  - **each combo taken is its own bundle of quantity-1 lines** (two combos = two bundles), so the per-unit `discount` is the line's discount exactly. **Why not one line of quantity 2:** the discounts would still be exact (per-unit share × 2), but VAT is rounded per line, and the doubled line rounds differently: two combos with large fries as quantity-2 lines would be 196.92, 82.06 and 41.02 with VAT 27.57 + 11.49 + 5.74 = **44.80**, a piastre more than two combos rung up one by one (2 × 22.39 = **44.78**). As bundles they are 364.78, exactly twice 182.39 (tested). It also keeps R2a simple: a combo is split, put back and voided whole, with no fraction of a bundle to fold back.
- **What changes, with figures** (`restaurant/tests_combos.py`; VAT 14% per line, the clock on Wed 11 Nov 2026 noon Cairo; «وجبة برجر» 150.00 = burger 120.00 + fries 40.00 (or large fries 50.00, +10.00) + Pepsi 25.00 (a stocked can)):
  1. **Taking it:** burger 120.00 − 22.70 = 97.30, fries 40.00 − 7.57 = 32.43, Pepsi 25.00 − 4.73 = 20.27: discounts **35.00**, nets **150.00**, the order's total 150.00. **VAT on:** 13.62 + 4.54 + 2.84 = **21.00**, total **171.00**; the posted invoice has those three rows with those `line_discount_amount`s, subtotal 150.00, tax 21.00, total 171.00, invoice discount 0.00; the can leaves stock like any can. R2b's «الباقي» (`bill_total`) reads 171.00 before paying. **VAT off:** 150.00.
  2. **Large fries:** charged **160.00**, saving 35.00 as 21.54, 8.97 and 4.49; nets 98.46, 41.03 and 20.51; **VAT on 13.78 + 5.74 + 2.87 = 22.39** (not 22.40: rounded per line), total **182.39**; VAT off 160.00.
  3. **Two of the same combo:** two bundles, discounts 70.00, subtotal 300.00, VAT 42.00, total **342.00**; with large fries 320.00, VAT 44.78, total **364.78** (VAT off 320.00). A quantity that is not a whole number from 1 to 99 is refused.
  4. **Return:** the fries of the first of two combos refund **36.97** with VAT on (32.43 + 4.54), **32.43** with VAT off, through `sales.services.create_sales_return` unchanged.
  5. **A running 10% offer** (`promotions.apply` on the table bill's rows): **12.00** off a burger sold alone, **0.00** on the combo's three lines (their discounts stay 22.70, 7.57, 4.73).
  6. **A component's R1a add-on:** a burger with «جبنة زيادة» (15.00) in the combo: the cheese is its own line under the burger at **15.00, never discounted**; the order 165.00; VAT on 23.10, total **188.10** (21.00 + 2.10). A required choice on a component («درجة الاستواء») is asked on the combo sheet and refused when missing; an add-on sent for another slot's item is refused.
  7. **Family rules:** removing (unsent) or voiding (sent) the combo's first line (its head) removes or voids every component and its add-ons; a sent combo's void needs the selling right and a reason (R13) and is audited with the combo's name and every component. A component is never changed, removed, voided or split alone («الكومبو بيتغيّر كله مع بعض»); a head's quantity stays 1 («كل كومبو بيتسجّل لوحده»); a component's kitchen note can be changed. The components go to the kitchen in the same send (each to its station). A plain burger never merges into a combo's burger.
  8. **R2a:** splitting one component alone is refused; naming the head splits the **whole bundle** (all three lines, their discounts and add-ons); with a koshary (60.00) beside it, VAT on: the part **171.00** and the rest **68.40** total **239.40**, the bill unsplit. A combo alone is the whole bill and is not split. **Put back** returns the bundle's own lines (same pks, discounts, tickets): the bill is identical; if the bill took another combo meanwhile under the same number, the returning bundle takes the next free number (bundles {1, 2}, total 370.00). **Merge:** two tables each with a bundle 1 → one order with bundles 1 and 2 (the merged-in table's renumbered after both orders' highest), 310.00, paid 353.39 (171.00 + 182.39).
  9. **Kitchen:** the printed ticket prints the combo's name once over its components on that ticket; the kitchen screen's line carries `combo` (and `bundle`) only on a component, so a plain line's entry is as before.
  10. **R12:** a combo on a Maadi table posts to «BR-HALL»/«BR-TILL», 150.00.
- **The report calculation change (`restaurant.insights.period_report`), stated precisely:** dish figures are computed as before (each live line at its menu price, `quantity × unit_price`; add-ons in their dish's row), so a combo's components count as dishes at **menu price**. New: `totals["combo_discount"]`, the sum of the period's live lines' discounts, shown under «الأكثر مبيعًا» as «خصم الكومبو» (only when it is not zero) with a note that the dishes less it come to the lines' net value. **Figures** (one combo + one burger alone, VAT off, one invoice of 270.00): **before** (the same calculation with no combo figure) orders 1, sales 270.00, dishes burger 2 / 240.00, fries 1 / 40.00, Pepsi 1 / 25.00 (305.00), and nothing explaining the 35.00 between the dishes and the sales; **after** the same, plus combo discount **35.00**: 305.00 − 35.00 = 270.00 = the invoice's net sales. An order without a combo: combo discount 0.00 and the row hidden; every existing report figure and test is unchanged. The combo discount is counted on paid orders' lines like the dish figures (not reduced by a later return, as the dish figures are not).
- **Other money paths touched:** `OrderLine.amount` is now the line's menu price less its discount (`gross − line_discount`; identical on every line without a combo), so `Order.total`, the order page, a part's amount and the audits read the net; `services.bill_rows` sends `line_discount_amount = line.line_discount` (was a literal 0) and describes a component «برجر — وجبة برجر»; `services.bill_total` (R2b's «الباقي») sends the discounts and sums the nets.
- **Set-up:** «الكومبو» (`/restaurant/combos/`, `views_combos.setup`, `master_data.manage_items`: the permission R1a's add-ons set-up uses), linked from the tables board. It lists every combo with its price, slots, choices (menu price and «+upcharge»), and **its saving at the cheapest choices**; it creates or edits a combo (names ar/en, price, order, active) with up to 5 slots of up to 8 choices (item + extra charge). An emptied saved slot or choice is removed (a line keeps its own item, price and discount, so nothing printed before changes). Refused with nothing saved: no name, a name another combo has, a price that is not above zero, no slot, a slot with no name or no active choice, a negative extra charge, an item twice in a slot, an inactive item, **a choice that is a combo** (or the combo's own item), another combo's slot or choice id, and **a price above what the cheapest choices come to** («سعر الكومبو (185.01) أكبر من أرخص اختياراته مع بعض (185.00)…»; 185.00 itself is accepted). An R1a add-on group refuses a combo's item. Set-up is audited (`create_combo` / `change_combo` with before/after names, price, slots and choices).
- **Taking a combo** re-checks at that moment: the combo and its item active; one choice for every slot, each the slot's own, active, its item active; the price not above what the chosen items cost now («الكومبو بقى أغلى من أصنافه بأسعار المنيو»: a price raised or an item made cheaper since set-up). Everything is checked before anything is written.
- **Screens:** the order page's menu shows a combo as a tile («كومبو») that opens its sheet (R1a's sheet pattern): one choice per slot (radios, «ضمن السعر» or «+10.00»), a chosen dish's own add-ons and required choices nested under it and shown (and sent) only while it is chosen, the number of combos and a note. On the bill a combo is one block: «1 × وجبة برجر», its net, each component at menu price with its choices and add-ons, «توفير الكومبو −35.00», and one remove/void for the whole. «ادفع جزء» lists a combo as one row at its net (150.00). Arabic and English; existing classes and `--hs-*` tokens only, no new colour literal, 44 px targets, no horizontal scroll at 390 px (measured: scroll width 390 on the order page and the set-up). `?v=resto-r1b` on every restaurant page (`restaurant.css` and `restaurant.js` changed); `?v=r1b-1` on the kitchen screen (`kds.css`, `kds.js`). Screenshots at 390 px from a demo database served by `runserver` and opened in headless Chrome: the combo sheet (ar, en, with a required choice under the kofta), the order with a combo block (ar, en), the set-up page.
- **Demo:** «وجبة التوفير» / «Saver combo» set up in the restaurant demo through `combos.save_combo`, **after every demo order** (`prepare_demo._floor`, after R1a's groups): «الطبق» (the grilled half chicken 180.00, or the kofta 220.00 for +20.00) and «المشروب» (the small water 15.00 or the can 25.00), at **175.00**, 10% under the cheapest 195.00, rounded down to 5 (saving 20.00 at the cheapest). Nothing is ordered with it, so **no pinned demo figure moved** (9 paid orders, the report's orders and the combo discount 0.00, tested); safe to run twice.
- **What does not change:** `checkout`, posting, VAT per line, the e-receipt, stock, plate-making, cashboxes, the shift engine, ledgers, `promotions`, the permission core, R12's branch rule, R13's void rule, R1a's families, R2b's tenders, R2a's split by whole items. A bill without a combo sends the same rows (its `line_discount_amount` is 0.00 instead of 0) and the same totals.
- **Protected items touched:** model/schema/migration (three tables, three columns); the table bill's rows (`restaurant/services.py`: `bill_rows` sends each component's discount, `bill_total` reads it); report calculations (`insights.period_report` gains `combo_discount`). Not touched: `sales/*`, `settlements`, `taxes`, `inventory`, `cashboxes`, `ledger`, `promotions`, `permissions`, `closing`.
- **Files:** `restaurant/models.py`, `restaurant/migrations/0008_combos.py` (new), `restaurant/combos.py` (new: the rules, `spread`, set-up, choosing, bundles, the bill's blocks), `restaurant/services.py` (`add_combo`, `add_item`'s refusal and merge guard, `change_line`, `void_line`, `bill_rows`, `bill_total`, `split_order`, `put_back`, `merge_orders`, `_copy_line`, `_origin`), `restaurant/modifiers.py` (an add-on is never a combo), `restaurant/insights.py`, `restaurant/stations.py`, `restaurant/views.py`, `restaurant/views_combos.py` (new), `restaurant/urls.py`, `restaurant/demo.py`, `settings_core/management/commands/prepare_demo.py` (one line), `templates/restaurant/combos.html` (new), `order.html`, `ticket.html`, `report.html`, `board.html`, `kds.html` (+ `?v=` on `stations.html`, `modifiers.html`, `dish_ingredients.html`), `static/hesba/css/restaurant.css`, `static/hesba/js/restaurant.js`, `static/hesba/css/kds.css`, `static/hesba/js/kds.js`, `docs/MARKET_PARITY_ROADMAP.md`.
- **Existing tests edited:** one line, `restaurant/tests_split_pay.py` (R2b, not merged yet): its `?v=resto-r2a` pin is now `?v=resto-r1b`, because this PR changed the stylesheet and script again and bumped the version as required. No figure in any existing test moved; `tests_pay_characterization.py` and `tests_pay_tenders_characterization.py` pass unedited.
- **Tests (new):** `restaurant/tests_combos.py` (46, written first): the spread (the two pins and its invariants), taking a combo and its invoice (VAT on/off, large fries, two combos, whole counts, the combo item refused, no merge), the return, the 10% offer, set-up (the item, the audit, above the parts, a combo in a combo, nine bad set-ups, an add-on group), component add-ons and required choices, family rules (remove, void with right and reason, a component alone), crafted requests (another slot's choice, a missing slot, a bad or unknown id, an inactive choice, an inactive item, an inactive combo, a combo now dearer than its parts, and through the order page), R2a (a component alone, the whole bundle with exact bills, the part sheet's one row, a combo alone, put back with and without a collision, merge), the report and the kitchen (ticket and screen), the screens (set-up list and create, English, 403 for a waiter, the sheet, the block, the waiter taking a combo), R12, and the demo.
- **Where the plan was wrong (the code was followed):**
  1. The migration is `0008_combos`, not `0006_combos` (R1a took 0006, R2a 0007).
  2. Quantity above 1: the plan sent `line.discount × quantity`; each combo taken is instead its own quantity-1 bundle, because one doubled line can differ from two combos by a piastre of VAT (364.80 against 364.78, shown above).
  3. The table bill **never calls `promotions.apply`** today (offers on the table bill are R6). The 10% test runs `promotions.apply` on `services.bill_rows`, the rows R6 will feed it; `promotions` is not edited.
  4. The set-up's refusal is «price above what the cheapest picks come to, each at its menu price less its upcharge»: the plan's «sum of the cheapest choices' menu prices» when the cheapest choice has no upcharge, and the exact condition for a saving that is never negative when it has one.
  5. HG-111's note (a combo's discount would «need its own rule» under R2a) is settled by splitting a combo whole: no discount is ever shared between two bills.
- **Risks and what is left open:**
  1. **R6 trap (a share of 0.00):** `promotions._open` treats a line with no discount as open to an offer. A component whose share rounds to 0.00 (a combo that saves nothing at a choice, or a tiny item beside a dear one) would take a running offer once R6 applies offers to the table bill. R6 must skip every line with a `combo` (the row can carry it); not changed here because no offer reaches the table bill today.
  2. **The till and the invoice form can sell a combo's item as one line at the combo price** (it is an ordinary active service item there). That is the combination trap 7 warns about (no dish revenue, VAT at the item's rate). `sales/pos.py` is not touched; the owner may prefer the item hidden from the till (a later task) or keep it.
  3. **An item used in a combo cannot be deleted** (PROTECT, as an R1a add-on's item). The demo restart uses `flush` and the first run swaps a database copy, so neither is blocked.
  4. A combo's price lives on its item: changing the price on the items screen is not checked against the parts until the combo is next taken (then refused if it became dearer than its parts).
  5. Two bundles of the same combo show as two blocks and six invoice rows, not «2 ×».
  6. The kitchen screen marks each component ready on its own (they may be cooked at different stations); the combo is not a single card.
- **Default decisions for the owner (built in brackets; change on request):**
  1. How is the saving spread? (In proportion to the menu prices, piastres by largest remainder; never on an add-on.)
  2. Two combos: one line of 2 or two combos? (Two combos of quantity-1 lines, so two are exactly twice one.)
  3. A combo priced exactly at its parts? (Allowed: it saves 0.00; above them is refused.)
  4. Who sets up combos? (Whoever manages items, `master_data.manage_items`, as for add-ons.)
  5. Does set-up make the combo's menu item? (Yes, a non-stock service item `COMBO-nnn` named and priced from the form.)
  6. Splitting a combo between two bills? (Only whole; a component alone is refused.)
  7. Can a component's add-ons be chosen on the combo sheet? (Yes, billed at their own price, not discounted.)
  8. Can a combo hold another combo? (No.)
